# World Cup Fantasy — Project Brain

## Product summary
A standalone web app for a **private friends' league**: a FIFA World Cup fantasy game
modeled on Sleeper.com's polish and feature depth, redesigned for a **single-tournament**
format. Snake draft (unique player ownership per league), staggered per-match player
locking, head-to-head group stage, guillotine playoffs on a reduced roster, FAAB waivers.
Not client-facing — fun with friends. Guiding constraint: **"boring and reliable" over clever.**

## Build surfaces (who owns what)
- **Claude Code** — implementation; owns the git lifecycle for its work — commit → `--ff-only` merge to main → push to origin → trigger the Render deploy; **holds by default** (user owns the merge); may commit+merge+deploy autonomously only when the user explicitly preauthorizes for that change; always holds for high-risk classes (resolver / purity / migration / shared-validator / anything touching live scoring or production data) — see CLAUDE.md § Definition of Done / Merge Policy and DECISIONS.md § Merge policy (2026-06-19)
- **Claude Cowork** — progress tracking + manual/operational steps (draft kickoff, stat
  overrides/corrections, FAAB processing oversight)
- **Claude Chat** — architect / reviewer / advisor (this surface) — not a blocking merge-gate
- **Claude Design** — UX/UI

## Working protocol
- **Merge & git discipline (updated 2026-06-19):** `--ff-only`, no force-push. **Hold by default** — once DoD gate is green (typecheck/lint/format/build/tests) with implementation + tests + docs delivered, Code holds and waits for the user's merge decision. Merge authority sits with the user. Contained changes may be delegated: the user may explicitly preauthorize Code to commit + `--ff-only` merge + push + deploy on green; absent that explicit authorization, Code holds. High-risk classes (resolver / purity / migration / shared-validator / live scoring or production data) always hold regardless. Sergio retains live Supabase DB writes (production data / RLS / commish_override). Parallel work runs in isolated git worktrees — never share a working tree (the real concurrency guardrail). Sergio re-uploads the four brain files to Project knowledge after each merge. See CLAUDE.md § Definition of Done / Merge Policy and DECISIONS.md § Merge policy (2026-06-19).
- **One decision/theme per conversation thread.** Keeps context lean and decisions clean.
- These files are the source-of-truth "brain":
  - `PROJECT.md` (this) — overview, surfaces, protocol, status
  - `DECISIONS.md` — running decision log across all themes + agenda for open ones
  - `SCORING.md` — the locked, build-ready scoring model
  - `ARCHITECTURE.md` — the locked, build-ready stack / infra spec (split out the way SCORING.md was)
- **Start of each new thread:** attach these files (keep them in the Project's knowledge so
  every thread sees them).
- **End of each thread:** update the files with whatever was locked.
- **Verification discipline (applies to Code AND Chat):** state only what's directly verified — read the
  code, queried the DB, or ran a command. Anything not directly observable — Render env/process,
  dashboard config, request origin, deploy status — is labeled an **inference to confirm**, never
  asserted as fact.

## Locked requirements (from brief)
1. Snake draft; unique player ownership per league.
2. Per-player staggered locking — **a player locks the instant he plays ≥1 minute; until then he
   stays swappable** (refined from "locks at his match kickoff" — a benched 0-minute starter is
   *not* locked).
3. ~~Pre-set subs; a sub only fires if the starter played 0 minutes.~~ **Superseded (Theme B): no
   auto-subs.** Substitution is manual — swap any not-yet-played player. Lock-on-play delivers the
   original 0-minute protection.
4. Group stage: head-to-head — **all-play-all ("power record"); top N advance** (refined from 1v1
   and "top X" — N is the playoff field, set at the transition; likely 8 or 10, see Theme C).
5. Playoffs: guillotine (lowest scorer eliminated each round), reduced roster.
6. Waivers: FAAB blind bid. **(Full rules locked — see DECISIONS.md → Theme D: $100 budget,
   daily pre-dawn batch, free-agency fallthrough, reverse-seed rolling tiebreak, playoff
   reinforcement.)**
7. Standalone website.
8. **Live "vs the field" screen** — required by all-play-all; must show points-so-far alongside
   how much is still to come (see ARCHITECTURE.md → Real-time layer).

**Live-incident fixes (2026-06-11, MD1):** **premature lineup locks** — ~35 MD1 `lineup_slot` rows
carried `locked_at ≈ now` while their `fifa_match` was still `scheduled` (kickoff days out). Root cause:
the lock-write path (`packages/ingest/src/lock.ts` → `ingest.ts` → `setLockedAt`) stamped `kickoffAt`
for any official-XI starter / entering sub **with no `now` gate**, and both read sites
(`loadLineup.ts`, `loadVsField.ts:148`) treated *presence* of `locked_at` as locked. Fix **merged to
`main`** (write-side now-gate `ee4c18b`, read-side predicate `fab4105`): (1) **write side** — the pure
primitives now take `now` and emit a lock only once its instant has arrived (starter `now >= kickoff`,
sub `now >= entry`), threaded via `MatchCtx.now` from the scheduler tick — a self-guarding write
boundary; (2) **read side** — both sites use the new shared pure `isLockedNow` (`@app/shared`):
locked iff `locked_at != null && locked_at <= now`; (3) a **cleanup SQL artifact** (Sergio runs against
live) nulls the bad MD1 stamps whose fixture is still `kickoff_at > NOW()`, leaving completed-match
locks intact. +8 tests (1719 → 1727), all gates green. See DECISIONS (lock invariant) + ARCHITECTURE §3.

**Live-incident fix (2026-06-12, MD1): `locked_at` UNDER-stamped for players who played (the opposite of
the above).** Genuinely-played slots were left `locked_at = NULL` — e.g. **Raúl Rangel** (Mexico–SA, 90′,
rating 7.3) — because the only lock writers were the **racy** pre-match XI-pull + per-event live sub-locking;
the settle pass held the appearance proof and wrote nothing. **Fix merged (`e888f66`):** `reconcileAppearanceLocks`
stamps every **appeared** player (the `score_player_match` participant set scoring already gates via
`playerAppearedInMatch`) at kickoff, called from **both `ingestLive` and `ingestSettle`**, monotonic +
**period-scoped** `setLockedAt` (only `locked_at IS NULL` slots in the match's OWN `period_id` — never the
ambiguous `team → future-fixture` join). The Rangel-class is pinned by existing tests (`lock.test.ts`
`lockInstantsFromAppearances`/`lockInstantFromSub`, `adapter.test.ts` `playerAppearedInMatch` all-null-stub
exclusion, `ingest.test.ts` settle reconcile incl. monotonic latch). **Despite the merge, 5 prod slots were
under-locked and hand-backfilled** — the **deploy-timing lesson**: `e888f66` was committed **2026-06-12 06:28Z**,
*after* both completed fixtures (Mexico–SA kickoff 19:00Z, Korea–Czech kickoff 02:00Z) had finished; a merged
fix only self-heals a completed match while it is still settling (`hasRating` false, `≤ kickoff + 12h`) **and**
the running Render worker has redeployed to that SHA. "On `origin/main`" ≠ "running on Render." **Residual gap
— now CLOSED (`1723b5a`):** `reconcileAppearanceLocks` ran only on a live/settle tick, so a completed
match that had already dropped from the poll (rating landed, or 12h elapsed) was never reconciled again; a
bounded **48h "recently-completed matches" sweep** (`sweepCompletedMatchLocks`, hourly schedule-sync cadence)
now reconciles those and removes the manual-SQL dependency. See DECISIONS (appeared ⇒
locked backstop) + ARCHITECTURE §3.

**Live-incident fix (2026-06-11, MD1): `score_player_match` generated for NON-participants** —
`score_player_match` rows were written for players who never appeared in a match. Trigger: the
completed **Mexico–South Africa** fixture charged every rostered **GK/DEF** whose team was neither
home nor away a phantom **−1** (the match's goals scored as "conceded" by their uninvolved team),
dragging the whole field negative; MID/FWD non-participants scored 0. Two defects on the per-match
scoring path (`packages/recompute`): it scored ANY dirty `(match, player)` with **no participation
check** (and `markStatPlayerDirty` mints all-null stubs / an upstream player↔match mis-join tags
cross-team rows), and `concededByPlayerTeam` had **no team-in-match guard** (§6 conceded is gated on
role, not minutes). Fix **merged to `main`** (SHA rebased-rewritten; in-main evidence: `recomputePlayerMatch`
participant gate + `deleteScorePlayerMatch` in `packages/recompute` + `ops/2026-06-11-clear-nonparticipant-scores.sql`
artifact): `recomputePlayerMatch`
now gates on the pure `playerAppearedInMatch` (team-in-match **AND** an appearance signal) and a
non-participant gets **no row** (bogus row deleted via new `deleteScorePlayerMatch`, rollup
re-enqueued); `concededByPlayerTeam` hardened to require team-in-match. The **rollup was correct and
untouched** (`recomputeManagerPeriod`/`job:recompute` only re-sum). Live remediation =
`ops/2026-06-11-clear-nonparticipant-scores.sql` (delete cross-team + stub rows) **then**
`pnpm --filter @app/worker job:recompute -- --period "MD1"` — restate-alone won't clear bogus rows.
+9 regression tests; full suite **1747** green. See DECISIONS (participant invariant) + ARCHITECTURE §3/§7.

**Lineup forfeit engine (C1, 2026-06-12):** replaces the cancelled keeper-lock prompt. New rule (DECISIONS
Theme-B forfeit amendment): a played player is **NOT hard-locked** — his slot stays movable; benching a
played starter is a **FINAL, one-way FORFEIT** (`lineup_slot.voided_at` stamp + `is_starter=false`),
forfeiting his period points and barring his return to the XI. **C1 = data + server engine + read contract
only; the destructive-confirm UI is C2.** **Merged to `main`** (`b63f0a4` engine, `9811ff4` tests +
recompute-mirror drift guard, `121f45f` docs). Pieces: (1) migration `20260612120000_lineup_forfeit_voided_at` adds the
column AND extends `enforce_lineup_lock()` to permit EXACTLY the forfeit transition + back-stop the one-way
door (no un-void / no start-of-voided), self-tested + **verified on throwaway Postgres with a uuid-returning
`auth.uid()` shim**; (2) `@app/lineup` engine — `validateLineup` now keys off per-slot play state
(`hasPlayed` = a `score_player_match` row exists, `voided`) + a forfeit-confirm id set; the store stamps
`voided_at` and enqueues a manager-period recompute in-tx; (3) read contract `PeriodLineup.slotMeta`
(`hasPlayed/pointsAtStake/voided/movable`) for C2, rendered by nothing in C1. **Two reconciliations
(reported, not silently chosen):** the read-site change is an **augment, not a replace** (keep `locks` so the
unchanged client exposes no destructive bench affordance; `loadVsField` untouched), and `locked_at` is
**retired from movability but kept** (worker stamping + the IN-direction hindsight backstop intact; full
retirement proposed for C2). **No live destructive path pre-C2:** the route passes no confirm → benching a
played starter is rejected (`forfeit-requires-confirm`), behaviour byte-identical to today. Rollup
unchanged (starters-only). +N tests, all gates green. See DECISIONS (forfeit amendment) + SCORING (principle
6) + ARCHITECTURE §16.

**Lock/forfeit state — reconciled + forfeit UI shipped (2026-06-12):** *(the lock-state "fixed/retired" claim in this entry is **SUPERSEDED** — see "cross-match / non-participant lock leak" below; the over-stamp direction was NOT fully fixed and the statusgate branch was un-retired.)* **Both lock directions are now fixed in `main`** — premature
**over**-stamping (now-gate write `ee4c18b` + now-respecting read `fab4105`) and played-but-unlocked
**under**-stamping (appeared⇒locked backstop `e888f66` + bounded 48h sweep `1723b5a`). The **C1 forfeit engine
is merged** (`b63f0a4` / `9811ff4` / `121f45f`) — earlier "merge HELD" notes above are superseded. **C2 forfeit
UI is merged and render-verified** (`9bee8d1`) — played starters show a pts badge + "Forfeit" affordance (no
padlock — movable, not hard-locked); `ForfeitConfirmSheet` is the one-way confirm gate; Cancel reverts the bench
swap fully; pre-flight `fillEligibleIds` empty → affordance disabled; voided slots render strikethrough +
non-interactive. See ARCHITECTURE §16 (C2 section) + DECISIONS (forfeit amendment).

**Forfeit-sub spurious-error fix (2026-06-20, `9935472`, merged + render-deployed):** a forfeit-sub save
SUCCEEDS (200 `{ok:true}`, slot voided server-side) but the SaveBar then re-painted a
`forfeit-requires-confirm` error beside the "Lineup saved." toast. Cause: clearing `pendingForfeits` on
success made the post-save re-validation (`validateLineup` rule 4c) run against the stale, never-refetched
SSR `slotMeta`/`locks` (which still model the benched man as a played, un-voided starter). Fix = **do NOT
clear `pendingForfeits` on success** (client-only; validator/contract/persistence untouched). **Tracked
residual:** period switch-away-and-back re-arms the same error (`onSelectPeriod` resets `pendingForfeits`
while `slotMeta` stays stale) — full fix needs a post-save `slotMeta` refetch / period-state lift
(contract-adjacent, its own thread). See DECISIONS (forfeit amendment).

The
throwaway branch **`fix/premature-locks-statusgate` is retired**: rebased onto `main` it showed **zero unique
delta** and never carried the agreed four-layer fix — branch + worktree deleted. The **"four-layer fix" plan is
superseded**: **Layer 2** (no stamp on a not-yet-kicked-off match) is **already met by the in-main now-gate**,
leaving only **optional, non-blocking hardening** — **Layer 1** (kickoff-import: **skip/flag** a missing kickoff
instead of coalescing to ≈now) and **Layer 4** (live cleanup SQL: drop the `p.label = 'MD1'` filter to clean
**all periods**). See DECISIONS (lock invariant + forfeit amendment).

**Live-incident fix (2026-06-12 evening, MD1): cross-match / non-participant lock leak — the THIRD recurrence**
(`fix/premature-locks-statusgate`, merge HELD). During the Canada–Bosnia live window, the sub-event lock path
stamped `locked_at` on **44** MD1 `lineup_slot` rows belonging to **non-participants** — pooled WC players
(Mbappé, James Rodríguez, Ronaldo, …) whose own fixtures are `scheduled` Jun 13–18, **one slot each** (ownership
is unique per league). **Root cause:** `feed.matchEvents` returned **foreign-fixture** substitution events (the
GOAT `match_id` filter is not honoured); `ingestLive` applied the **live** match's kickoff + period and
`setLockedAt` stamped each stranger at `kickoff + their-other-match minute`. Because those instants are
legitimately past, the now-gate could not catch it — **the defect is identity/scoping, not timing.** The
2026-06-11 "`≈ now`" reading was the **same** leak, misdiagnosed; **`ed7717a`'s retirement of
`fix/premature-locks-statusgate` was premature** and is un-retired here. **Fix:** one **`lockSlot`** write
boundary enforcing `isLockWriteAuthorized` (**team-in-source-match + status in-play-or-later + now + period**) —
`reconcile`/XI-pull exonerated (they stamp only at kickoff; the appeared set had **zero** strangers); plus
`ingestLive`/`ingestSettle` foreign-event guards, a feed-client `match_id` re-filter, structured
`lock.slot.{stamped,refused}` + `*.foreign_skipped` logs, trigger migration **`20260612220000`** (scheduled-only
self-heal unlock; verified on throwaway Postgres — scheduled-clear succeeds, completed-clear raises), and an
all-periods GUC-wrapped cleanup **`ops/2026-06-12-clear-cross-match-locks.sql`**. **Cleanup-vs-trigger finding:**
the committed `ops/2026-06-11-clear-premature-md1-locks.sql` (bare `set→NULL`, no GUC / `DISABLE TRIGGER`) would
be trigger-blocked as written, yet the Jun-11 stamps were in fact cleared (James now carries a Jun-12 stamp) →
the cleanup that ran used a **manual override not captured in the artifact** (same-leak-twice, not never-cleaned).
**Corrupt-kickoff theory RETIRED** — `mapMatchRow`/`upsertMatch` never coalesce a missing kickoff to `now`
(`Invalid Date` fails loudly). **Remediation order is load-bearing:** Render deploy (migration + new worker) →
cleanup SQL → live-window verify (fact-7 predicate returns 0 throughout; James reads movable, Rangel locked).
Tests **1861 → 1874**. See DECISIONS (RECURRENCE) + ARCHITECTURE §3.

**C2 branch-hygiene incident:** during C2 development, 22 stray session-generated files were committed directly
onto `main` alongside the feature work. Recovered via `wip/c2-recover` (isolated the clean delta), rebuilt clean
on `feat/lineup-forfeit-ui` — `9bee8d1` is tidy. Reinforces the **worktree/branch-disjoint discipline**: feature
work on an isolated branch (or worktree), never directly on `main`, even for small changes.

Both follow-ups above are now **CLOSED** (DECISIONS.md → "Mock-draft session — open items"): the
**lobby→active flip** and **live pick + autopick streaming** were confirmed across **two authed clients
with no reload**. Root fix = the browser Realtime client now authorizes with the **user JWT** (`setAuth`)
before subscribing — the anon socket received zero RLS-gated `postgres_changes`; autopick totality came
via the pure `orderDraftPool` (queue → `default_rank` NULLS LAST → `playerId`). · **Prompt 10 —
set-lineup flow (XI picker + lock-on-play binding), COMPLETE ✅** (605 tests, +~110). New pure
`packages/lineup` (`@app/lineup`): `validateLineup(squad, proposedXI, lockState, period, now)` →
`ok | a typed LineupError family` (`illegal-formation` / `incomplete-xi` / `not-your-player` /
`locked-player-moved` / `wrong-period`), with the Theme-B bounds sourced from `@app/shared`
(`FORMATION_BOUNDS` / `STARTING_XI_SIZE`, never re-derived) and **lock-respecting** on each locked slot's
frozen `is_starter` (a played player can't be moved into or out of the XI); behind a thin `LineupStore`
port (Memory + Prisma impls) + a store-backed `setLineup` controller — the decision core is
**purity-proven** (`purity.test.ts`, comment-stripped). The **player lock is consumed, not
reimplemented**: it is ingestion's `lineup_slot.locked_at` (Prompt 05a), read through the SSR loader. The
ONLY write path is the gated **`POST /api/lineup`**, mirroring `POST /api/draft/pick` (resolve session →
assert it owns the lineup, **401/403 before** the controller, scope `self`), with a **triple-defended
server-authoritative lock re-check**: `validateLineup` on the authoritative lock state → `saveLineup`
writes only `WHERE locked_at IS NULL` and **aborts the whole commit if a locked row would change** → the
`enforce_lineup_lock` trigger is the DB backstop (the Prompt-01 latch is **not regressed**).
`wrong-period` gates on `period.closes_at`, and **future windows are legal by construction** (the
validator's `PeriodWindow` carries no `opens_at`) — so pre-setting upcoming windows is allowed, and an
unplayed, never-locked starter is still backstopped by period close. The **set-lineup screen**
(`app/lineup/`, mapped to `design_reference/setlineup/*` + `shell/*`) renders the formation pitch +
bench, swaps start↔bench with live legality feedback (the SAME `validateLineup`), freezes locked players,
and pre-sets per-period windows; lineup data reaches the browser only via the SSR Prisma loader (**no
browser-direct `lineup_slot` read / no Realtime on the table — Theme F needs no new authenticated SELECT
policy**). Opus adversarial review (6 lenses → verify): **0 blockers, 2 majors fixed pre-merge**
(cross-position swaps so the formation can actually change + the live illegal-formation feedback is
reachable; the FAAB-drop orphan-slot reconcile seam TODO'd). **Launch gates (pre-June-11):** (a) wire the
per-player **kickoff indicator** (`kickoffByPlayer` `TODO(confirm)` — `fifa_match.kickoff_at` via
`player.teamId`); (b) the **runtime lock-freeze + late-edit rejection** is deferred (no drafted roster /
live `locked_at` yet) → fold into the GOAT-trial ingestion smoke test. **Carried TODOs:** FAAB add/drop
reconcile (`saveLineup` orphan-slot DELETE + forbid dropping a locked-slot player), a dedicated
`FormationPicker` / `reshape()`, and a commissioner `admin` lineup scope. **Next: the live "vs the field"
screen** (ARCHITECTURE → Real-time layer) — points-so-far alongside how much is still to come,
all-play-all — then the commissioner/admin surface + the group→playoff transition. All themes remain

The first of two Waivers prompts (the **engine**; the `/waivers` UI is Prompt 26) — implements the LOCKED DECISIONS §D blind-FAAB → free-agency algorithm with **no UI**. **New `packages/faab` (`@app/faab`)** mirrors the draft/lineup pure-core + injected-IO shape: a **PURE, IO-free batch resolver** (`resolveFaabBatch`) encoding all 8 §D steps — void+refund a bid whose add target already kicked off, highest-bid-first player-by-player, tie → rolling waiver order, **move-to-bottom ONLY when the tiebreak is actually used** (winning on amount alone never moves you), own-bids resolve highest-first-skip (emergent from the global-highest loop against the manager's updated budget/roster/ownership), $0 bids legal, every won claim is add/drop + a budget debit, contiguous 1..N waiver order — proven clock/Prisma/Supabase-free by a **70-case purity matrix** (7 modules × 10 forbidden patterns). **Gated `POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/faab/bid`** (submit / edit / cancel) via the framework-agnostic `handleBid` on the `/api/draft/pick` template: `requireManager` → `assertCanActAsManager({scope:"self"})` → **401 (no session) / 403 (not your manager) BEFORE any DB write** (verified structurally with an exploding-Proxy store), then submission validation — `amount ≥ 0`, **over-commit rejection** (`amount ≤ budget − other pending bids`), add unowned league-wide + not-kicked-off, drop owned + `≠ add` + required-when-full + **drop-lock validation** (can't drop a player locked by play in an active matchday), 2/5/5/3 roster legality. **Real cron body** in `apps/worker/src/jobs/faabBatch.ts` (replaces the live-but-inert placeholder): opens a `FaabBatch`, reads pending bids + managers + roster + add-target kickoffs, calls the pure resolver, then writes ALL outcomes in **ONE atomic `$transaction`** (the no-double-spend / valid-drop guard) — assign winners (add/drop + debit), refund voids, mark losers, apply the move-to-bottom reorder as the schema-mandated **TWO-PHASE reassignment** (negate → final 1..N, preserving the contiguous `manager_waiver_order_uq` invariant), stamp each bid's `batchId` + terminal status (`won`/`lost`/`voided_refunded`), write the batch `complete`. **Idempotent re-run** (a `done` batch is not re-processed: guarded `updateMany WHERE status='pending'` + no-pending-bids → no batch row). **Lineup-slot release behind the `@app/lineup` domain boundary:** a won drop's UNLOCKED `lineup_slot` rows are released (so a dropped starter stops scoring) via exported `@app/lineup/prisma` `releaseDroppedPlayerSlots(tx,…)` inside the same batch transaction — **faab never touches `lineup_slot`**; the lock-on-play check (`findLockedSlotPlayerIds`) lives there too. **Cross-player equal-amount ordering = waiver order** (by the leading bidder's `waiverOrderPosition`, not player id / insertion). `addTargetKickoffAt` resolves through **`fifa_match.kickoff_at` via `player.team_id`** — the SAME schedule field lock-on-play derives `lineup_slot.locked_at` from (no second clock). **Deferred / flagged:** the `faab_bid.priority` column migration is deferred to Null when the player's team has no fixture this period or either side is TBD (knockout bracket not yet determined) — renders "TBD" with no flag, only when `kickoffAt` is also non-null (no double-TBD). +4 tests (1647). `feat/lineup-opponent-label`; merge HELD for Chat clearance; no force-push. · **Prompt 54 — period-select fix: `opensAt`-NULL sort → `selectCurrentPeriod` with per-screen isCurrent latch (waivers + vsfield), COMPLETE ✅ (merge HELD).** Root cause: `period.opensAt` is never populated by the provisioning CLI — `ORDER BY opensAt ASC, label ASC` degrades to alphabetical, so "Final" (F) sorts before "Group MD1" (G) and both `/waivers` and `/vsfield` resolved to the Final period on opening day. **Fix:** new pure `selectCurrentPeriod<T>(periods, isCurrent: (p: T) => boolean)` in `@app/shared/periodOrder.ts` (same module as P51's `sortByPeriodOrder`) sorts in JS by `matches[0].kickoffAt` then applies a per-screen `isCurrent` predicate. **Per-screen latches differ:** waivers `= p => p.batchClearedAt === null` (batch hasn't run yet); vsfield `= p => now < matches.at(-1).kickoffAt + 120min` (at least one match still live). The split is required because `batchClearedAt` is stamped **~6h BEFORE first kickoff** (`effectiveBatchAt = firstKickoff − 360min`) — using it as the vsfield latch would drop the live wave while MD1 matches are being played, binding the Realtime subscription and lineup/score reads to MD2 instead. `period.status` never transitions (always `"pending"` from provision to freeze); the `status === "open"` fast-path is retained future-safe. `loadWaivers` + `loadVsField` both updated; `selectCurrentWaiverPeriod` removed from `waiversLogic.ts` (hoisted). vsfield period query extended to fetch all matches (removed `take: 1`) so `matches.at(-1)` gives the last kickoff. `resolveFaabBatch` / `resolve.ts` / purity matrix byte-unchanged. `// TODO(confirm)` for overlapping group waves (sequential periods only). +7 tests (1658 total). `feat/waivers-period-select-fix`; merge HELD for Chat clearance; no force-push. · **Prompt 54 — set-lineup formation picker + roster-fillability filter (LAUNCH-BLOCKER), COMPLETE ✅.** Repro: MR. His squad IS fieldable (3-4-3 / 3-5-2) but there was **no formation control** in the live UI (the carried `FormationPicker`/`reshape()` TODO) and the default was blind. **Closes that carried TODO.** Fix = pure helpers added to `view.ts`: `GROUP_FORMATIONS` (the DECISIONS Theme-B standard 7), `formationFillable` (GK≥1 + roster supply ≥ shape per pos — the gap `validateLineup` never covered: it checks a proposed XI, never roster supply), `formationLockLegal` (live port of the design's `formationLegal`), `offeredFormations` = fillable ∩ lock-legal, `reshapeToFormation` (keeps locked starters, promotes only MOVABLE reserves — never a played bench man), `formationKeyOf`, `defaultFormationKey` (canonical 4-3-3 if fillable, else first fillable in canonical order). `defaultStarterIds` now builds from the fillable default (a 3-DEF squad opens on **3-4-3, savable immediately**); a persisted lineup still loads its OWN saved shape (never overridden). Root cause = held branch + the control was only **source-contract-smoke**-tested (a smoke can't prove a control renders/taps — the lesson). This prompt re-bases on current `main`, and: (1) renders `<FormationPicker>` **directly under the FORMATION panel** (moved from inside `.sl-pitchcol`), populated by `offeredFormations` (fillable ∩ lock-legal); (2) when `offered.length <= 1`, renders a **static indicator** (no dead single-tab control) — chosen over a disabled tab; (3) updates the help copy to name the picker. **Replaced the 3 contract smokes with a real RTL/jsdom interaction test** (`apps/web/app/lineup/FormationPicker.test.tsx`, mounts the actual `SetLineupClient`): all offered shapes render, selecting a non-active one reshapes + re-validates (Save stays enabled), unfillable shapes absent, single-shape → static. Required NEW test infra (the repo was Node-only): `jsdom` (root), `@testing-library/react` + `@testing-library/dom` (apps/web, shares React 19), `oxc: { jsx: "automatic" }` + `.tsx` include globs in `vitest.config.ts`, per-file `// @vitest-environment jsdom`. Verified with a headless-browser screenshot of the real component (esbuild bundle + Playwright). Still NO new write path; validator + route byte-untouched. 1672 tests (123 files), all gates green. `feat/lineup-formation-picker-render` off `main`; merge HELD for Chat clearance; no force-push. · **Prompt — FA-grant Waivers UI ($0 free-agency surfaced), COMPLETE ✅ (merge HELD).** Prompt 48 shipped + tested `POST /api/faab/free-agent` but **no UI ever called it** — the Waivers tab rendered only the sealed-bid claim form, so in a period's free-agency window the only action was a sealed bid that wouldn't clear until the next batch (verified live: MD1 in free-agency, 1077 snapshot-eligible). **Lesson: route-level tests ≠ a working user path** (same class as P54's FormationPicker). Wired (UI-only): new `FreeAgentPanel` (instant Add/drop, reuses the composer's `droppableRoster` + `claimableFreeAgents` + `KitChip`/`NationFlag`/`Pos`) + `WaiversClient.handleGrant` → `POST /api/faab/free-agent` → `router.refresh()`; phase switch on the SAME `acquisitionWindowState` the BatchBar uses (sealed-bid → sealed form unchanged; free-agency → FA list; locked → Add disabled). Loader now offers the **snapshot-eligible** pool via new `listFaIneligiblePlayerIds` (`@app/faab/prisma`), the FA predicate factored into ONE `snapshotOwnershipWhere` shared with `getFaTargetFacts` (list + grant can't drift; a stale list only → `fa-conflict` 409, surfaced inline). HARD STOPS held: `resolve.ts` / purity matrix / scoring + the route / `validateFaGrant` / `claimFreeAgent` byte-untouched; no schema change. Closed the missing **end-to-end** gap: an RTL/jsdom test mounts the real `WaiversClient`, drives Add → asserts the `/api/faab/free-agent` POST + refresh + the `fa-conflict` path; added a `@/` alias to `vitest.config.ts` so jsdom mounts resolve app-router `@/` imports. **1680 tests (124 files), all gates green** (typecheck/lint/format/test; baseline was 1672). `feat/faab-fa-ui` off `main`; merge HELD for Chat clearance; no force-push. · **Prompt — Commissioner-override CLI (`commish:roster` / `commish:lineup`), COMPLETE ✅ (stacked on feat/faab-fa-ui, merge HELD).** Render-Shell tool letting the commissioner repair "our-fault" moves the missing FA UI blocked (operator side of the same incident). `apps/worker/src/commish/` — dry-run by default, `--apply` to execute. THE DESIGN = the bypass/enforce line: `commish:roster` bypasses the FA/waiver **window** (calls `claimFreeAgent` directly; reuses `validateFaGrant` with window/snapshot/drop-lock neutralized) but KEEPS roster cap + valid-drop + active-ownership unique + slot-release ($0, no budget/waiver-order change); `commish:lineup` bypasses ONLY the lineup edit-window lock (`relaxPeriodLock` → `validateLineup` window no-op) but KEEPS formation/position legality + ownership + 11-XI + the lock-on-play latch (+ DB trigger, unbypassable). Both ALWAYS enforce: commissioner gate (`is_commissioner` or smrios07@gmail.com), a required `--reason`, idempotent skip, and a structured `commish-override {json}` audit line per applied action. Per-player **kickoff guard ON by default** (blocks an already-played add; explicit `--allow-post-kickoff` honors it + logs LOUDLY — the post-kickoff integrity caveat). Names resolved team→managerId (manager.displayName) + player→id (displayName/first/last), ambiguity is an ERROR with candidates printed. STOP SEAMS held: engine/route/schema untouched, **no migration** (audit = stdout); `claimFreeAgent` + `@app/lineup` service reused, validation never re-derived. Pure core + injected-deps orchestrators unit-tested (resolver/ambiguity, gate refusal, kickoff default-block vs allow, cap kept under window-bypass, formation legality kept under lock-bypass, dry-run applies nothing); IO `cli.ts` tsc-covered like the provision CLI. Added `@app/lineup` dep to apps/worker. **1712 tests (126 files), all gates green.** `feat/faab-fa-ui` 3rd commit; merge HELD for Chat clearance; no force-push.

(3) `commish:lineup --allow-locked-slot` relaxes the lock-on-play latch in all three places (`validateLineup` phase-4 empty `lockState` + `saveLineup` re-check + the **DB trigger** via a tx-local `app.commish_override` GUC) while KEEPING formation/ownership/XI/gate/reason; ONE migration `20260611120000_lock_on_play_commish_override` (embedded self-test + throwaway-Postgres verified; `search_path` stays pinned). **HELD:** #4 (remove the FAAB FWD cap) — the DECISIONS gate found it is the *still-valid* FAAB `2/5/5/3` cap, NOT the retired *draft* cap (Prompt 44 was draft-only); it needs a separate commissioner decision first, so `checkDropAndRoster`/`resolve.ts` are byte-unchanged.      `onForfeitFromModal` handles eligibility gate + modal-close → toast or `ForfeitConfirmSheet`.
     `forfeitPropsForModal` memo wires played-starter classification → modal prop.

   - `ForfeitConfirm.test.tsx` updated: tap → modal (not forfeit confirm), forfeit via modal button.

## Mobile bottom-nav + overflow containment (Prompt 40 — `feat/mobile-bottom-nav`, HOLD for clearance)

- **The top-strip-widens-the-document bug is closed.** Root cause: the 8 top-nav items' intrinsic width
  set the document scroll width, causing every authenticated page to be wider than the viewport; the whole
  page slid sideways when the active tab auto-scrolled into view. Fixed by: (1) `.sh-topnav-scroll`
  scroll container (`overflow-x:auto; min-width:0; flex:1`) + `min-width:0` on `.sh-app`/`.sh-topbar`
  (real fix); (2) `html, body { overflow-x:hidden; max-width:100% }` in `ds.css` (document backstop);
  (3) `MoreSheet` `useEffect` calls `scrollIntoView({ inline:'nearest' })` on the active item, which
  is captured by the scroll container not the document.
- **Mobile bottom tab bar shipped** (phones < 640 px): Dashboard · Set lineup · Vs the field · Pool ·
  More. More sheet: Scoring · Waivers · Draft room · Settings · identity · sign-out. Pure CSS 640 px swap
  — no matchMedia, no hydration fork. Bar clears iOS home indicator via `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` +
  `viewport-fit=cover` (real-device confirmation gated on deploy).
- **Playwright proof:** 75/75 assertions passed at 320/375/390/414 px (phone) + 768 px (desktop) across
  5 routes. Screenshots in `apps/web/screenshots/`. iOS safe-area/touch-ergonomics gated on real-device
  deploy pass.
- **Per-page mobile fit closed — mobile sweep complete (Prompt 41).** The two pages whose content was
  built wider than the viewport (and so *clipped* by the global backstop, not reflowed) now fit:
  `/lineup` (the formation pitch column was pinned ~384 px by a bare-`1fr` grid track → `minmax(0,1fr)`
  + token scaling 78→62→52 px; full XI in formation, tappable at 320 px) and `/pool` (auto-layout
  leaderboard forced ~670 px by long names/emails → `table-layout:fixed`, MANAGER ellipsis-truncates,
  PLAYED/CORRECT/POINTS fully visible, YOU row intact). Fixes live in per-route `lineup.css`/`pool.css`;
  `ds.css` byte-identity untouched. **Proof (`verify-page-fit.mjs`) asserts element bounds** — every
  `getBoundingClientRect()` within `[-1, clientWidth+1]` at 320/375/390/414 (8/8), not document
  `scrollWidth` (which the backstop satisfies by clipping); screenshots eyeballed. Merge HELD for Chat.

## Dashboard/Pool tournament-phase discriminator → `period.kind` (Prompt 44 — `fix/dashboard-phase-discriminator`, HOLD for clearance)

- **Bug (verified against the live DB):** `selectTournamentPhase` decided group-vs-knockout on
  `fifa_match.round` (`round === null → group`, `round !== null → knockout`). The live BALLDONTLIE feed
  populates `round` with the **matchday number** for group games — MD1 rows carry `round = "1"` with
  `in_progress`/`completed` status — so the selector's playoff branch fired and the dashboard rendered
  the **knockout interim while the tournament was still in the group stage**. Pool reuses the same
  selector, so it raised its knockout-bracket skeleton early too.
- **Fix:** `selectTournamentPhase` now keys on `period.kind` (`group_md`/`knockout_round`) and detects
  the Final by `period.label === "Final"`; `TournamentMatchSummary` is now `{ status, periodKind,
  periodLabel }`. `loadDashboard` + `loadPool` join `fifa_match.periodId → period.kind`/`period.label`
  and feed the mapped shape — `round` dropped from `loadDashboard`'s select and Pool's `MATCH_SELECT`;
  neither surface touches `fifa_match.round` any more. `loadDashboard`'s earliest-group-kickoff filter
  moved from `round === null` to `period.kind === "group_md"`. `periodKind === null` (period not linked)
  advances no phase. No migration; pure-selector + two thin loaders. This closes the one holdout that
  still trusted `round` — the rest of the system (locking / recompute / period-close / Pool engine)
  already keyed on `period.kind` (DECISIONS → Pool).
- **Tests (delta +4):** rewrote `selectTournamentPhase.test.ts` onto the `period.kind` shape (new `m()`
  helper; all precedence + exhaustiveness cases re-expressed) and **added** a Prompt-44 live-MD1
  regression `describe` proving `group_md` in_progress/completed + scheduled knockouts → `"group"` (not
  `"playoff"`), plus matchday-style-label, lone-scheduled-knockout, and unlinked-period cases.
  `selectDashboardCompose.test.ts` helper type + cases translated. `dashboard.test.ts` P38
  source-contract assertions updated (`periodKind === "knockout_round"`, `periodLabel === "Final"`,
  `period: { select: { kind: true, label: true } }`). `poolContracts.test.ts` green unchanged.
- **Gates:** `pnpm -w typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm format:check && pnpm test` all green. **1949 tests
  passed** (140 files; baseline 1945, +4). Live-deploy proof (group dashboard on `/`, no knockout
  bracket on `/pool`) gated on deploy; merge HELD for Chat clearance.

### 2026-06-14 — Feed match-scoped pagination fix (feat/feed-match-ids, merge HELD)
The five match-scoped feed helpers (`matchLineups`/`matchEvents`/`playerMatchStats`/`teamMatchStats`/
`matchShots`) now filter **server-side** via the bracketed `match_ids[]=<id>` array param at `per_page=100`,
so a single fixture resolves in **one page** instead of walking the entire tournament dataset (~1,800 req /
~3 min per single-match peek that monopolized the rate budget and stalled live polling via the re-entrancy
guard). Root cause: the GOAT FIFA **paginated** endpoints silently ignore the scalar `match_id` we sent (it
is recognized only on the non-paginated `/odds/player_props`) → the server returned an unfiltered firehose —
also the deeper cause of the 2026-06-12 cross-match lock leak. `matchScoped` reuses the same `toQuery` array
path rosters uses for `team_ids[]`/`player_ids[]`; the client-side `match_id` re-filter is **retained** as
belt-and-suspenders; `playerProps` (scalar `match_id`, the one endpoint that honours it) +
`FIFAMatchLineupEntry`/`res.data` mapping (c9e8990) untouched. +7 tests (2041 passed | 3 skipped, 150 files)
asserting `match_ids[]=<id>` (not a bare `match_id=`) + `per_page=100` per helper, the belt-and-suspenders
cross-fixture drop, and a rosters array-scoping regression. Gates: `pnpm -w typecheck` / `pnpm lint` /
`pnpm format:check` / `pnpm test` all exit 0. Two commits (fix+tests `38a3a56`; brain files `[skip render]`).
Merge HELD pending clearance.

### 2026-06-15 — Group→playoff transition + playoff lineup mode (Phases 1+3, merged to main)

**feat/playoff-transition** (87a7e1a→b7dc9d3, 7 commits) + **feat/playoff-lineup-mode** (706351d + 3952e62), ff-merged to `main`. 2115 tests ✓. **Schema:** new `playoff_entry` table (`id/league_id/manager_id/seed/status/eliminated_round/eliminated_at`, `PlayoffEntryStatus = alive|eliminated|champion`, `@@unique([league_id, manager_id])`, `@@index([league_id, status])`, FKs CASCADE, RLS league-scoped SELECT, added to `supabase_realtime` publication). **Pure transition derivation** in `packages/recompute/src/transition.ts`: `cutScheduleFor` (front-loaded `⌊÷5⌋+rem` distribution, field ≥ 6; supersedes the design's illustrative preset), `selectPlayoffField`, `carryForwardWaiverOrder`. **`commish:transition` CLI:** dry-run default (prints field+seeds, cut schedule, plan, `standings: FINAL ✓/⚠` gate); `--apply` runs 6-step `$transaction` (status claim → `cut_count` writes → `playoff_entry` rows → roster release → $100 FAAB reset → two-phase waiver carry-forward); D6 standings-frozen guard + `--allow-incomplete-standings` override. **FAAB roster-cap split:** `rosterCapForLeagueStatus(league.status)` in `@app/shared` (15 group / 9 playoff) enforced at both FAAB validators — submission + batch resolver (`league.status` axis, NOT `period.kind`). _(Later — CONTRACT-P3, 2026-06-27 — migrated to `rosterCapForPlayoffPhase(loadPlayoffPhaseActive)` keyed on `playoff_entry` existence; `rosterCapForLeagueStatus` deleted.)_ **Playoff lineup mode:** `validateLineup` branches on `period.kind === "knockout_round"` → `PLAYOFF_ROSTER` (cap ≤9, 7 starters 1 GK + 6 outfield); `FORMATIONS_PO` 2-2-2/2-3-1/3-2-1 emerges from `playoffBounds()`, not hard-coded; drift-guard test pins the emergence. Period-driven Set-Lineup tab (`loadLineup` reads `period.kind`, `LockHero` shows "Playoff XI · 7 starters"). **Key invariant (D5):** 9-cap is a gate, not a driver — a 15-man advancer who never drops is blocked from setting their R32 lineup and forfeits (by design). **Open seams (trim-down phase, next):** manager-facing release-to-9 flow; commish force-trim CLI; non-advancer playoff FAAB gate; trim window structure. See DECISIONS.md → Group→playoff transition block + ARCHITECTURE.md §20.

### 2026-06-16 — Phase B: `league.status='complete'` decision (docs-only, no code)

**Decision ratified, no code touched:** at tournament end `league.status` STAYS `'playoff'` — `'complete'` is never written, only DERIVED read-only from the champion `playoff_entry` row (the `PlayoffsView`/dashboard `complete` arm). This closes the `playoff → complete` routing seam that was flagged open in two places (Playoff per-round cut application — Scope; Playoff theater route gate). **3-arm FAAB safety map with status held at `'playoff'`:** FA claims — **shut** (no future fixture → no add window opens); blind bids — **accepted-but-inert** (the bid row persists but the batch resolver has no upcoming period to resolve against, so nothing is granted); releases/drops — **drop-only, benign** (a release frees a slot with no competitive consequence once the tournament is over). **Accepted residual (not a bug):** a post-tournament bid path can return a misleading `200` and a phantom-clear-on-outage can occur, but neither has any competitive effect — any phantom clear is an inert budget debit / roster swap on a dead league (scoring is over). Evidence: `vitest run` **204 passed / 5 skipped**. No code change, no deploy (docs `[skip render]`). See DECISIONS → 'Tournament end leaves `league.status='playoff'` — never complete' + ARCHITECTURE.md §21.

### 2026-06-17 — FAAB sealed→free-agency boundary is the batch-clear LATCH, not first kickoff (fix/faab-sealed-bid-latch-boundary, merge HELD)

**fix/faab-sealed-bid-latch-boundary** (source commit `b05b78d` + this brain commit `[skip render]`). **2331 passed | 18 skipped** (measured baseline at the branch point — main `b88b7b2` — was 2325 passed | 18 skipped via root `vitest run`; **+6 passing** = validate 3 [sealed-accept / cleared-reject / locked-outer-bound, all non-UTC] + window 1 [MD1 non-UTC regression] + handleBid 2 [post-clear submit 409-no-write + edit-path re-gate 409-no-write]). Full `pnpm db:generate && pnpm -w typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test && pnpm --filter @app/web build` all exit 0; `pnpm format:check` flags ONLY two PRE-EXISTING untouched audit `.md` files (every changed file passes prettier). **The incident:** in MD1, two $0 sealed bids submitted during the free-agency window (after `period.batch_cleared_at` was stamped, before first kickoff) were accepted by `POST /api/faab/bid` and stranded permanently — the latch blocks any batch re-run, so they never resolved. **Root cause:** `validateBidSubmission` gated only on the period first kickoff (`acquisitionCutoffAt`), so the sealed-bid phase never actually ENDED at batch-clear. **Fix (latch-driven boundary):** the validator now routes through the shared **`acquisitionWindowState`** (single source of truth) — "locked" (now ≥ first kickoff) → `add-kicked-off` (unchanged outer bound, byte-equivalent condition); "free-agency" (`batch_cleared_at IS NOT NULL`) → the new **`bid-window-closed`** (409) — and only the still-sealed phase accepts a bid. The add-period's `batch_cleared_at` is threaded through `PlayerFacts` (resolved via the SAME `resolveAddPeriodWindow` the $0 FA grant uses → the bid route and FA route can never disagree on the gating period; the now-dead `periodFirstKickoff` helper was removed, firstKickoff resolution byte-equivalent) → `BidValidationContext` → BOTH the submit AND edit handler call sites; `WaiversClient` maps the code to a friendly message (no UUID leak). **Scope fences (proven):** `window.ts` already keyed sealed→FA on the latch (the real gap was the validator never consulting it); `resolveFaabBatch`/`resolve.ts` **byte-unchanged** (sha `a7b6666…` identical, 0 diff lines), §D purity test green, worker cadence (`selectPeriodsToClear`/dispatch/commitBatch) + the FA-grant route untouched; no schema migration. **Adversarial 3-lens review (× verify):** zero confirmed blockers/highs — boundary-logic CLEAN (full edge matrix), spec/DoD CLEAN; the one finding (the new code missing from `WaiversClient`'s `ERROR_MESSAGES` → raw-UUID leak) + a missing edit-path test were both applied. See DECISIONS.md → Theme-D amendment (latch-driven boundary) + ARCHITECTURE.md §3. **Merge HELD pending Chat clearance.**

### 2026-06-17 — Period `status` lifecycle is WIRED into the period-close cron (feat/period-status-lifecycle, MERGED + DEPLOYED)

**feat/period-status-lifecycle** (off latest merged main `50fda2c`) — code commit `46cf899` + a separate
`[skip render]` docs commit. **The incident:** the `period.status` lifecycle (`pending → open → closed`,
`PERIOD_STATUSES`) was never advanced — provisioning seeds `pending`, and the hourly period-close cron only
stamped `frozen_at`. So a matchday opened at seed and **never closed**, and `findLockedSlotPlayerIds`
(@app/lineup, gates on `status !== "closed"`) kept every played player locked forever → **all waiver drops
froze the moment a matchday's matches ended**. MD1 was unblocked by hand on 2026-06-17 (`MD1 → closed`,
`MD2 → open` via SQL); this wires it so it can't recur. **Fix:** a new PURE
`selectPeriodStatusTransitions(periods, fixturesByPeriod)` in **@app/recompute** (IO-free sibling of
`selectPeriodsToFreeze`; reuses `selectAnomalyPeriods` + @app/shared `comparePeriodLabels`) returns
`{ toClose, toOpen }` — **close** a wave once every fixture is `completed` (∧ ≥1 fixture ∧ not an anomaly),
**open** the earliest canonical-order period not in `closed ∪ to-close` iff still `pending` (maintains
**exactly one open period**, self-heals a no-open bootstrap, empty at tournament end). **DECOUPLED from
freeze** — keys on "all fixtures completed", NOT `frozen_at`/`result_freeze_hours` (dropping a played player
after close is safe: his locked slot stays, only unlocked slots release). Wired into `periodClose.ts` AFTER
the byte-untouched freeze block: a second unfiltered period query (the freeze query is `frozenAt: null`-scoped
→ would miss already-closed waves the earliest-current pick needs) feeds the fn; applied as ONE guarded,
idempotent `$transaction` (`updateMany` matching the expected prior status); new `job.periodClose.statusAdvanced`
logs + `closed`/`opened` counts on `done`. **Signature note:** the prompt's illustrative `now` arg was DROPPED
— the close conditions + structural `toOpen` use no clock, none of the spec tests exercise it, and sibling
`selectAnomalyPeriods` likewise takes none. **Scope fences:** freeze logic (`selectPeriodsToFreeze`/`frozen_at`),
`findLockedSlotPlayerIds`/`slotRelease.ts`, `periodOrder` ordering/fallback all UNTOUCHED; no schema migration
(column + enum exist); no web change. **DoD green:** `pnpm db:generate && pnpm -w typecheck && pnpm lint &&
pnpm format:check && pnpm test` all exit 0 on the `50fda2c` base — **2325 passed | 18 skipped (+18 new
`periodStatus` tests)**. Purity proof: `periodStatus.ts` imports only `@app/shared` + `./freeze`; the only
runtime `@app/db` in @app/recompute lives in `prismaStore.ts` (the separate `@app/recompute/prisma` subpath,
not the index chain). **Adversarial 3-lens review (× verify), opus/high:** 0 confirmed findings. See
ARCHITECTURE.md §22 + DECISIONS.md → Theme C amendment. **MERGED + DEPLOYED:** rebased onto `68eeb76`, ff-merged to main as `0369d35` (feat) + `c1e3629` (docs [skip render]) — rebased twins of `46cf899` + `a414158`; shipped in the 2026-06-18 worker deploy (manual, since the [skip render] tip suppressed auto-deploy).

### 2026-06-18 — FAAB free-agent pool → live-unowned (feat/faab-live-unowned-fa, merge HELD)

**Commissioner decision (Jun 18 2026):** the FAAB free-agent pool changes from the Prompt-48 batch-clear **snapshot** to **live-unowned** — a player is a free agent the moment he holds no active roster spot, including a player dropped by a winning waiver bid AND a player dropped mid-window. **The anti-snipe hold (DECISIONS Prompt 48) is removed.** The eligibility predicate drops the `OR dropped_at >= batch_cleared_at` term → `EXISTS roster_player WHERE league=L AND player=X AND dropped_at IS NULL` (currently rostered ⇒ ineligible), factored into the pure IO-free `liveOwnedWhere` (`packages/faab/src/faEligibility.ts`) and shared by all THREE eligibility sites — the waivers pool (`listFaIneligiblePlayerIds`, `snapshotAt` param dropped), the per-player re-check (`getFaTargetFacts.faEligible`), and the grant tx re-check (`claimFreeAgent`) — so a player shown as a free agent is exactly one the $0 grant accepts. `/waivers` now offers the live-unowned pool in EVERY phase (the snapshot branch + dead `ownedRows` read retired). **NOT touched:** the sealed→free-agency WINDOW machinery (`acquisitionWindowState` / `batch_cleared_at` latch / kickoff cutoff / `claimFreeAgent`'s `T===null` guard / commish `--period` pin) is a SEPARATE gate; the blind-bid batch resolver, `resolve.ts`, and SCORING.md are byte-unchanged. Tests: snapshot/anti-snipe cases flipped (`handleFaGrant` "dropped this window" now 200) + new `faEligibility.test.ts` pins (a) batch-dropped→eligible, (b) mid-window drop→eligible, (c) rostered→not, (d) dropped-then-re-added→not. See DECISIONS.md → Theme D (Jun 18 2026 amendment) + ARCHITECTURE.md §3. **Merge HELD pending Chat clearance.**

### 2026-06-19 — Informational Draws in the all-play-all power record (feat/standings-draws, merge HELD)

**Commissioner decision (Jun 19 2026):** a tied matchday matchup is now **recorded as a Draw**, reversing the original Theme-C "a tie is NEITHER a W nor an L" rule. Records read **W–L–D** where **W + L + D = opponents compared** per period. **Seeding is UNCHANGED** — still `all_play_all_W` desc → `total_points` desc → `managerId` asc; **draws are informational and never enter the comparator.** This aligns the backend with `design/design_reference/standings/data.jsx`, which already modeled W/L/D (`games = W+L+D`) — the prior design-vs-backend conflict is resolved in the design's favor. **Pure** (`packages/recompute/src/standing.ts`): `PeriodRecord.d` counts the `comparePeriodPairwise` "tie" branch, `StandingComputation.allPlayAllD` sums it; seed comparator byte-untouched. **Persistence:** migration `20260619120000_standing_all_play_all_d` (`ALTER TABLE "standing" ADD COLUMN "all_play_all_d" INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` — DEFAULT 0 load-bearing so pre-restate reads return 0, not error), Prisma `Standing.allPlayAllD`, `StandingUpsert` + `recomputeStanding` payload + `upsertStanding` create/update branches. **Display (group-phase / live surfaces):** the 6 live vsfield record lines use `record.d` (already computed as `opponents − w − l`); `SeasonEntry.allPlayAllD` is threaded (`vsfield/types.ts` + `buildVsField` + `loadVsField`) into the vsfield season table, Dashboard "season W-L-D" + Standings rows, and the PrimaryBanner group-phase banner. **FLAGGED / out of this cut:** the complete/playoff-phase "power record" (Dashboard ~626, PrimaryBanner ~284) still renders `powerW–powerL` because it sources from `playoffsView` (a pure module adjacent to the SCOPE-OUT seeding logic) via `selectViewerFinish`; threading a draw there is a multi-hop change through playoff selectors + their `toEqual` tests and renders only in the not-yet-live knockout phase. **NOT touched:** seeding logic, the (C) joint-seed display, the standings page build, the net-matchday season chip, the per-opponent H2H "D" glyph. See DECISIONS.md → Theme C amendment + ARCHITECTURE.md §4. **Merge HELD (migration + purity = high-risk).**

**P0 RESOLVED.** The `faab_bid_select_settled` policy (`20260603223500_invariants`) was `TO public USING (status <> 'pending')` with **no identity predicate**, so the anon/publishable key could read **every settled FAAB bid across ALL leagues** via the PostgREST Data API (confirmed live). Fixed in migration `20260620120000_fix_faab_settled_rls` — a **DDL-only** policy swap (DROP + CREATE `TO authenticated` with a league-member EXISTS keyed on the row's own `faab_bid.league_id` + the caller's OWN `manager` row, the `draft`/`standing`/`pool_pick` idiom; **NOT** a bid-owner join, which `manager_select_own` would collapse to owner-only; no SECDEF helper since `faab_bid` carries `league_id`). Composed visibility: own pending → owner-only; settled → league member; others' pending → hidden (anti-copying); cross-league settled → hidden; anon → nothing. **No data writes on `migrate deploy`** (no embedded self-test); no portability shims (the chain provides `authenticated` + `auth.uid()` before this migration). Regression coverage = `packages/faab/src/faabSettledRls.integration.test.ts` (role-switched anon/authenticated, gated on `FAAB_RLS_PG_TEST_URL`; **+3 tests → 2397 passed | 21 skipped**). Verified on a throwaway Postgres (anon = 0, league-wide positive, cross-league negative, anti-copying preserved; the test goes red on the old policy). Render auto-applied via `preDeployCommand: pnpm db:migrate:deploy`. See DECISIONS.md → Theme F (2026-06-20 P0 learning + durable RLS rule). **Post-deploy confirm owed:** an anon Data-API SELECT on `faab_bid` returns 0 settled rows.

### 2026-06-20 — Vs-the-field H2H: opponent + own bench at the bottom (feat/vsfield-benches, MERGED + DEPLOYED `7929512`)

The live "vs the field" head-to-head now shows **each manager's bench** (the 4 non-starters) beneath the scoring XI — a scouting surface. **Display-only sibling, engine byte-untouched:** the `@app/vsfield` engine (`buildVsField`) has no bench concept and its input/output are unchanged; benches are composed in the apps/web loader and carried alongside the snapshot. **Premise correction:** `loadVsField` was **starters-only** (its `lineup_slot` read filtered `isStarter: true`), unlike `loadLineup` which reads the full squad — so the bench rows weren't even fetched. The change drops that filter so ONE query returns the full current-period lineup, selects `isStarter`, and **partitions in JS**: starters feed `buildVsField` exactly as before (so the engine input is byte-identical), bench rows feed the pure exported `groupBenchesByManager` (GK→DEF→MID→FWD order) → the new `benches` field. `loadVsField` now returns `VsFieldViewWithBenches = VsFieldView & { benches }` (`apps/web/src/vsfield/benches.ts`) — a **width-compatible** superset, so the other consumer `loadDashboard` (group/playoff modules) keeps reading the same `VsFieldView` fields and ignores `benches` (typecheck confirms). A bench entry is identity-only (name + `fifa_team.name` nation kit — NEVER `player.country`, P34 — + role); no points / lock-on-play (bench players never score). **Vsfield-surface only, server-side:** no new Realtime / RLS / migration; the `/api/vsfield` shape only gains the additive `benches` field. Tests: `loadVsField.benches.test.ts` (pure partition/sort) + `Benches.test.tsx` (jsdom bench-surface mount). Clean rebase onto `7ffd1c6`; full gate green (typecheck/lint/format/test/web build); **2436 passed | 21 skipped**. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §24 + DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-20 (display-only sibling, engine byte-untouched).

See DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-20 (dashboard standings-row link).

### 2026-06-20 — Vsfield pre-selects the H2H from the `?manager=` deep-link (feat/vsfield-manager-deeplink, MERGED + DEPLOYED `4231da0`)

`/vsfield` now **consumes** the `?manager=<id>` deep-link the dashboard standings rows carry (T3, prior entry) — clicking a row lands the viewer on `/vsfield` already focused on that manager's head-to-head, completing the dashboard→vsfield pinpoint round-trip. **Server-validated, mount-only seed:** `page.tsx` reads the Next 15 `searchParams` Promise and runs the raw param through the pure guard `seedManagerSelection(rawManager, view.field)` (`apps/web/src/vsfield/seedSelection.ts`) **before** the client sees it; the guard returns `null` (→ client keeps its existing default) for any untrusted param — **absent / empty / array (`?manager=a&manager=b`) / unknown id** — so a stale or typo'd id can never seed a non-existent manager into an empty/broken H2H. Only a valid in-field id seeds. The validated seed threads as `initialSelection` and is consumed as the `useState` **initializer** for `effSel` (`VsFieldClient.tsx`) — applied on **mount only**, so live refetches and later manual selection are never overridden / re-seeded. **Self-id collapses to `"field"`** (the aggregate cockpit), mirroring the client `select()` semantics — never an H2H against yourself. **Vsfield-surface, presentation/wiring only:** `loadVsField` and the `@app/vsfield` engine are **byte-untouched** — no loader / data-shape / engine / Realtime / RLS / migration / `/api` change. The guard is pure (no Next/React/IO), unit-tested directly (+6: valid→seed, self→`"field"`, absent/empty/array/unknown→`null`). Branch base was `origin/main` (`3cc05a2`) so the rebase was a no-op; full gate green (typecheck/lint/format/test/web build); **2446 passed | 21 skipped**. See DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-20 (vsfield consumes the `?manager=` deep-link).

### 2026-06-21 — Lineup T7 (forfeit confirm survives a period switch) + T8 (opponent tag never leaks a team UUID) (MERGED + DEPLOYED `0003f50`)

Two display-only lineup fixes, validator byte-untouched. **T7:** a forfeit-substitution confirmation is now per-period client state (`forfeitsByPeriod`) so it survives a period switch — the earlier fix (`9935472`) had stopped the spurious `forfeit-requires-confirm` error after a successful sub, but a global confirm `Set` was wiped on a period switch and re-tripped rule 4c against the prior period's un-refetched SSR locks/`slotMeta`; tracking the confirm per period (as `lineups` already is) closes it without a server `slotMeta` refetch, keeping `validateLineup` untouched. **T8:** the shared `resolveOpponentByPlayer` (used by the lineup `<OpponentTag>` and the waivers `<OpponentLine>`) now falls back to a fixed `UNNAMED_OPPONENT` sentinel when `fifa_team.name` is null — never the raw team UUID — fixing both surfaces at once in the single shared resolver. See DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-21 (forfeit confirms per-period) and ARCHITECTURE.md (`resolveOpponentByPlayer`).

### 2026-06-21 — P1 RLS fix: `pool_pick` SELECT clock-gated so pre-kickoff picks can't leak via Data API / Realtime (MERGED + DEPLOYED `4cb29e5`, live-verified)

`pool_pick` was league-member-only on SELECT **and** in the `supabase_realtime` publication, so a rival's **pre-kickoff** picks were readable cross-member through the Data API / Realtime, bypassing the `readVisiblePicks` loader gate (anon = 0, but cross-member = leak). Migration `20260621120000_fix_pool_pick_realtime_rls` now enforces the reveal rule **at the DB layer**, mirroring the loader: USING = `league-member AND (own-pick OR pool_pick_match_kicked_off(match_id))` — own picks always, others' only after their fixture kicks off. The kickoff check is a `SECURITY DEFINER` helper (not an in-policy join to the RLS-default-deny `fifa_match`, which would silently hide every not-own pick forever — the nested-RLS trap). `pg_policies` confirms the predicate and `prosecdef=t` on the helper; the role-switched `poolPickRls.integration.test.ts` suite is green (red on the old policy). Deployed and live-verified 2026-06-21. See DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-21 (`pool_pick` SELECT clock-gates the reveal) and ARCHITECTURE.md (`pool_pick` SELECT clock-gated).

### 2026-06-21 — Game detail: real-match box score + fantasy overlay (T5/T6) (feat/game-detail, **merge HELD** — review-class)

Clicking a single real WC match now opens **`/games/[matchId]`** — a full-match box score (both squads: starting XI + substitutes who came on + named bench, with subbed-on/off minutes and cards) overlaid with the **fantasy layer** (each player's points for the match + a `started`/`benched`/`owned` tag for the fantasy manager who fielded/rosters him). Reached from the **dashboard** matchday rows (`MatchRow` is now an `<a>` to `/games/<matchId>`) and from the **Quiniela** (`/pool`) fixture cards via a **separate** non-intrusive tap target on the teams-score area (a `.pl-fx-view` link + chevron) — the HOME/DRAW/AWAY pick buttons are untouched. ("The user's own Games tab" was confirmed to mean `/pool`/Quiniela; there is no surface literally named "Games".)

**Coverage gate confirmed (the contract held).** `score_player_match` (+ `stat_player_match`) exist for **every match participant**, not just rostered players: recompute's candidate set is every dirty raw stat row (`claimDirtyPlayerMatches`, NO roster join) and the only filter is the `playerAppearedInMatch` participation gate — so a full 22+ box score with fantasy points is **fully backed by stored data**, no contract change needed.

**Read-only, snapshot-not-Realtime.** New pure `buildGameDetail` (`apps/web/src/games/`) + thin owner-bypass `loadGameDetail` (`apps/web/app/games/[matchId]/`) — reads already-scored rows (`fifa_match`, `match_lineup_entry`, `stat_player_match`, `score_player_match`, `event_match`) + the owner overlay (`lineup_slot` keyed on the match's `period_id`, + `roster_player` for owned-not-fielded; manager names attached server-side). **NO engine re-run, NO recompute, NO new RLS, NO migration, NO Realtime publication change** (the boring first cut reuses the same server-only owner-bypass path `loadPlayerBox`/`loadVsField` already use; per-player live points come from `score_player_match`, which is not browser-readable and not in any publication). Nation comes from the `fifa_team.name` join — **never** the `player.country` scalar. Team names from the home/away `fifa_team` join, falling back to the shared `UNNAMED_OPPONENT` (never a raw UUID). A null `fifa_match.period_id` renders the box score with **no owner overlay** rather than erroring. The per-player drill-in **reuses `PlayerScoreSheet` verbatim** (info-only, like /vsfield) — tap a row → the existing `/api/player-box` modal. **Out of scope (as specified):** two-yellow→red dismissal banding (needs cross-row pairing at aggregation) — rows are shown as `classifyCard` classifies them.

Tests: pure `buildGameDetail.test.ts` (10: role grouping, card classification incl. the 2nd-yellow staying a 2nd yellow + rescinded/non-card ignored, sub on/off, owner overlay states, team-name fallback, period-null degradation, unresolved counts) + `loadGameDetail.contract.test.ts` (source-contract smoke: reuse sites, the `fifa_team.name`/no-`player.country` discipline, period-keyed overlay, the read-only invariant) + assertions added to `dashboard.test.ts` and `poolContracts.test.ts` for the click wirings. Also added `BACKLOG.md` to `.prettierignore` (it was the one brain doc omitted from the existing "do-not-reflow" list — pre-existing drift was failing `format:check`). Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/test/web build); **2466 passed | 24 skipped**; `/games/[matchId]` builds as `ƒ` (dynamic). Merge **HELD** for Chat clearance (review-class). See ARCHITECTURE.md → §25 + DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-21 (game detail = read-only snapshot, no Realtime widening).

### 2026-06-21 — T11 corrections round 2: prior-lineup SCORES + batch results player-grouping (fix/t11-corrections-2, merged `938ab21`)

Two more display items from live verification, stacking on `fix/t11-corrections`. Still **display-only** — no engine, validator, write path, RLS, migration, or Realtime change.

**Fix A-2 — the Lineup pitch shows per-player points on every LOCKED-ON-PLAY tile + the manager's canonical matchday total, on the CURRENT matchday and prior ones alike.** A starter tile drew a **bare padlock with no number** wherever its match had kicked off — most visibly on the current matchday, where the **bench already showed its points in the same view**. Root cause: the bench renders its pill on the single slot-level condition `!movable` (locked-on-play), but the pitch token gated the pill on the period-level `readOnly` flag and on `slotKind`/`pointsAtStake>0` — and `classifySlot` only returns `"played-starter"` when `meta.movable` (=`!periodFrozen`) holds, so a kicked-off/frozen starter fell to `"locked"` → the padlock branch won. Fix: the pitch token's pill now keys on the **SAME `!movable` (locked-on-play) condition the bench uses** — decoupled from `readOnly` and `slotKind`. A locked-on-play tile shows its `pointsAtStake` as the existing clickable `ScorePill` (tap → period-aware `PlayerScoreSheet`); a not-yet-kicked-off (movable) starter keeps its plain editable token (no pill). **Movability is unchanged** (`movable ? onSelect : onScore`, exactly the bench split — locked stays non-movable/non-selectable, movable stays selectable; no write path). The headline **matchday total** reads **`score_manager_period.points`** — the SAME column `loadStandings` reads — single-sourced via pure `selectManagerPeriodTotal(rows, periodId)` onto `PeriodLineup.matchdayTotal`, shown in a `MatchdayTotalBanner` **whenever the period has a stored total** (a completed prior matchday OR the current one once scoring starts; absent pre-kickoff). It **picks the stored total, never re-sums per-player points**, so the lineup number reconciles with standings by construction. Scoped read: a server-only owner-bypass Prisma read of an already-public-server-side table (no new RLS/policy/Realtime).

**Fix B-2 — Batch results group by PLAYER, all bids together.** Settled-batch results were one row per bid ordered by amount, so a contested player's competing bids scattered (the live example: Felix Nmecha appeared twice — Jager FC WON $15 dropping Ugarte, yader.rosales LOST $7). Pure presentation regroup `groupResultsByPlayer(results)` collapses each contested player to **one entry** (`WvResultGroup`) with all bids beneath, amount-desc (winner on top); the dropped-player detail still rides the winning bid, the matchday label stays on the batch header. **No new query** (the loader already returns every winning + losing + voided bid), no schema/RLS/engine change; the player identity + flag render once on the group header. Tests: pure `selectManagerPeriodTotal` (single-source / not-a-re-sum) + `groupResultsByPlayer` (one entry per player, winner-on-top, loses no bid) + jsdom render proofs — `LineupScores` mounts the real `SetLineupClient` and asserts a locked-on-play starter shows its points pill **on the editable (readOnly=false) period**, the total banner shows there, a not-yet-kicked-off starter still selects while the locked one cannot (movability unchanged), banner absent pre-scoring; plus `batchResultsGroupingRender` (one Nmecha entry shows winner + loser). Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/**2515** tests/web build); merged `938ab21`. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §26 + BACKLOG.md → T11.

### 2026-06-21 — T11 corrections: lineup historical snapshot + waivers period-scoping (fix/t11-corrections, merged `b309003`)

Two corrections found in live verification of T11 (entry below). Both **display-only** — no engine, validator, write path, RLS, migration, or Realtime change.

**Fix A — Lineup prior matchday shows the HISTORICAL lineup, including since-dropped players.** Viewing a prior (completed) matchday omitted a player who was FIELDED that matchday but DROPPED afterward, because the prior-period XI was rendered from the CURRENT active roster (`roster_player WHERE dropped_at IS NULL` → `squad`) instead of that period's `lineup_slot` snapshot. **Landmine confirmed clear** (adversarially verified): a fielded player's slot is **locked-on-play at kickoff**, and the FAAB drop reconciliation (`releaseDroppedPlayerSlots`) deletes only UNLOCKED slots — so the locked historical row survives the drop (a DB trigger also blocks deleting any locked slot), and `lineup_slot` references `player` directly (no `roster_player` FK) so the soft-delete drop never cascades into it. `loadLineup` already read every saved slot by `managerId` alone, so the dropped player's id was already present — only his identity was missing. Fix: the loader derives the slot-only player ids (in a shown period's `lineup_slot` but absent from the current roster = since-dropped), fetches their identity (name/position/`teamId` + `fifa_team.name` for nation, mirroring the roster select), and attaches a per-period **`snapshotPlayers`** (the period's full slot set, resolved) to each **read-only** period; the score/kickoff/opponent/availability maps widen to that display universe so a dropped starter's points + fixture resolve. The client renders a read-only period from `snapshotPlayers` (else the live `squad`), so a prior matchday shows exactly who was set — **the editable current/upcoming path is byte-unchanged** (no snapshot → `squad`).

**Fix B — Waivers: the period concept is confined to the Batch results tab.** T11 over-applied a top "Stat sheet" matchday selector that re-scoped the player drill-down across the whole screen. The FA pool / claims / player cards are live/global and period-less by design, so that selector is **removed** (the drill-down is always the period-less `FaPlayerCardSheet`; `loadWaivers` no longer computes `selectablePeriods`/`currentPeriodId`, and the period read narrows back to `matches[0].kickoffAt`). The period concept now lives ONLY in **Batch results**, where each settled batch is **labelled with the matchday/round it cleared** — the period whose `batch_cleared_at` equals the batch's `run_at` (stamped together in one settlement transaction; `faab_batch` has no `period_id`). Display-only: no `take:5` change, no schema/engine change. (Chat chose "matchday labels only" over a selector/filter or a durable `period_id` column — the latter would touch the live FAAB settlement transaction.)

Tests: a real jsdom render proof `PriorMatchdaySnapshot.test.tsx` (a since-dropped starter appears on the prior matchday's pitch; a control proves he would be absent without the snapshot) + `batchResultsMatchday.test.tsx` (the over-applied selector is gone; the FA card is the period-less `FaPlayerCardSheet`; the batch card carries its matchday label). Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/test/web build). Merged `b309003`. New BACKLOG: **SEC-P3b** (lineup validator has no period-done gate — defense-in-depth) + **T16** (re-skin `/games/[matchId]`, sequenced after T12). See ARCHITECTURE.md → §26 + BACKLOG.md.

### 2026-06-21 — Prior-matchday stat-sheet selector: Lineup / Vs-the-Field / Waivers (T11) (feat/prior-matchday-selector, merged `9c9ceb5`)

A matchday **selector** on the three live surfaces so a user can open the full stat sheet for any **prior** (completed) matchday, not just the live one. The box-score read path (`PlayerScoreSheet` → `/api/player-box` → `loadPlayerBox`, period-scoped, no clock gate) was already period-aware and is **reused verbatim**; the only gap was period SELECTION. **Every period read — current and prior — goes through the EXISTING period-aware read model with a `periodId` param; no parallel/ungated read path is added** (the single read path is what preserves each surface's posture).

**Premise correction (cleared by Chat).** The original prompt assumed a vsfield "reveal gate" (`readVisiblePicks`) that gates the current live period. There is none: **vsfield shows every manager's XI by design** (ARCHITECTURE §5 — squads are public from the draft; only pick'em *picks* are secret, and `readVisiblePicks` is the *pool* surface). T11 builds on that existing posture: prior periods reveal fully because they are over, the current period is unchanged, and the selector can never reach a future one.

**Shared started-set helper** (`apps/web/src/period/selectablePeriods.ts`, pure, tested): `periodHasStarted` = `isPickLocked` (`@app/pool`) on the first fixture (no new clock predicate), `periodIsDone` = last kickoff + `MATCH_DURATION_MS`, `selectableStartedPeriods` (started-only, `sortByPeriodOrder`, **future/unstarted NEVER selectable**), `resolveDisplayedPeriodId` (server-side future-rejection; the no-request path is byte-identical to pre-T11).

**Per surface.** **Lineup** extends `PeriodTabs` with the prior periods the manager played, **strictly read-only** (`readOnly = periodIsDone`, anchored on the fixtures' clock NOT `period.status` which stays `"pending"` in prod) — the client gates the formation picker, Save, AND the tap-to-swap `onSelect` (closing the never-appeared-bench-player gap the review caught); default pinned to the live wave. **The write path is unchanged** — POST `/api/lineup` rejects any edit touching a played slot via the lock-on-play latch + forfeit play-state rules (the data-integrity backstop; the read-only flag is a UI layer). **Vs the Field** threads `?period=` → `loadVsField(periodId)` (downstream reads follow the resolved period; `selectablePeriods` + `isLivePeriod` added as sibling fields; `buildVsField` untouched); the client renders the selector and **suppresses the Realtime subscription for a prior** (static → "Final" pill), keying the client on the period so a switch remounts. **Waivers** keeps the FA pool / claims / batch window live/global (Jun 18 2026 commish decision) and re-scopes ONLY the drill-down: a client `selectedPeriodId` (default = current) swaps the card from the period-less `FaPlayerCardSheet` to `PlayerScoreSheet` for a prior matchday.

**Invariants held.** No new table, no RLS, no migration, no Realtime publication change; no new write path. One latent finding recorded, **not fixed here** (pre-existing, byte-identical default pre/post-T11): vsfield reveals the next wave's XIs during the inter-matchday gap → **BACKLOG SEC-P3** for a commish decision.

Tests: pure `selectablePeriods.test.ts` (started-set, ordering, isLive/isDone, future-excluded, resolver future-rejection, gap force-include — the loader contract for the `periodId`-param path) + a prior-matchday read-only block in `controller.test.ts` (the write path rejects edits to played slots; an identical re-save is an inert no-op) + a real jsdom render proof `ReadOnlyPeriod.test.tsx` (Save + formation picker disabled, a movable token tap performs no swap, with an editable-period control). Adversarially reviewed (4-lens workflow): reveal-safety PASS, read-only integrity PASS. Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/test/web build); **2490 passed | 24 skipped**. Merged `9c9ceb5`. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §26 + DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-21 (prior-matchday selector; vsfield has no reveal gate).

### 2026-06-21 — T12: "Season" tab + season-by-matchday score grid on `/standings` (feat/season-tab, contained → Code-autonomous merge on green)

The `/standings` page gains a third tab, **Season**, alongside Matchday + Cumulative: a managers × matchdays **score matrix** — rows = managers (in the cumulative season-seed order), columns = matchdays (in canonical order), each cell = that manager's points for that matchday, with a trailing **Total** column. **Presentation only** — the per-(manager, matchday) points were already computed and threaded onto the existing `StandingsView` (each cumulative `StandingsRow` carries `perPeriod[]`, aligned to `view.periods`, since `131eaba`/T10). STEP 1 confirmed the data is fully present, so **no new query, no new scoring, no schema/RLS/migration/Realtime change** was needed.

**Pure re-projection (`buildSeasonGrid`).** A new pure helper `buildSeasonGrid(view: StandingsView): SeasonGrid` in `@app/recompute` (`packages/recompute/src/standingsView.ts`, auto-exported via the barrel's `export * from "./standingsView"`) re-projects the SAME view into the grid — it adds **no arithmetic**: columns follow `view.periods` (the loader's `sortByPeriodOrder`), rows follow `view.cumulative` (the season seed), each started cell reads the cumulative row's `perPeriod` points (re-keyed by `periodId` so cells stay column-aligned even if the strip and `periods` orders ever diverge), and the Total column **echoes the cumulative PF** (`row.points`) — which equals the Σ of the started cells by construction, so the grid **can never disagree with the Cumulative tab**. A column is `started` iff it is **live OR has ≥1 scored row** (`view.matchday[id].length > 0`) — the SAME started-set convention the Matchday tab uses; **unstarted (future) matchdays render an empty cell** (points `null`), while a **live-but-unscored** matchday is `started` → a real 0. `rank`/`qualified`/`isMe` are carried through from the cumulative row.

**Render + wiring.** `SeasonGridPanel` (`apps/web/app/standings/components.tsx`) renders a real `<table>` inside a horizontally-scrollable container (`.st-season-scroll`, `overflow-x:auto`) with a **sticky Manager column** (`position:sticky; left:0`, opaque background so scrolled cells slide under it) — read-only (no per-row expansion). `StandingsClient` widens its `Tab` union to add `"season"`, adds the tab button (same shape as the siblings), and renders `{tab === "season" && <SeasonGridPanel grid={buildSeasonGrid(view)} />}` — computed inline off the same `view` state, so it rides the existing Realtime nudge → `GET /api/standings` refetch → re-render path for free (no new live wiring). `standings.css` gains a `.st-season-*` block (real ds tokens, gold-free; `--accent-soft` = you; mobile = horizontal scroll, full mobile polish deferred to T15). Reuses `MgrAvatar` / `.st-mgr-name` / `.st-you` / `.st-fc-dot` verbatim.

**Closes SEC-P3.** Commish decision (recorded here): the inter-matchday-gap XI reveal on Vs-the-Field is **accepted** — upcoming lineups are public by design (ARCHITECTURE §5), so vsfield will NOT gain a per-fixture reveal gate (won't-fix/accepted). **T16** (re-skin `/games/[matchId]`) is now NEXT; its design source is `~/Downloads/design_handoff_match_detail`, and step 0 of that thread is to move it into `design/design_reference/match_detail/` and commit before building.

Tests: +7 contract tests in `standingsView.test.ts` (`buildSeasonGrid` — canonical column order + season-seed row order, started-cell fill aligned to columns, Total = cumulative PF, unstarted → null cell, live-but-unscored → real 0, rank/qualified/isMe passthrough, empty-periods, and a started-but-absent manager → real 0 with total = PF). Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/**2522 passed | 24 skipped**/web build). Adversarially reviewed by a 4-lens workflow (run single-concurrency to ride out a transient API 529 overload that failed three parallel attempts): **7 confirmed findings, all P3 polish, all fixed pre-merge** — the `is-me` sticky-cell translucent bleed (composite `--accent-soft` over an opaque base), an empty-state guard for the pre-tournament no-columns case, the not-started cell `role="img"`, a sticky-column width cap (`.st-season-mgr-inner` max-width) so long names ellipse, the sticky divider drawn via `box-shadow: inset` (not a collapsed `border-right` that doesn't travel with the sticky cell), and a live/not-started header `title`; the started-but-absent test closes the lone coverage gap. No correctness/scoring/a11y-breaking issues. Contained (presentation-only, no schema/purity/auth) → Code-autonomous merge on the green gate. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §23 + BACKLOG.md → T12.
