### 2026-06-22 — T17: Game-detail **Statistics** tab (team match-stats) (feat/team-match-stats, merged `1662356` + `4840f1f`)

`/games/[matchId]` gains a **Statistics** tab — a home-vs-away team-stat comparison (possession, xG, big chances, shots/on-target/blocked/woodwork, corners, offsides, passes + accuracy %, tackles/interceptions/clearances, duels won %, GK saves, fouls, yellow cards) rendered exactly as `design/design_reference/match_detail/`: six grouped sections (Overview/Shots/Attacking/Passing/Defending/Discipline) of two-track comparison bars (teal home / violet away data hues), the leading side's number brightened (inverted for the lower-is-better stats). It sits between **Lineups** and **Ratings** and appears **only when the feed has posted team stats** (graceful for not-played / early-live matches; any metric the feed omits renders "–").

**Shipped as display-only / fantasy-safe — three contained touches, NO scoring/engine/recompute/resolver/RLS/Realtime/migration change** (grep-confirmed: `stat_team_match` is feed→ingest→DB; its only Prisma accessor is the ingest upsert; the recompute/resolver read surface provably excludes it):

1. **Ingest-retain (live worker).** `mapTeamStat` (`packages/ingest/src/map.ts`) now retains the FULL remaining BALLDONTLIE team payload in `stat_team_match.extra` (JSONB) via a new catch-all `buildTeamStatExtra` + `TEAM_STAT_EXTRA_OMIT` (omit = the three typed feed keys `possession_pct`/`offsides`/`shots_blocked` + the identity/structural keys `match_id`/`team_id`/`is_home`) — mirroring the player-stat `extra` path exactly (skip-undefined / keep-explicit-null / empty→null). The three typed columns (`possession`←`possession_pct`, `offsides`, `shots_blocked`), the `requireNumber` id guards, and the `matchId_teamId` upsert key are **byte-untouched**; the prismaStore write adds `extra` with the same `Prisma.DbNull` handling as the player path. **NO migration** — the `extra` column pre-existed (was written by nothing until now).
2. **Additive read (loader).** `loadGameDetail` gains a read-only `prisma.statTeamMatch.findMany({ where: { matchId } })` inside the existing parallel read, passed through `BuildGameDetailInput.teamStats` verbatim — **mirroring the T16 Ratings additive-read pattern exactly**: the loader stays thin/read-only (the contract test still bars writes), and the home/away assignment + resolution live in the **pure** builder. Same server-only Prisma owner-bypass posture as the rest of the loader.
3. **Pure builder.** `buildGameDetail` emits a new `statistics` view-model (`GameStatistics` — grouped `GameStatRow`s with `home`/`away`/`format`/`neutral`). Each side is matched by `stat_team_match.team_id === match.homeTeamId`/`awayTeamId` (the ids already on the input — **not** an `extra.is_home` key); values come from the typed columns + parsed `extra`, with pass-accuracy and duels-won derived as each side's **own rate** (`accurate/total·100`, rounded). Pure/deterministic, null-safe (a missing metric → `null` → the client renders "–"); returns `null` when neither side has a row OR every value is null, which hides the tab.

**Scoped out:** the design's **All / 1st-half split** — the feed provides full-match team aggregates only (no per-half team data), so the tab is full-match.

**Backfill — `job:ingest-team-stats` worker CLI (Option A, approved).** Completed matches ingested **before** this landed carry an empty `extra` (rich rows render "–"), and `ingestSettle` — the only path that touches completed matches — does **not** re-pull team stats by design; the only team-stats pull is `ingestLive`. So a one-time enrichment is needed. The new CLI lists `fifa_match WHERE status='completed'` (ALL completed matches — MD1, MD2, and any later; all were ingested pre-T17) and runs `@app/ingest`'s new `ingestTeamStats(feed, store, matchBdlId)` per match — a thin extraction of `ingestLive`'s team block that reuses the **EXACT** `mapTeamStat` + `store.upsertTeamStat` path and the same `eachItem` per-item isolation + foreign-event guard, with **per-match try/catch** so one bad match logs and is skipped without aborting the run. It writes `stat_team_match` **only** — no player dirty-mark, no recompute (the team-stat write path never populated `touched`, verified) — and is idempotent via the `(match_id, team_id)` upsert. Run **after deploy** from the wc-fantasy-worker Shell: `pnpm --filter @app/worker job:ingest-team-stats`.

Tests: `map.test.ts` gains team-`extra` retention cases (omit-set proven · explicit-null kept · empty→null); `buildGameDetail.test.ts` gains a `statistics` contract pin (group order/titles · typed-column + extra sourcing · derived own-rate pcts · per-side null → "–" · null block hides the tab) threaded through the existing fixtures; `loadGameDetail.contract.test.ts` pins the new `statTeamMatch.findMany` read; `ingest.test.ts` gains `ingestTeamStats` cases (typed-cols + `extra` upserted · foreign rows skipped · counts · **no `markPlayersDirty`** = no recompute). Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/**2530 passed | 24 skipped**/`@app/web` build; `/games/[matchId]` = `ƒ` dynamic). Merged to main `1662356` + `4840f1f`. See ARCHITECTURE.md → `stat_team_match` (now retains the full payload; the games loader is its first consumer) + BACKLOG.md → T17.

### 2026-06-21 — T16: `/games/[matchId]` re-skin to the match-detail design handoff (feat/game-detail-reskin, merged `cbf4d36`)

The T5/T6 game-detail page (a two-column box-score) is **re-skinned to the Sofascore-depth match-detail design** (`design/design_reference/match_detail/`, committed `36642b4`). The page now reads as: a **scoreboard** (team names + flag-kit crests + big score that turns `--live` red while `in_progress`, a status/clock pill, kickoff + matchday meta) → a **FANTASY exposure line** ("Feeds N of the league's XIs · S started, B benched") → the personal **YOUR-XI stake strip** (your owned players as chips — kit · surname · rating square · fantasy pts — + a combined `+N your fpts`) → a **two-tab bar** over **Lineups** (a both-teams **formation pitch** of flag-kit jersey tokens in position lanes, each carrying a rating corner, a `YOU` chip / rival dot, and a fantasy-pts foot; a legend; then enriched **team lists** — Starting XI / Substitutes / Bench rows with kit · name · pos badge · owner chip · goal/assist/card/sub-minute glyphs · fpts · rating) and **Ratings** (a top-3 **podium** with a #1 green wash, a **Fantasy MVP** banner, and a full ranked board). Every player shows the product's two clearly-separated lenses — the **0–10 RATING** square (real match) and the **FANTASY** points + owner tag (league) — never blurred.

**Visual re-skin only; the loader/`buildGameDetail` contract is reused verbatim except ONE approved additive read.** Per the Step-1 mapping (PAUSED for Sergio's sign-off), three design tabs are **unbacked by loaded data and deferred** (see BACKLOG): **Statistics** (no team match-stats are kept — `stat_team_match` drops the rich payload at ingest, ARCHITECTURE schema note), **Events timeline + the scoreboard scorers row** (the builder folds events into per-player facts and emits no ordered array — a pure-builder contract change ⇒ **T16b**), and **Standings** (the app has only the fantasy power-record, not a real FIFA group table). Also dropped (not loaded): formation strings, coach, shirt numbers, captain badge, card minutes, VAR rows, venue/competition/stage meta — so pitch tokens render by position lane + kit (no number).

**The one contract touch — the Ratings additive read.** `loadGameDetail` gains a read-only `prisma.ratingPlayerMatch.findMany({ where: { matchId } })` (source-tagged 0–10 ratings, already populated by balldontlie ingestion — `ingest/prismaStore.ts:upsertRatingBalldontlie`, and ingestion's settle-mode waits on `hasRating`); the rows pass through `BuildGameDetailInput.ratings` to the **pure** `buildGameDetail`, which resolves ONE rating per player via the shared `resolveRating` (`@app/recompute`, manual > balldontlie — so the displayed number matches scoring) onto a new nullable `PlayerLine.rating` ("–" until the feed posts one). **NO migration / RLS / Realtime / write-path / engine / validator / scoring change** — same server-only owner-bypass posture as the rest of the loader; the contract test now pins the new read, and the read-only invariant (no `recompute`/write strings in the loader — the pick lives in the builder) still holds. `PlayerScoreSheet` is reused **unchanged** for the per-player drill-down (period-keyed; rows/tokens/chips/podium are plain divs, not dead buttons, when the match has no fantasy period). Reuses `app/vsfield/kitOf.ts` for the flag-kit jerseys (the multi-layer-background kit gotcha respected — kits set via inline `background`, never `background-size: cover`). `page.tsx` / `layout.tsx` (AppShell `active="pool"`) untouched. `games.css` rewritten as a route-scoped `.gd-*` port of the design `.md-*` on ds tokens only (responsive: desktop horizontal pitch → mobile vertical, away-on-top).

Tests: `buildGameDetail.test.ts` gains the rating-resolution case (single source · manual overrides balldontlie · present-but-null and absent → null) threaded through the existing fixtures; `loadGameDetail.contract.test.ts` pins the `ratingPlayerMatch` read. Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/**2523 passed | 24 skipped**/`@app/web` build; `/games/[matchId]` = `ƒ` dynamic). Adversarially reviewed by a 4-lens workflow (contract-preservation · correctness · design-fidelity · CSS/reuse) → **no P0/P1/P2; 5 P3 polish findings**, the 3 high-confidence ones fixed pre-merge (stake-chip `:not(button)` cursor guard, `fpt`/`fpts` pluralization, "in this match"). Merged to main `cbf4d36`. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §25 (+ the `stat_team_match` schema correction) + BACKLOG.md → T16 / T16b.

### 2026-06-22 — Kickoff-XI reconciliation: `/games` pitch = the computed kickoff XI, not the raw `is_starter` sheet (feat/lineup-kickoff-reconciliation, merged `8385c93`)

**Problem (data-confirmed, NOT an ingest bug).** BALLDONTLIE over-marks `match_lineup_entry.is_starter` on some completed matches, so the games pitch could render 12 (or be re-added to 11 incorrectly). `match_lineup_entry` is `(match_id, player_id, is_starter)` only — no `team_id` (team via `player.team_id`), no `is_substitute` — so the prior T-PITCH (`02ebfe5`) / `roleFor` fixes, which trust `is_starter` as the XI, couldn't help. Substitution events are ground truth for who was on at kickoff.

**Fix — read-time reconciliation cascade in the pure `buildGameDetail` (no ingest / stored-data change).** Per side: candidates = (`is_starter` rows) ∪ (any substitution `player_out`); keep iff (a) NOT a `player_in` (a come-on is a Sub — came-on wins even if he later went off), AND (b) he was a `player_out`, OR `minutes_played > 0`, OR named in an on-field event (a card/goal — keeps a null-minute red card). A flagged starter with no minutes + no events is a feed phantom → dropped to the bench. The phantom drop fires **only for a TERMINAL match (`completed`/`abandoned`) with minute data** — a live match ingests minutes per-player incrementally, so before full-time the announced sheet is kept (the came-on removal / player_out add-back still apply via events). `pitch` = the kept set = `starters` on a sheet side (invariant `starters === pitch` held); a no-sheet side keeps the appearance-inference fallback with an empty pitch. Validated against the three real bad shapes (England — Saka flagged but a come-on → dropped, Madueke kept; Czechia — Jurásek null-minute phantom → bench; Croatia — off-sheet Modrić re-added via the player_out union → 11 not 10), each → exactly 11.

**Safety net (≠11 logs-not-drops).** When a side can't resolve to 11 the builder renders the kept set as-is (never padded/trimmed) and returns a `LineupAnomaly`; the loader `console.warn`s it (matchId/teamId/count/kept/removed) — observable, never swallowed. **Sub-pairing badges:** withdrawn starters show "↓ min′ for [replacement]", come-ons show "↑ min′ for [replaced]", from the `player_in ↔ player_out` pairing.

**Scope:** pure-builder contract change (`PlayerLine.subbedOff/OnForName`, `LineupAnomaly`, `GameDetailView.lineupAnomalies`) + read-only loader (the cascade's columns — `player_in_id`/`player_out_id`/`minutes_played` — were already selected; the only loader addition is the anomaly log) + client `RowGlyphs` "for [name]" badges. **NO engine/recompute/resolver/scoring/RLS/migration/Realtime/write change**; `PlayerScoreSheet` and ingest untouched. Test-first (England/Czechia/Croatia RED on main first), then GREEN. Adversarially reviewed by a 5-lens workflow → cascade-logic caught a **P1** (a GLOBAL `matchHasMinutes` collapsed a LIVE XI the instant the first minute posted, since the page is `force-dynamic` over in-progress matches), fixed by the terminal-status gate + a live/partial-minutes regression fixture; the other four lenses (invariant, scope-fence, badges/types, safety-net) passed. Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/**2548 passed | 24 skipped**/`@app/web` build; `/games/[matchId]` = `ƒ` dynamic). Merged to main `8385c93`. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §25 (Kickoff-XI reconciliation) + BACKLOG.md → T-RECON.

### 2026-06-22 — Mobile pitch CENTERLINE BLEED fix: full-token-height sizing + halfway-line containment (merged `d7efad8`)

**Regression (surfaced by real MD2 data).** With the empty-pitch collapse already fixed and shipped, dense real lineups (most players carrying a rating square AND many a `▾{min}′` sub-off badge; two-digit fantasy points) made each side's bands **overflow their 50% half and bleed across the halfway line** — the two teams' attacking lines collided in the centre (screenshot-confirmed on Sergio's iPhone: NED–SWE, ARG–AUT).

**Root cause (two compounding parts, traced + guard-reproduced).** (1) The fantasy-points NUMBER is pinned at a fixed **13px** on mobile: `.gd-fpts-sm b{font-size:13px}` outranks the mobile `.gd-tok-foot .gd-fpts{font-size:clamp(...)}` rule (which sizes only the `.gd-fpts` SPAN, not its `<b>`), so the points pill stayed ~20px tall (13px number + 1px border) regardless of `--shirt` — far over the `0.42×line` jersey budget. (2) `--gd-line-h = 50cqh/--pitch-rows` never subtracted the `.gd-phalf` vertical padding nor the inter-band `.gd-pcol` sub-line gaps, so `rows×line-h` overshot the half's true content height; and `.gd-phalf` had no overflow containment, so the overshoot bled across centre instead of clipping. **The old guard couldn't see any of this** — its token replica rendered a bare `gd-fpts` (no `gd-fpts-sm`), so its `<b>` shrank with the jersey and it measured a fake ~8px foot, and it had no within-half / centerline assertion at all (only "within the whole pitch").

**Fix (presentation/CSS only, mobile `@media max-width:720px` block).** (a) **Unpin the number** — `.gd-tok-foot .gd-fpts b{font-size:inherit}` (+ `small`) so the whole pill scales with the jersey. (b) **Budget the FULL token** — reserve the name + foot strip EXPLICITLY (`--gd-name-h`, `--gd-foot-h`, applied as the actual pinned heights of `.gd-tok-name`/`.gd-tok-foot`) and subtract `2×--gd-half-pad + --gd-grid-slack` from the line budget, so `--gd-shirt-h = line − strip − gaps` and **token height ≡ line height by construction** (`--shirt` floor 11→7 — legibility yields to the fit). (c) **`.gd-phalf{overflow:hidden}`** as a hard centerline barrier. The jersey shrinks before anything clips; the fit still wins. `buildGameDetail.ts`/`types.ts`/`pitchRows.ts`/`GameDetailClient.tsx` byte-untouched; desktop (516px) unaffected.

**Guard hardening (the part that kept failing — now closed).** `verify-pitch-layout.mjs`: (i) the fpts replica renders the real `gd-fpts gd-fpts-sm` classes so it measures the SAME 13px-pinned number; (ii) `squad()` is now a REAL worst-case dense XI (every player rated, ~half subbed off, two-digit points, real-length names); (iii) new assertion **(e) NO CROSS-CENTERLINE** — no away token's `getBoundingClientRect` box reaches below the pitch vertical midpoint, none of home's above it (it uses the LAYOUT box, NOT the visual clip, so the fix must genuinely RESIZE tokens, not just clip them). **Proven red → green:** 12 phone-667 checks all crossing centre on the shipped CSS → **30/30 green** on the fix.

**Gate.** typecheck · lint · format:check · `pnpm test` (2557 passed / 24 skipped) · `pnpm --filter @app/web build` · `pnpm test:layout` (30/30, dense fixture + centerline assertion) — all green. Decisive gate is Sergio's iPhone on a high-sub match. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §25 (full-token-height sizing + centerline containment; guard (e)). [skip render]

### 2026-06-22 — Mobile pitch height-chain fix: the flex-fill needs a DEFINITE-height chain that production never had (merged `33e71e5`)

**Regression.** On a real iPhone the game-detail **Lineups** tab rendered an **empty pitch** (collapsed to ~0 height). The `pnpm test:layout` guard was green because it ran against a **fabricated definite-height shell** — it forced `html,body{height:100%}` and mounted `.sh-app` directly under `<body>`, skipping the real route-layout wrapper — so the height chain could never go indefinite and the collapse was invisible.

**Root cause (real ancestry traced `.gd-pitch` → `html`).** The mobile pitch fills its tab body via `height:100cqh`, and `.gd-tabwrap` is a `container-type:size` container — so its block-size MUST come from an external definite-height chain (size containment zeroes its own intrinsic height). The App Shell's `.sh-app{height:100%}` is the design's propagation hook, **but its containing block is the route layout's `<div data-theme>` wrapper, which has no definite height** → `.sh-app{height:100%}` computes to `auto`. `.sh-app{min-height:100dvh}` gives it a 100dvh *used* height, **but a used-size-via-`min-height` is NOT "definite"** for percentage/flex resolution → `.gd-app{height:100%}` also → `auto` → `.gd-tabwrap` collapses to **0px** → pitch `100cqh` ≈ 0. (`.sh-app`/`.sh-content` still measure 100dvh via the min-height floor + flex stretch — which is exactly why it looked fine in DevTools’ box model but the pitch was empty.) The **first indeterminate link is `.sh-app`**; nothing above it (`html`/`body`/wrapper) supplies a definite height. Empirically reproduced: real chain → `.gd-tabwrap` 0px, pitch 2px (clientHeight 0); the guard's fabricated chain → 373px.

**Fix — re-establish the definite chain for the games route only (no chrome constant).** `GameDetailLayout`'s wrapper `<div>` gets a `gd-host` class (**the single structural JSX touch**); games.css (inside `@media max-width:720px`) adds `:root:has(.gd-host), body:has(.gd-host){height:100%}` + `.gd-host{height:100%}`. `<html>` resolves `height:100%` against the viewport (always definite), so html → body → `.gd-host` → `.sh-app` → … → `.gd-tabwrap` is now an unbroken `height:100%` chain and `100cqh` resolves. `:has(.gd-host)` scopes it to this route — every other screen is byte-unchanged (all routes wrap `AppShell` in an unclassed `<div data-theme>`, so `.sh-app{height:100%}`→`auto` everywhere; `/draft` brings its own `.dr-app{height:100dvh}`, the rest scroll naturally — only the games mobile `cqh` chain ever needed the height to *propagate through* `.sh-app`). Still **no `dvh − <guess>` math.** Post-fix: pitch 339px @ 360×667, 516px @ 360×844.

**Guard hardened (this is why it slipped).** `verify-pitch-layout.mjs` now **mounts the REAL ancestry** — `<html>` (no height) → `<body class="min-h-screen">` (Tailwind `min-height:100vh`, emulated) → `<div.gd-host data-theme>` wrapper → `.sh-app`/`.sh-topbar`/`.sh-content`/`.sh-btmnav` → `.gd-app` → `.gd-tabwrap` → `.gd-lineups` — with NO forced root height, so it collapses iff the real chain collapses. New **non-collapse floor (a0)**: on phone viewports `.gd-tabwrap ≥ 40% of clientH`, pitch fills it (`≥ 90% of .gd-tabwrap`), pitch clears `≈ 0.36·clientH` (≈240px @ 667), and every token has non-zero width AND height — an empty pitch is RED. **Proven red→green:** with the games.css fix reverted, every phone viewport fails (`.gd-tabwrap 0px`, `pitch 0px below the non-collapse floor`); with it restored, all 30 checks pass.

**Scope:** CSS + one layout class + guard. **NO loader/builder/`side.pitch`/`pitchRows.ts`/engine/RLS/migration/write change.** Full DoD gate green (typecheck / lint / format / **2557 passed | 24 skipped** / `@app/web` build) + `pnpm test:layout` 30 checks green @ 667 + 844 with the floor. Merged `33e71e5`. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §25 (height-chain correction + hardened guard).

### 2026-06-22 — Mobile pitch = formation grid (merged `33e71e5`; SUPERSEDES lane-wrapping below)

**Problem.** Lane-wrapping stopped the clip but the mobile pitch was still unreadable: one tall single-file column per side — oversized tokens, bands stacked DOWN the screen instead of spread ACROSS it, the pitch grown to `min-height:600px` so the page scrolled (screenshot-confirmed broken on a real iPhone). Visual target was approved in Chat via mockup.

**Fix — a true formation grid.** On a phone the pitch turns vertical (away top / home bottom, halfway split), bands stack down the short axis, and **each band's players spread horizontally across the width** (a flat back-4 = 4 tokens side-by-side, `justify-content:space-evenly`). The pitch **FLEX-FILLS the real leftover height and NEVER scrolls/grows** (`overflow:hidden`), and **every token is auto-sized off the pitch's true height** so the FULL XI of both sides fits with no scroll — token size is the variable, legibility yields to the fit.

**Height model (revised after review — no magic constant).** The first cut used `height: min(540px, calc(100dvh − 232px))`, which hardcodes a 232px chrome estimate. Replaced with a real flex-fill, scoped to the lineups tab via `.gd-app:has(.gd-lineups)`: `.gd-app` is bounded to the shell content area (`height:100%; overflow:hidden`), the back/scoreboard/stake/tab bar are fixed rows (`flex:0 0 auto`), and the tab body `.gd-tabwrap` takes the rest (`flex:1; min-height:0; overflow-y:auto`) as a `container-type:size` container. The pitch fills it with **`height:100cqh`** (the team lists scroll BELOW it inside the tab); tokens size off `cqh` (`--gd-line-h = 50cqh / --pitch-rows`). A literal `flex:1` on the pitch can't be used — `.gd-tl-grid` is its flex sibling and would collapse it — so the bounded flex region is `.gd-tabwrap`. Stats/Ratings tabs are untouched (`:has`-scoped). The token strip scales proportionally (no fixed reserve) so the fit always wins even at the legibility floor — confirmed by the guard at the tight 667 viewport.

- **Helper `pitchRows.ts` is now BAND-aware, not axis-aware** (`pitchRows.test.ts`): ≤4 → one line (a flat back-4 / mid-4 SHRINKS, never wraps to 2+2 — locked real-app rule); **DEF 5 → `[3,2]`** (back three + wing-back pair); **MID/FWD 5 → `[2,3]`** (holding pair + front trio — the 4-2-3-1 / 4-5-1 case); ≥6 → balanced (anomaly safety net). Deeper line first. The `narrow` axis param is GONE (flat-4 reads the same on both axes, only the SIZE differs) — which deleted the wide/narrow dual-render.
- **Render + sizing.** `PitchHalf` renders one `LaneColumn` per band (no dual-render). `Pitch` computes `pitchMetrics` and sets `--pitch-rows` (busier side's formation-line count) + `--pitch-cols` (widest line) on `.gd-pitch`; mobile CSS derives `--shirt = clamp(11px, min(0.42 × cqh-line-height, width-budget), 42px)` and scales every token element off it. `.gd-pline` is `flex-wrap:nowrap` (a flat 4 shrinks, never wraps).
- **Token corners (uniform):** rating LEFT shoulder, subbed-off `▾min′` / red marker RIGHT shoulder (opposite, never overlap — each overhangs its own side, no vertical overhang so neither reaches the adjacent line), ownership bottom-left. Gotcha fixed: `.gd-rev.is-off`'s fixed-9px `font:` shorthand is re-overridden on mobile so the minute scales with the jersey.
- **Desktop unchanged** structurally (horizontal pitch, vertical lanes, 516px); the only change is DEF 5 → `[3,2]` from the new helper.
- **Layout guard extended** (`apps/web/scripts/verify-pitch-layout.mjs`, `pnpm test:layout`, opt-in / not in `pnpm test`, SKIPs without Chromium): renders a faithful replica of the **full screen chain** (shell content area → `.gd-app` → fixed scoreboard/stake/tabs → flex `.gd-tabwrap` → `.gd-lineups`, with a tall list stand-in so the tab scrolls) with the real `ds.css`+`shell.css`+`games.css` in headless Chromium, so the pitch flex-fills the real leftover. For 6 formations at 360 AND 390 across a **roomy (844) and a tight (667) viewport height** (+1280 shown) it asserts (a) full pitch fits / no scroll **at both heights**, (b) zero token overlap incl rating-vs-sub-badge on the same token, (c) zero horizontal clip, (d) correct per-band line structure — 30 render checks. Saves `/tmp/pitch_{4-2-3-1,4-4-2,3-4-3}_360x{667,844}.png` for the pre-merge mockup review (rendered screenshots are now part of the pause).

**Scope:** presentation/layout/CSS + the pure grouping helper only. **NO loader/builder/data-contract/engine/RLS/migration/Realtime/write change** (`buildGameDetail.ts`/`types.ts`/`loadGameDetail.ts` byte-untouched; `side.starters === side.pitch` holds; the reconciled kickoff XI from 8385c93 is unchanged). Full DoD gate green (typecheck / lint / format / **2557 passed | 24 skipped** / `@app/web` build) + the layout guard's 18 render checks. Merged `33e71e5`. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §25 (Mobile pitch = formation grid).

### 2026-06-22 — Pitch lane-wrapping + subbed-off ↓ badge on the pitch token (feat/pitch-lane-wrapping, **merge HELD** — presentation/layout on a live page)

**Problem.** With the kickoff XI now guaranteed to be 11 (reconciliation above), the remaining `/games/[matchId]` pitch defect was pure layout: each band rendered as ONE flex line inside the fixed-height pitch, so a deep band (5–6 mids/defenders — confirmed Uruguay–Cape Verde, both sides) overflowed `.gd-pitch` (`overflow:hidden`) and clipped off the bottom (desktop column) or the edge (mobile row).

**Fix — `PitchHalf` lays `side.pitch` into balanced formation lines.** New pure helper `apps/web/src/games/pitchRows.ts` (`pitchRows.test.ts`): 5 → `[2,3]`, 6 → `[3,3]`, 7 → `[3,4]`, 8 → `[4,4]`, returned BACK→FRONT with the fuller line biased toward midfield ("a line of 3 with a line of 2 behind"). The split is **orientation-aware for exactly one band, the flat back-4**: wide (desktop) axis keeps ≤4 on one line; narrow (mobile) axis keeps ≤3 on one line and wraps **4 → balanced `[2,2]`** (back 2 | front 2) so a back-4 never overflows a phone's width — the 2+2 is helper-driven, deterministic, not greedy CSS `flex-wrap`. Each lane `.gd-pcol` holds one `.gd-pline` per line (single-line bands are byte-visually identical). The orientation invariant — **`.gd-pcol` flex-direction MATCHES its parent `.gd-phalf`** (GK→FWD axis = band back→front axis) — makes the back→front order render correctly for home/away AND desktop/mobile with no per-side branching. Because the back-4 is the only axis-divergent band and SSR can't know the viewport, `PitchHalf` renders both groupings only when they differ (`.gd-pcol.is-wide-only`/`.is-narrow-only`, toggled by the same `max-width:720px` breakpoint that flips the half); all other bands render once. Desktop keeps the fixed 516px (two sub-columns of ≤4 always fit the height; a wrapped lane gets `.gd-pcol.is-wide` for depth); mobile `.gd-pitch`/`-grid` height becomes a `min-height` floor and halves/lanes size to content (`flex-shrink:0; flex-basis:auto`) so a wrapped band grows the pitch downward instead of clipping. `.gd-pline`'s `flex-wrap` survives only as a last-resort clamp for an unusually wide sub-line.

**Subbed-off ↓ badge on the pitch token (Step 2B).** Pre-fix the `▾min′`/red glyphs were list-only (`RowGlyphs`); the pitch `KitToken` showed none. It now renders a `.gd-tok-status` pill on the jersey hem (reusing `.gd-rev is-off`/`is-red`) for a subbed-off (`wentOffMinute`) or red-carded (`redCard`) starter — `▾{minute}′` visible at any token size, replacement name (`subbedOffForName`) in the `title` + the Starting XI list below. Presentation-only: all fields were already on `PlayerLine` from the reconciliation (the lone VM gap is the red-card MINUTE — only the boolean is carried, so the red badge shows the marker, no minute).

**Scope:** presentation/layout only — `GameDetailClient.tsx` (pitch render + token) + `games.css` + the new pure helper/test. **NO loader/builder/data-contract/engine/RLS/migration/Realtime/write change** (`buildGameDetail.ts`/`types.ts`/`loadGameDetail.ts` byte-untouched; the helper is presentation logic, not in the builder). Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/**2557 passed | 24 skipped**/`@app/web` build; `/games/[matchId]` = `ƒ` dynamic). **Merge HELD** for Chat clearance — visual fidelity is the test; Sergio verifies on live Render (the Uruguay 5-deep band wrapping 3+2 with no clip on desktop + mobile, the back-4 wrapping to a balanced 2+2 on mobile, and the ↓ badge on a subbed-off pitch token). See ARCHITECTURE.md → §25 (Pitch lane-wrapping).

### 2026-06-24 — T13: lineup-wide flag-kit jerseys + kit-resolver neutralized to `apps/web/src/kit/` (feat/lineup-kit-jerseys, **merge HELD** — review-class)

The set-lineup pitch + bench now render **flag-kit jersey chips** in place of the position-color `PlayerAvatar` disc, shipping the long-deferred "lineup kit adoption" (DECISIONS Theme F). The chip is a shared **`.sl-kit`** silhouette (clip-path + the global `--kit-outline` over a `var(--surface-4)` base, geometry promoted from the `feat/t13-bench-kit-chips` `.sl-bench-kit` reference), used by BOTH starters (inside the existing `.sl-av-anchor`, so the real-XI medallion + availability glow are untouched) and bench rows. The per-nation kit gradient is applied **inline** per the roster `.rt-kit` convention — **never `background-size: cover`**. The flag badge (`FlagBadge`, now exported additively from `components/PlayerAvatar.tsx`) rides the jersey as a SIBLING of the clipped silhouette (a child would be cut by the clip-path). Lock-on-play visuals (`is-locked` / `sl-tok-played` + `ScorePill`) and every tap route are byte-unchanged. **Resolver neutralized (Decision A=B):** `JERSEY_BG_V2`/`KIT_FALLBACK`/`kitOf` were MOVED to a surface-agnostic `apps/web/src/kit/kitOf.ts`; `app/vsfield/kitOf.ts` is now a one-line `export *` shim, so vsfield + game-detail keep importing `vsfield/kitOf` byte-identically while lineup imports the neutral module directly (no screen owns the kit primitive). **There is NO name-keyed `JERSEY_BG` in apps/web** — prototype-only; the app keys by ISO-2 through `kitOf()`. **Presentation + module relocation only:** no loader (`country` = `team?.name`, already loaded), engine/scoring, data-contract, RLS, Realtime, or migration change; the `--kit-outline` 5-copy byte-identity invariant is untouched. Tests: jsdom render proof in `ForfeitConfirm.test.tsx` (kit chip + flag badge + medallion + lock state + tap route, on starters AND bench) + pure-Node `lineupKit.test.ts` source contract (no `background-size: cover`; neutral-module wiring); `vsFieldSkin.test.ts`'s kit source-read repointed to the neutral module. Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/test/`@app/web` build). **Merge HELD** for Chat review of the diff. See DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-24 (T13 lineup kit adoption + kit neutralization).

### 2026-06-26 — T16b: Game-detail **Events timeline** tab + scoreboard scorers row (feat/game-detail-events, **merge HELD** — review-class, pure-builder contract change)

The `/games/[matchId]` page gains the deferred-from-T16 **Events tab** (placed between Statistics and Ratings per the match-detail handoff tab strip `desktop.jsx` `MD_TABS`) and a **scoreboard scorers row** (`⚽ Surname 11'`, goals grouped by side, own goals tagged `(OG)`). Both are fed by a NEW ordered **`GameDetailView.events[]`** emitted by the pure `buildGameDetail` — previously the builder folded `event_match` into per-player facts only and emitted no ordered array (the T16 deferral).

**The pure-builder contract change.** `buildEvents` produces a chronological KO→FT timeline of goals (scorer + assist + a replayed running score), substitutions (on/off), cards (yellow + red — a 2nd-yellow renders red, addition #2), and the **synthesized KO/HT/FT markers** (KO at 0–0; HT at the `event.period` 1H→2H boundary carrying the 1H score; FT **only on a terminal match**, carrying the final running score — live-safe). Goals/cards/VAR are classified on the **shared `@app/recompute` predicates** — `classifyCard` (already shared, T-CARD1) plus the newly-exported `isGoalEvent` / `isOwnGoalEvent` / `overturnedGoals` (**export keyword only — scoring byte-identical**). So the timeline keys EXACTLY as scoring: an **own goal credits the opposing side**, a **VAR-disallowed goal** (its `goal/*` row is not rescinded — paired `varDecision/goalNotAwarded` void) is **excluded**, and every `varDecision` row is dropped (the VAR theme is closed — no VAR display). The running score replays by accumulating goals in order; a goal whose scorer can't be placed on a side is **counted, never silently credited**, and a **terminal computed-vs-stored mismatch** is surfaced as a soft `EventScoreAnomaly` (mirrors the T-RECON ≠11 `LineupAnomaly` — the loader `console.warn`s it; the timeline still renders the accumulated score, never a silent desync). Same-minute ordering is deterministic on `(period rank, effective minute, kind, content-tiebreak)`, independent of input order. Added time renders as `45+2'`.

**The data change is TWO additive selects, nothing more.** `loadGameDetail` adds `assist_player_id` (assist scorers, joined into the player-id union so their names resolve via the same `player.findMany`) and `period` (the HT/FT boundary + sort rank) to its EXISTING `event_match` read. **Both columns pre-exist → NO migration.** `GdEventInput` is reshaped to carry the raw `time_minute`/`added_time` (for the `45+2'` form + the engine's effective-minute reuse) — a structural SUPERSET of the engine's `EventRow`, so the shared predicates accept it directly.

**Fantasy-safety — the NARROW truth (NOT "event_match is display-only").** Unlike T17's `stat_team_match` (genuinely display-only), **`event_match` IS a scoring input** — the engine reads it independently (cards, goals-conceded, and `assist_player_id` in the `namedInAnyEvent` participant gate). T16b adds only the `assist_player_id` + `period` SELECTS to the **display** loader and folds them into the pure display builder: **NO engine read/logic/stored-data change, NO migration, NO RLS, NO Realtime, NO validator**; the `@app/recompute` touch is **export-only**. The `@app/web` build (the engine-import IO-free reconfirm) stays green.

Tests: `buildGameDetail.test.ts` gains a 10-case events suite (ordered KO→FT timeline + running score · own-goal opposing-side credit · penalty/regular inclusion · the `openPlay`→`isPenalty:false` "pen"-substring guard · VAR-overturn + plain-`varDecision` exclusion · assist resolution · deterministic same-minute order · regulation→ET→PEN knockout ordering · unresolved-scorer + terminal-desync anomalies · live-safe no-FT-mid-match + HT-only-in-2H · added-time label); `loadGameDetail.contract.test.ts` pins the `assistPlayerId: true` + `period: true` selects + the assist id-union; a new jsdom `GameDetailEvents.test.tsx` mounts the REAL `GameDetailClient` and proves the scoreboard scorers row (per-side surnames, a brace's joined minutes, an own goal's `(OG)` tag on the beneficiary side) + the Events tab (full-name timeline, assist line, PEN tag, sub ▲/▼, card icon, latest-first HT/FT running-score markers). **Adversarially reviewed by a 4-lens workflow** (scoring-parity · render-live · reuse-contract · test-coverage): the scoring-parity (export-only byte-identity + own-goal/VAR/penalty parity + the reference-identity of `overturnedGoals` across the `GdEventInput`/`EventRow` boundary), render-live, and reuse-contract lenses were **clean — no P0/P1**; the 3 P2s were all **test-coverage gaps** (ET/PEN ordering, penalty-negative, OG-in-scorers-row), now closed, plus cheap P3 robustness fixes (sub-detection aligned to ingestion's `includes("substitut")`, strictly-total `tieKey`, the empty-state gated to `length<=1`, the `eventScoreAnomaly` surfaced as a UI note, a content-derived timeline key). Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/**2594 passed | 24 skipped**/`@app/web` build; `/games/[matchId]` = `ƒ` dynamic). **Merge HELD** for Chat review. See ARCHITECTURE.md → §25 (events[] contract + the narrow `event_match` safety framing) + BACKLOG.md → T16b.

### 2026-06-26 — T18: Game-detail **Standings** tab (real FIFA group table) (feat/game-detail-standings, **merge HELD** — migration-class)

**This IS a migration** (new `group_standing` table + RLS), so Sergio is merge authority and applies the prod migration + runs the backfill himself. STEP-0 live smoke (read-only, no DB writes) confirmed the feed BEFORE building.

**Premise correction (verified from the repo).** The brief assumed group identity was absent. In fact `fifa_group`/`fifa_stage` + `fifa_team.group_id`/`fifa_match.group_id` ALREADY exist in the schema — but are **never populated** (the match mapper `map.ts` only reads `group.name` transiently to derive the period label; nothing upserts `fifa_group` or sets the FKs). So in prod `fifa_group` is empty and the `group_id` columns are NULL → a match's group is derived via the **standings rows' own `team_id`**, NEVER `fifa_match.group_id`.

**Feed (STEP 0).** New `@app/feed` `groupStandings()` — GET `/fifa/worldcup/v1/group_standings`, season-scoped (`seasons[]`, default 2026), **non-paginated** (a single `getPage`, never the cursor helper). Requires ALL-STAR-or-higher tier; the app's existing **GOAT key covers it** (same credential, no new vendor). `FIFAStanding` keys to a team via NESTED `team.id` (not a flat `team_id`) + nested `group{id,name}`; **NO `form` field**. Live smoke against the real key: **48 rows, 0 null/non-numeric cells, `group.name`="Group A"** (the full string — baked into the mapper).

**Schema (D1 self-contained).** New `model GroupStanding` → `group_standing` (PK `team_id`; denormalized `bdl_group_id`+`group_name`; `season` default 2026; the stat columns; FK `team_id`→`fifa_team` Cascade; `@@index([bdl_group_id])`). **NO** FK to `fifa_group`, **NO** column on `fifa_team`/`fifa_match`. Migration `20260626120000_group_standing` mirrors `match_lineup_entry` EXACTLY: DDL (from offline `prisma migrate diff`) + `authenticated`-role portability shim + `ENABLE RLS` + `group_standing_select_all` (`FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (true)`); NO realtime, NO anon. **Proven on a throwaway Postgres:** all migrations apply cleanly; 14 cols, RLS on, policy + FK + both indexes present, 0 write policies, not in any publication.

**Ingest (display-only / fantasy-safe).** `mapGroupStanding` (nested ids, `requireNumber`-validated → malformed row throws → `eachItem` skips) + `ingestGroupStandings(feed, store, season=2026)` (ONE feed call) + `IngestStore.upsertGroupStanding` (prisma + memory double) with a store-level FOREIGN-GUARD (`teamIdFor` null → skip + count) returning `boolean` for precise counts. **NO `touched` set, NO `markPlayersDirty`, NO recompute.** New `job:ingest-group-standings` worker CLI (mirrors `job:ingest-team-stats`) + a daily `wc-fantasy-group-standings` render.yaml cron (mirrors period-close; ⚠️ operator may suspend after the group stage). **Live end-to-end proof** (real feed → throwaway DB, one seeded team): `fetched:48 upserted:1 foreignSkipped:47`, the row landed correct.

**Read + builder.** Additive `groupStanding.findMany` in `loadGameDetail` (resolve the two in-match teams' group(s), then fetch a superset of those group rows — read-only, owner-bypass). Pure `buildGroupStandings(rows, homeTeamId, awayTeamId)` returns **null (tab HIDDEN, A1)** when a side is TBD/null, the two teams are in DIFFERENT groups (knockout), or no rows ingested; else sorts by the feed's authoritative `position`, flags `isQualifying` (top-2 cutline) + `inMatch`, with `groupName` + a static advancement note + tie-break footnote. Team name → `UNNAMED_OPPONENT` fallback (never a UUID).

**UI.** Standings tab LAST (`Lineups·Statistics·Events·Ratings·Standings`), gated on `view.standings` + double-guarded panel (degrades to Lineups). `<StandingsTab>` renders `# · Team · P · W · D · L · GD · GF · Pts` (`.gd-gr-*` ported from handoff `.md-gr-*`): top-2 green `.is-qual` badge, `.is-inmatch` accent row tint + dot, signed GD, `gf:ga` pair, footnote. **`Last`/form column omitted (D2)** — the feed has none. Position-3 gets no distinct style (only the note), faithful to the handoff.

**Fantasy-safety (R2, grep-proven).** ZERO references to `group_standing`/`GroupStanding` in `packages/scoring`+`packages/recompute`; `recomputeStanding` still sources ONLY `score_manager_period` (group_md); **no file under `packages/scoring`/`packages/recompute` changed**. A brand-new table with zero engine readers cannot be a scoring input (unlike T16b's `event_match`).

Tests: feed (`groupStandings` non-paginated + season), `map.test` (nested keys + soft-season + fail-loud), `ingest.test` (foreign-guard + idempotent + **no dirty-mark** + malformed-row isolation), `buildGameDetail.test` (null→hide · TBD/cross-group/empty A1 · sort-by-position · top-2 cutline · in-match flags · footnote · superset filter), `loadGameDetail.contract` (group-scoped read pin + never `match.groupId`), jsdom `GameDetailStandings.test` (REAL render: tab appears, sorted table, cutline + highlight classes, GD/GF, hidden when null), gated `groupStandingRls.integration` (real-Postgres role-switch: anon→0, authenticated→all, owner→all). `renderBlueprint.test` updated for the new cron. Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/**2613 passed | 27 skipped**/`@app/web` build; the gated RLS suite proven on a throwaway PG). **Operator go-live (Sergio):** `prisma migrate deploy` on `DIRECT_URL` (the `match_lineup_entry` path), then `pnpm --filter @app/worker job:ingest-group-standings` once. **Merge HELD** for Sergio (migration-class). See ARCHITECTURE.md → `group_standing` + DECISIONS.md → 2026-06-26 (T18) + BACKLOG.md → T18.

### 2026-06-26 — P2: FAAB/waiver phase keyed on `playoff_entry` existence, not `league.status` (fix/league-status-phase-contract, **merge HELD** — shared-contract, Sergio is merge authority)

**The contract violation.** Two FAAB/waiver READ sites derived their phase (roster cap 15→9, D4 participation, the R32 forfeit/trim hint) directly from the `league.status` field — `loadWaivers.ts` (`leagueStatus = league?.status; isPlayoffPhase = leagueStatus === "playoff"`) and `@app/faab/prisma` `loadReleaseContext` (`status = manager.league.status; isPlayoffPhase = status === "playoff"`) — violating the data-existence phase contract the dashboard/playoffs loaders honor (phase from `fifa_match.status`/`period.kind`/`playoff_entry`, never the status field).

**The fix (the equivalence-safe signal).** Both sites now read a new `loadPlayoffPhaseActive(db, leagueId)` = `playoffEntry.count({where:{leagueId}}) > 0` — the **atomic twin** of `league.status === "playoff"`: the group→playoff transition writes the status flip AND the `alive` playoff_entry rows in ONE `applyTransition` `$transaction` (`transitionStore.ts:88-125`), so the two never diverge in a reachable phase. The change is **behavior-identical on the live path** — it just reads past a field that can lag the data.

**Why NOT `selectTournamentPhase`.** The discovery pass proved that swapping in the dashboard's kickoff-based helper would REGRESS twice: in the **R32 pre-kickoff trim window** it returns `group` (no knockout kicked off → cap would re-open to 15, re-admitting eliminated managers during the exact window the trim must hold 9), and after the Final it returns `complete` (and since `league.status` stays `playoff` — no `playoff→complete` writer — the cap would re-open again). `playoff_entry` existence is the only signal that stays correct in both. So **two data-existence phase signals now coexist**: the dashboard DISPLAY phase (kickoff-based) and the FAAB roster-cap phase (entry-existence).

**Scope + the P3 follow-up.** Source-only — no schema/migration/RLS/DB-write; the only new read is `playoffEntry.count`. The shared `loadIsPlayoffParticipant` was refactored to take a `playoffPhaseActive` boolean; its two OUT-OF-SCOPE callers (`loadBidContext`/`loadGrantContext`) are fed a status-derived boolean so they stay **byte-identical** (their cap stays on `rosterCapForLeagueStatus`). Migrating the bid/grant/batch ENFORCEMENT cap off `league.status` + a `switch`/`never` exhaustiveness guard over `LeagueStatus` (the `complete` arm is dead; `group` is not — the transition gate is `WHERE status='group'`) is **CONTRACT-P3** (filed). `packages/scoring`+`packages/recompute` untouched/unread (grep-proven) — no scoring/standings change.

**TDD red→green.** `packages/faab/src/playoffPhase.test.ts` (unit: predicate + boolean participant gate + `rosterCapForPlayoffPhase` parity with `rosterCapForLeagueStatus`) + gated-PG `release.integration.test.ts` (new disagreement case: alive entries + `status='group'` ⇒ cap 9) + new gated-PG `loadWaivers.integration.test.ts` + `loadWaivers.contract.test.ts` (source-shape flip + `not.toContain` `league.status` gating). All RED-first for the right reasons (functions absent / `expected false to be true`), then GREEN. Full DoD gate green (typecheck/lint/format/**2622 passed | 30 skipped**/`@app/web` build; gated PG T2/T4 proven on a throwaway Postgres). **Merge HELD** — shared-contract on the live FAAB/waiver path; Sergio is merge authority. See DECISIONS.md → "FAAB/waiver phase derives from `playoff_entry` existence" + BACKLOG.md → CONTRACT-P2/P3.

### 2026-06-27 — P3: FAAB ENFORCEMENT cap + participant phase off `league.status` onto `playoff_entry` existence (fix/league-status-enforcement-cap, **merge HELD** — live spend path, Sergio is merge authority)

**What moved (Option B).** P2 moved the two READ sites; P3 moves the four ENFORCEMENT loads in `@app/faab/prisma` — `loadManagerBidContext` (bid submit/edit), `loadManagerFaContext` ($0 FA grant + commish roster), `loadBatchContext` (blind-bid resolver), `listOverCapPlayoffSurvivors` (`commish:trim --report`). Each computes `playoffPhaseActive = loadPlayoffPhaseActive(prisma, leagueId)` ONCE and feeds BOTH the cap (`rosterCapForPlayoffPhase`) AND the playoff-participant signal (the `=== "playoff"` reads P2 left tagged "P3 follow-up" at `prismaStore.ts:163/311/524`). Dead `league.status` selects dropped (existence guards kept on a minimal `select:{id:true}`). `rosterCapForLeagueStatus` is **DELETED** from `@app/shared` (+ its now-unused `LeagueStatus` import); `validate.ts`/`resolve.ts` cap-doc comments retargeted; `playoffPhase.test.ts` parity assertions (which named the deleted helper) reduced to the literal 9/15.

**Provable no-op on live spend.** `status='playoff' ⟺ ∃ playoff_entry` (atomic twin, one `applyTransition` `$transaction`); nothing writes `league.status='complete'`, and the `champion` flip is a `playoff_entry.status` update that deletes no rows — so in every reachable phase the data form equals the old status form. The one divergent arm (`complete` → old form would re-widen to 15) is unreachable today → robustness, not a behavior change. The cap is read pre-commit (a plain `playoffEntry.count` SELECT, no lock) and NEVER inside the `commitBatch`/`claimFreeAgent` `$transaction`, so claim→apply serialization is untouched. The plan pass's 5-agent adversarial workflow returned `refuted:false` on both the transaction-no-op and equivalence claims.

**No `never`-guard; one deliberate survivor.** A repo-wide census found NO exhaustive `switch` over `LeagueStatus` anywhere (the deleted helper was a ternary; every remaining `league.status` site is an `===`/`!==` equality), so the planned `switch`/`never` guard would be ceremony — the corrected final shape is "delete, don't guard". The worker batch CADENCE selector (`apps/worker/src/faab/prismaStore.ts:23`, `league: { status: { in: ["group","playoff"] } }`) still reads `league.status` BY DESIGN — a separate axis (which leagues to batch), NOT the cap, NOT a P3 gap. `packages/scoring`+`packages/recompute` untouched/unread.

**TDD red→green.** NEW `enforcementCap.contract.test.ts` (source-shape) RED 3/4. NEW gated-PG `enforcementCap.integration.test.ts` under its OWN `FAAB_CAP_PG_TEST_URL` var (distinct from release's `FAAB_PG_TEST_URL` so the two table-wiping suites never co-run) + the `SAFE` guard: for all four loads a DISAGREEMENT fixture (alive entry + `status='group'` ⇒ cap 9 / participant set `{MGR}` / survivor listed) RED-on-old (15 / null / []), plus CHAMPION (`status:'champion'` entry, `status='playoff'` ⇒ cap 9), GROUP BASELINE, and a SPEND e2e (full 9-man squad ⇒ no-drop bid `drop-required`, RED: legal at 15) — RED 5/12, then GREEN. Full DoD gate green: typecheck/lint/format/**2626 passed | 42 skipped**/`@app/web` build; gated PG enforcementCap 12 / release 6 / faabSettledRls 3. **Merge HELD** — live FAAB spend-enforcement path; Sergio is merge authority. See DECISIONS.md → P2/P3 block + BACKLOG.md → CONTRACT-P3.

### 2026-06-28 — FA pool: exclude eliminated-WC-team players, ADD-SIDE ONLY (feat/faab-exclude-eliminated, **merge HELD** — pure resolver + shared FA eligibility, Sergio is merge authority)

**Decision.** Reverses the Theme-D "natural filtering — eliminated-team players sit in the pool, worthless" note: a player whose WC team is eliminated is now **removed from the FAAB pool and cannot be ADDED**. Sourcing is a **manual commissioner flag** (additive `fifa_team.eliminated Boolean @default(false)`, set by raw SQL) — **no worker/derivation/standings logic**, and the `@app/ingest` team upsert writes `name` only so a daily roster sync never resets it (migration `20260628120000_fifa_team_eliminated`; additive, default back-fills existing rows, no data migration).

**One rule, five add sites, add-side only.** A SECOND, ORTHOGONAL pure predicate `isAddTeamEliminated(teamEliminated: boolean | null)` (co-located in `faEligibility.ts`, kept SEPARATE from the ownership `liveOwnedWhere` so neither absorbs the other; a no-team player ⇒ `null` ⇒ eligible) is applied by the IO adapter at: the pool list (`listFaIneligiblePlayerIds` unions in eliminated-team ids — removes even UNOWNED ones), the per-player re-check (`getFaTargetFacts` folds `!teamEliminated` into `faEligible` ⇒ existing `fa-not-eligible`, **no validator change** — D4), the **grant tx race belt** (`claimFreeAgent` → `FaConflict` beside the live-unowned re-check), the sealed bid (`validateBidSubmission` ⇒ new `add-team-eliminated`; edit re-validates the fixed add ⇒ also rejected, **cancel runs no validator** ⇒ always withdrawable), and the **batch resolver**. **DROPS + lineup untouched:** `release.ts` and `@app/lineup/validate.ts` are byte-identical (their inputs carry no team field — the gate is structurally unreachable), so dropping an eliminated-team player stays allowed and an already-fielded player keeps scoring.

**`resolve.ts` / purity diff (the sensitive change).** `resolveFaabBatch` (the pure batch resolver) gains ONE optional `BidInput.addTeamEliminated?: boolean` and a THIRD disjunct in its existing **pre-loop void split** (alongside kicked-off + non-participant): `kickedOff || nonParticipant || teamEliminated` ⇒ `voided_refunded`. **Same terminal semantics as a kicked-off add** — the bid never enters `competing`, so **no debit, no roster change, no waiver-order move** (so a bid placed while the team was alive, then eliminated before the batch clears, can never grant). Mirror of the existing void shape, NOT new machinery. `purity.test.ts` stays green (the predicate is pure; the `fifa_team` read lives only in the adapter). The resolver test matrix is EXTENDED (a new describe mirroring the step-1 kickoff-void + the non-participant backstop), not rewritten.

**D2 — commissioner override.** `claimFreeAgent` takes `allowEliminated` (default false); `commish:roster` passes `true`, bypassing the belt for a deliberate manual add exactly as it already neutralizes the window + live-unowned eligibility (mirrors `--allow-post-kickoff`). The live manager route never passes it.

**TDD red→green.** Resolver shown RED (3 eliminated cases `won`→fail) → GREEN after the pre-loop disjunct, all prior 22 resolver cases byte-identical. Pure: `faEligibility`/`validate`/`resolve` unit cases. Handler-level DB-free via the memory doubles (extended `MemoryFaGrantStore` to model elimination): `handleBid` (submit reject / edit reject / cancel unaffected), `handleFaGrant` (`fa-not-eligible`), `commish:roster` (`allowEliminated:true` ⇒ granted). NEW gated-PG `eliminatedTeam.integration.test.ts` under its OWN `FAAB_ELIM_PG_TEST_URL` var + `SAFE` guard (open-window fixtures so the belt — not the window guard — bites): pool exclusion (unowned eliminated removed, alive/no-team kept), `getFaTargetFacts` fold, `getPlayerFacts.addTeamEliminated`, grant belt → conflict, `allowEliminated` → granted, drop-of-eliminated still allowed — 6/6 GREEN on real PG; release 6 / enforcementCap 12 / faabSettledRls 3 unchanged (no regression from the shared `prismaStore`). Full DoD gate green: typecheck/lint/format/test (+`@app/web` build). **Merge HELD** — pure resolver + shared FA eligibility; Sergio is merge authority. See DECISIONS.md → "AMENDMENT — eliminated-team players are GATED out of adds" + ARCHITECTURE §3 "Eliminated-team add gate".

### 2026-06-28 — Playoff lineup formation bounds loosened 2/2/1 → 1/1/1 (feat/playoff-formation-loosen, **merge HELD** — shared-validator class, Sergio is merge authority)

**Decision.** Knockout (`period.kind === "knockout_round"`) lineups now accept ANY complete 6-outfield shape with ≥1 DEF / 1 MID / 1 FWD (GK stays exactly 1) — the emergent set widens from 3 shapes {2-2-2, 2-3-1, 3-2-1} to the **10 shapes** 1-1-4 / 1-2-3 / 1-3-2 / 1-4-1 / 2-1-3 / 2-2-2 / 2-3-1 / 3-1-2 / 3-2-1 / 4-1-1. Core change is a 3-line edit to `PLAYOFF_ROSTER.bounds` mins in `@app/shared`; the per-lane MAXes keep deriving in `playoffBounds()` (now `6 − (other two mins) = 4`, no hand-edit). Group mode byte-unchanged. **Old set ⊂ new set** ⇒ no already-saved playoff lineup is invalidated; lineups persist as `lineup_slot` rows (no stored formation), so nothing is re-validated against the looser bounds at write time — **no migration.**

**`playoffXIShapes()` exported = single source.** The (previously private) enumerator is now **exported** from `@app/lineup` and is the SINGLE source for BOTH the validator's legal universe (`canFieldPlayoffXI` + the formation-bound check) AND the Set-Lineup UI offer-set: `apps/web`'s `PLAYOFF_FORMATIONS` (`view.ts`) is now **DERIVED** from it (was a hardcoded 3-shape literal, correct only at 2/2/1), so picker and validator can never drift; default still `2-3-1`.

**Drift-guard made REAL (corrects the prior docs).** There is no `FORMATIONS_PO` constant, and a true set-equality drift-guard did NOT previously exist — the set was pinned only by hardcoded `it.each` acceptance literals over the private enumerator. New `packages/lineup/src/playoffShapes.test.ts` asserts `Set(playoffXIShapes keys) == the 10` (normalized, order-independent); `apps/web`'s `formation.test.ts` asserts `PLAYOFF_FORMATIONS keys == the derived set`.

**Fillability side-effect (D5 unchanged).** Looser bounds can only REDUCE release-/trim-unfillability (more squads can field a legal XI), never increase it. After loosening the ONLY unfillable cases are an empty lane or <6 outfield bodies (the lane max of 4 never binds), so the 6 flipped fillability assertions were re-fixtured to a still-unfillable empty-DEF end-state: `canFieldPlayoffXI.test.ts` (split the min-reject `it.each`), `release.test.ts` / `handleRelease.test.ts` / `trim.test.ts` / `ReleasePanel.test.tsx` (drop the only DEF). `validate.test.ts`: the old "rejects <2 DEF / <2 MID" became accepts (1-3-2 / 3-1-2), plus added 1-1-4 / 4-1-1 / 1-4-1 accepts and 0-DEF / 0-MID / 0-FWD / 6-0-0 / GK≠1 rejects.

**TDD red→green + sweep.** RED first: the new drift-guard (got 3, expected 10) + the lane-4 accepts (`1 DEF selected (need 2–3)`) failed on the current bounds while 45 others passed → GREEN after the mins edit. Importer sweep of `playoffXIShapes` / `canFieldPlayoffXI` / `squadCoversFormation` (apps + packages, excl. design/) confirmed **no consumer beyond the mapped sites changed behavior** (`ReleasePanel.tsx` / `release.ts` / `trim.ts` delegate, no source change). ### 2026-06-28 — Live group→playoff transition + FAAB budget-reset CORRECTION (fix/faab-budget-no-reset, MERGE ON GREEN — dormant one-shot)

The group→playoff transition was **executed live** today; a one-time-budget-rule violation it shipped was caught and corrected, plus two cron/ops findings. Summary:

- **Transition ran live** (`commish:transition --field 10 --apply`): `league.status` group→playoff, **10 alive `playoff_entry`** seeded from the final group standings, non-advancers' rosters released into the FAAB pool, and the rolling waiver order carried forward (eliminated removed, no re-seed). `--field 10` ⇒ `cutScheduleFor(10)` = {2,2,2,2,1} (10→8→6→4→2→champion).
- **MD3 needed a manual `frozen_at` stamp via SQL before the transition would run.** The hourly period-close had already advanced MD3 to `status=closed` (which also opened R32 — see the seam below), but the **6h result-freeze window had not yet elapsed**, so MD3's `frozen_at` was still NULL and the transition's standings-final precondition (D6 — refuses while any `group_md` period is unfrozen) **correctly refused**. Resolved by hand-stamping MD3 `frozen_at` via SQL once results were settled (rather than `--allow-incomplete-standings`). **Freeze ≠ status-close** — the two clocks are independent, exactly as documented.
- **The transition's budget-reset step fired and reset all 10 advancers to a fresh $100 — WRONG.** The $100 FAAB is a **one-time allowance for the ENTIRE tournament** (group + playoffs); it is **never** reset/replenished at the transition — group-stage spend carries into the playoffs. The shipped reset, and the spec that documented it ("equalize the qualifiers / no carryover / a fresh $100 for the playoff run"), were **both wrong**. **Real budgets were hand-reverted via SQL**: `faab_budget = 100 − Σ each manager's won-bid amounts`, scoped to each alive `playoff_entry`.
- **This branch (`fix/faab-budget-no-reset`) removes the reset for good.** _Code:_ dropped step 4 (the `faab_budget = 100` write) from the transition `$transaction` in `apps/worker/src/commish/transitionStore.ts` — atomicity unchanged, the removed step was standalone (no later step read it); removed the now-dead `budgetResetManagerIds`/`budgetResetTo` plan plumbing from `transition.ts` + the CLI dry-run line + the orchestrator log line (each had printed a now-false "reset N budgets to $100"). _Test:_ a dedicated **regression test** seeds advancers with distinct budgets (73/99/45/0) and asserts each is UNCHANGED through the transition — **RED-verified** (it fails the instant a reset is re-introduced into the store). _Docs:_ DECISIONS.md FAAB Budget + transaction-step list and ARCHITECTURE.md §20 + `manager.faab_budget` rewritten to the one-time-allowance rule. Full DoD gate green. **MERGE ON GREEN** authorized: the transition is a one-shot that has already fired this tournament and is idempotency-guarded (a re-run aborts at the conditional status claim), so it is effectively **dormant — zero live blast radius**.
- **`wc-fantasy-group-standings` cron was failing** (HTTP 401, then "DATABASE_URL not found"): its `BALLDONTLIE_API_KEY` and `DATABASE_URL` were declared `sync: false` in `render.yaml` but **never bound on the service** in the Render dashboard. Both are **now bound** (dashboard-only fix — `render.yaml` already declares them correctly; no IaC change needed). **Impact = the Groups tab only; no scoring effect** (the group-standings table is a display read-model, not a scoring input).
- **`wc-fantasy-period-close` confirmed healthy** — the MD3-not-frozen state above was the 6h freeze window working as designed, **not** a dead cron.
- **KNOWN SEAM (documented, NOT fixed here):** period-close's "open the next pending period" step **opened R32 before the transition had seeded the playoff field**. The hourly period-close job and the manual one-shot transition are uncoordinated on this ordering. No harm this tournament (the transition tolerated the already-open R32), but it is a **coordination seam for a future thread** — period-close should not open a knockout period ahead of the transition that seeds it.

See DECISIONS.md → FAAB Budget (2026-06-28 correction) + ARCHITECTURE.md §20.

### 2026-06-28 — Quiniela (`/pool`) knockout bracket gates on `playoff_entry` existence, not `selectTournamentPhase` (worktree-pool-playoff-bracket-gate, **merge HELD** — review-class phase gate on a live surface)

**Live bug, mid-tournament.** With the league in the playoff phase (`league.status='playoff'`, 10 `playoff_entry` rows, all five knockout periods seeded, every R32–Final `fifa_match` linked to its `knockout_round` period), the Quiniela **Picks** tab still showed **group matchday lists instead of the R32→Final bracket**, so managers could not pick the knockout games. **Root cause:** `selectPoolPicksView`'s bracket-vs-lists branch gated on the phase from `selectTournamentPhase`, which by design returns `group` (not `playoff`) while every knockout match is still `scheduled` — it flips only once a KO match is `in_progress`/`completed`. All R32 were `scheduled` (first KO that night), so the bracket never rendered — the documented R32-pre-kickoff blind spot (BACKLOG → CONTRACT-P2). **Fix (mirrors CONTRACT-P2/P3):** gate the bracket on the **data-existence playoff phase** — `playoffActive` = `loadPlayoffPhaseActive(prisma, leagueId)` (`playoffEntry.count > 0`), reused verbatim from `@app/faab/prisma` (the SAME helper the waivers loader already imports into apps/web). `loadPool` derives it in its existing `Promise.all` and threads an explicit `playoffActive: boolean` as the new 4th arg into the pure `selectPoolPicksView`; the gate becomes `playoffActive || phase === "complete"` — a strict **superset** of the old `phase === "playoff" || phase === "complete"` (a KO match underway ⇒ entries exist ⇒ `playoffActive`, so nothing regresses; the `complete` OR is a defensive carry-over). The web layer does **not** read `league.status` (worker concern); `selectTournamentPhase` stays in the loader to frame the page (`PoolView.phase`) but no longer gates the bracket. **View-selection ONLY:** per-fixture result/scoring derivation stays keyed on `period.kind`; the `readVisiblePicks` reveal gate, `group_md` Completed archive, leaderboard, `@app/pool` engine, and `selectTournamentPhase`'s dashboard semantics are byte-untouched. **No schema/migration/RLS/Realtime/scoring change** (`packages/scoring` + `packages/recompute` unread). **TDD red→green:** `poolView.test.ts` gains the gate case (RED-verified — populated R32→Final frame when `playoffActive` despite `phase="group"`; `playoffActive=false` regression-pin) + `poolContracts.test.ts` pins the loader's new read/thread/negative-`league.status` guard. Full DoD gate green: `pnpm -w typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm format:check && pnpm test && pnpm --filter @app/web build` — **2657 passed | 48 skipped**, `/pool` ƒ dynamic 6.78 kB. **Review-class → merge HELD** for Chat clearance. See DECISIONS.md → "Quiniela knockout bracket gates on playoff_entry existence" (2026-06-28) + ARCHITECTURE.md → §3 pool bracket-visibility gate.

### 2026-06-28 — Quiniela (`/pool`) knockout Picks: vertical round layout + TBD/non-pickable undecided matches + group phase hidden in playoff (worktree-feat+pool-knockout-vertical-tbd, **merge HELD** — review-class, live competitive pickability gate)

Follow-up to the bracket-gate fix above (the bracket now renders, but three problems remained). **(1) Vertical round-sequential layout:** the horizontal `.pl-bracket` column scroller (`.pl-bcol`, overflow-x) is replaced by a top-to-bottom stack of `.pl-round` sections (R32 → R16 → QF → SF → Final), each reusing the matchday section styling (`.pl-md-head` + responsive `.pl-md-list` grid) so it reads cleanly at 320–414px and collapses to one column. Friendly headers via a `roundTitle` map (`R32`→"Round of 32", …). Engine round set/order unchanged. **(2) TBD / non-pickable undecided matches:** the feed seeds undecided bracket slots as placeholder teams named `Team {balldontlie_team_id}` ("Team 273"…) with real `fifa_match` rows + pick buttons — so a manager could pick a match between unknown teams. A knockout match is now pickable ONLY when BOTH sides are resolved real teams; otherwise it renders TBD with NO pick buttons. **Detection is by team NAME, anchored `/^Team \d+$/`** — confirmed against the live DB that `fifa_team.country` AND `.abbreviation` are NULL for ALL 112 teams (64 placeholder / 48 real), so neither can be used. Pure predicates (`isPlaceholderTeamName`, `isTeamResolved` type-guard, `isKnockoutFixturePickable`) live in `poolView.ts`, keyed on the name already on each fixture, unit-tested directly; `TeamLabel` shows "TBD" for a null OR placeholder side (the raw `Team {id}` name never reaches the DOM). Live state: R32 = 16 resolved/pickable (first kickoff today); R16/QF/SF/Final = all placeholders → TBD. **(3) Group phase hidden in playoff (RENDER layer):** `PoolView` gains `playoffActive` (from `loadPool`); `PoolClient` renders the group matchday lists / Completed archive / unscheduled section only when `!playoffActive`, so the playoff Picks tab shows ONLY the bracket (also drops the unlinked 3rd-place match). `playoffActive=false` is byte-for-byte unchanged. **An adversarial multi-agent review caught a P1 in the first draft:** that draft stripped the buckets inside the pure `selectPoolPicksView`, which also starved the leaderboard drill-in modal (`selectManagerPicks → allFixtures(view.picks)`, which includes `completed`) — clicking a manager in playoff showed only knockout picks while the leaderboard still totaled full group history. Fixed by keeping the selector phase-agnostic (full buckets always) and hiding at the render layer; pinned by a `managerPicks.test.ts` seam test (RED against the strip). **OUT OF SCOPE (recorded):** the 3rd-place match (separate thread) is not sourced/linked/rendered; a server-side guard rejecting a pick on an undecided match is a **recommended follow-up** (a crafted POST can still store one) — this thread is view-selection + layout only. `@app/pool` engine / reveal gate / `selectTournamentPhase` / `period.kind` result derivation byte-untouched; **no schema/migration/RLS/Realtime/scoring change** (`packages/scoring` + `packages/recompute` unread). **TDD red→green:** `poolView.test.ts` adds scope-2 predicate + scope-3 PRESERVATION cases; `managerPicks.test.ts` adds the loader→modal seam test; new `PoolBracket.test.tsx` (jsdom RTL on the real `PoolClient`) pins the stacked rounds, a pickable R32 match, a TBD R16 match with no buttons, and no group sections rendering in playoff (data still in `view.picks`); `poolContracts.test.ts` stays green. Full DoD gate green: `pnpm -w typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm format:check && pnpm test && pnpm --filter @app/web build` — **2670 passed | 48 skipped**, `/pool` ƒ dynamic. **Review-class → merge HELD** for Chat clearance; live-deploy `/pool` screenshot owed. See DECISIONS.md → "Quiniela knockout Picks — vertical round layout …" (2026-06-28) + ARCHITECTURE.md → §3 pool Picks-tab layout.

### 2026-06-28 — Live FAAB incident: `/waivers` FA add 409s every playoff manager — UI roster-cap parity (feat/faab-fa-cap-parity, **merge HELD** — Chat reviews the diff + the playoff-phase RTL test)

**Live incident, WC2026 R32.** Every playoff manager got **HTTP 409 on `POST /api/faab/free-agent`** trying to add a free agent. **Root cause (diagnosed against the live DB, settled):** the group→playoff transition trims the roster cap to **9** (`rosterCapForPlayoffPhase`, keyed on `playoff_entry` existence) and the GRANT path enforces it correctly — but the **UI gate** in `FreeAgentPanel` still read a hardcoded `SQUAD_SIZE = 15`. A 9-man squad computed `squadFull = 9 >= 15 = false`, so the drop selector never rendered, the grant POSTed with `dropId: null`, and the engine correctly returned `drop-required` (409). (A 1-for-1 from a notional 15-man squad would instead 409 as `roster-illegal`.) Eligibility, window phase, and the eliminated gate were confirmed consistent between the FA list and the grant — out of scope. **The engine was right the whole time; only the UI lied about "full".**

**Fix — surgical UI/loader parity, engine byte-unchanged.** The server loader already derives the real cap (`WaiversView.rosterCap` via `rosterCapForPlayoffPhase`/`loadPlayoffPhaseActive`, already used for the over-cap release mount). This thread **threads that same `rosterCap` into `FreeAgentPanel` AND `BidComposer`** and keys their `squadFull` / "drop required" / add-enable gate on it instead of `SQUAD_SIZE`: a 9-man at-cap playoff squad now renders the drop selector and requires a 1-for-1; a below-cap squad reinforces an open slot with no drop. `BidComposer`'s drop field also became conditional on the threaded cap (it previously ALWAYS required a drop — the mirror over-constraint that would force a below-cap playoff squad into a needless 1-for-1), and `BidPayload.dropId` widened to `string | null` (the bid route already parses a null `playerDropId` via `nullableStr`; the engine's `checkDropAndRoster` already requires a drop only at `squadSize >= rosterCap`). Group squads are always exactly full, so **group behaviour is byte-identical**. **No new hardcoded 9 or 15** — the cap is read once on the server and threaded. **In scope: total-cap gate ONLY** — position-minimum (DEF/MID/FWD) roster-legality is untouched (a separate open playoff-composition decision). **Engine byte-unchanged (verified `git diff --stat`):** `resolve.ts`, `validate.ts`, `faEligibility.ts`, `packages/shared/src/constants.ts` all absent from the diff.

**Tests (load-bearing — the bug existed because the UI path was never tested against the playoff cap; the P48/P54 "a route test ≠ a working user path" lesson, now extended to the playoff cap).** `freeAgentGrant.test.tsx` gains a **PLAYOFF-phase fixture** (playoff_active, cap 9, a 9-man roster) that mounts the real `WaiversClient` and proves the drop selector renders on Add and a 1-for-1 swap POSTs `/api/faab/free-agent` with a **non-null** `playerDropId` and SUCCEEDS (no 409); a new `capParity.test.tsx` unit-tests `FreeAgentPanel` + `BidComposer` gating at cap 9 (full ⇒ drop required) vs cap 15 below-cap (no drop) vs cap 15 at-cap (group unchanged). `waivers.test.ts`'s source-contract smoke flips from asserting `SQUAD_SIZE` to asserting `roster.length >= rosterCap` + no hardcoded `SQUAD_SIZE` gate. Full DoD gate green: `pnpm db:generate && pnpm -w typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm format:check && pnpm test && pnpm --filter @app/web build` — **2678 passed | 48 skipped** (+8), `/waivers` ƒ dynamic 9.21 kB. Gated Postgres integration tests **not triggered** (no DB/RLS/scoring touched — pure UI prop threading). **Merge HELD** for Chat clearance (Chat reviews the diff + the playoff-phase RTL test). **Recommended commissioner unblock fallback** while held: `commish:roster --apply` a single manager's add+drop (template in the thread). See DECISIONS.md → "UI roster-cap gates must read the engine cap, never a hardcoded squad-size" (2026-06-28) + BACKLOG → FAAB-FA-P2 (the orthogonal FA-panel-won't-surface-for-later-KO-rounds finding).

### 2026-06-28 — FAAB-FA-P2 closeout: brain-doc reconciliation — `period.status` DOES transition (docs + memory only, self-merge on green)

**Not a bug — the premise was a STALE docstring.** The forward-looking FAAB-FA-P2 item (flagged by the `feat/faab-fa-cap-parity` thread above) worried the `/waivers` FA panel "won't surface for R16/QF/SF/Final once their batch clears." A read-only diagnosis against the lifecycle wiring settled it: the hourly **`wc-fantasy-period-close`** cron (`apps/worker/src/jobs/periodClose.ts`, via the pure `selectPeriodStatusTransitions`) is the **SOLE writer of `period.status='open'`** and **auto-opens each knockout round (R16→QF→SF→Final)** when the prior round's fixtures all reach `completed` — ~1 day before that round's FA window — so the `open` fast-path of `selectCurrentPeriod` selects the round regardless of `batchClearedAt`, exactly as for R32. **No production code change.** The premise came from a stale comment at `periodOrder.ts:67-69` (mirrored at the DECISIONS Prompt-54 bullet + ARCHITECTURE §15) that predated the 2026-06-17 `feat/period-status-lifecycle` merge and wrongly said "status never transitions / the open arm is dead."

**This thread = docs + memory only (self-merge on green; engine / shared-logic / migration / RLS untouched).** (1) Reconciled the contradiction: corrected the three SIDE-B sites — `periodOrder.ts:67-69` (comment-only, **no logic change**), the DECISIONS Prompt-54 bullet, and ARCHITECTURE §15 — to state the cron is the sole `status='open'` writer and opens each knockout round on prior-round completion, each with a "do not re-derive" note. SIDE A (the 2026-06-17 lifecycle entry, the DECISIONS AMENDMENT, ARCHITECTURE §22) was already correct and left as-is. (2) Closed BACKLOG FAAB-FA-P2 as `RESOLVED` (not-a-bug). (3) Added `docs/RUNBOOK.md` → "Knockout transitions — pre-flight" (verification SQL + cron-log check + fallback ladder). (4) Recorded the durable insight to memory. **Residual (real, narrow): the period-close cron is a single point of failure** — it stamps `frozen_at` AND advances `status`, so a stall (this week's MD3 freeze miss) or a postponed/abandoned prior-round fixture (anomaly blocks the close → the next round never opens) leaves the FA panel hidden; the new runbook pre-flight is the guard. Full gate green (state count on merge); `git diff --stat` = docs + the one `periodOrder.ts` comment.

### 2026-06-28 — T-3RD: 3rd-place play-off surfaced in the Quiniela via IO-loader period-kind synthesis (worktree `t-3rd-pool-third-place`, **migration-class, MERGED `4aadc82`/`8d3c76c`** — Sergio is merge authority + applies the migration)

**What.** The "Match for 3rd place" (the lone `period_id IS NULL` fixture) now appears in `/pool` as a pickable 2-way knockout fixture, scored +1 cumulative like any knockout pick — while staying **period-less** so it remains invisible to lineups, player scoring, the guillotine ladder, and `/playoffs`. Option A (a real `knockout_round` period) was **rejected** in discovery: `loadPlayoffs` keys rounds on `period.kind`, so a 6th period pegs `playoffsView`'s `liveIdx` forever and the tournament never reports `complete` (champion never crowned). See DECISIONS → 2026-06-28 (T-3RD).

**How (one seam, pure engine untouched).** A new pure `resolvePoolPeriod(match)` helper (`apps/web/src/pool/`) maps `is_third_place` → a synthetic `{ "knockout_round", "3P" }` for the pool engine; every other fixture passes through. `loadPool` routes BOTH the fixtures projection and the separate `leaderboardMatches` projection through it (the leaderboard is a distinct raw read — without it the +1 never scores); the `selectTournamentPhase` input is left RAW on purpose. The write path (`prismaStore.getMatchFacts`) routes through the same helper so `validatePickSubmission` rejects a DRAW on the 2-way. `@app/pool` (`pool.ts`) and `poolView.ts` are **byte-untouched** — the defensive non-canonical-label branch already renders "3P" as its own bracket round after the Final, gated non-pickable until both teams resolve (~Jul 15). `ROUND_TITLES["3P"] = "3rd Place"` (cosmetic).

**Identity + migration.** Additive `fifa_match.is_third_place Boolean @default(false)` (migration `20260628130000_fifa_match_is_third_place`; NO RLS, NO backfill). `@app/ingest mapMatchRow` sets it on `/3rd place|third place/i`; `derivePeriodLabel` is guarded to return null for it **before** the `/final/` branch, and `ingestSchedule` defensively forces `period_id = null` when flagged. `"3P"` is pool-local — **NOT** added to `@app/shared KNOCKOUT_ROUNDS` (a guardrail test pins the ladder to the five rounds).

**Verification.** TDD RED→GREEN throughout. Full DoD gate green: `pnpm -r typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + `vitest run` (2697 passed / 48 gated-skipped, incl. new `resolvePoolPeriod` 5, ingest map/orchestration, poolView bracket+`+1` scoring, `handlePick` DRAW→409, `thirdPlace` write-path + guillotine-isolation invariants) + `@app/web build`. Gated-PG migration check on a throwaway Postgres: `migrate deploy` applies all migrations incl. `is_third_place` (column verified `boolean not null default false`); pool-RLS (3/3) + advance-store/guillotine (3/3) integration suites pass. NB the recompute `sweepClaimClear` TOCTOU integration test fails **identically on clean origin/main** in this local PG env (a pre-existing environment-specific timing flake in `packages/recompute`, which T-3RD does not touch). **Go-live (Sergio):** apply `prisma migrate deploy` on `DIRECT_URL` from a Render shell, then confirm the 3rd-place match shows in the `/pool` bracket (TBD until ~Jul 15) and a pick saves.
