### 2026-06-28 — SEC-P4: server-side guard rejects a Quiniela pick on an undecided knockout match (`feat/sec-p4-undecided-pick-guard`, review-class, MERGED `184391a` 2026-06-29)

**What.** Close the SEC-P4 write-path hole: `POST /api/pool/pick` now **rejects** a pick on a knockout fixture whose two sides aren't both resolved real teams (a `Team {id}` placeholder or a null team FK). The Picks UI already hid the buttons; this is the server backstop — defense-in-depth, UI-unreachable today, but it had to land before the knockout sides resolve (~Jul 15) or a crafted POST becomes craftable against real fixtures. No scoring/reveal/schema change.

**How (one shared predicate, no UI/server drift).** The pure name predicate `isPlaceholderTeamName` (regex `/^Team \d+$/`) was lifted from `poolView.ts` into `@app/pool` (+ `isTeamNameResolved`); the server validator and the UI gate (`isKnockoutFixturePickable`) now key on the SAME regex (`poolView` re-exports it for its callers). `validatePickSubmission` gains a knockout-only rejection — new typed `pickOnUndecidedMatch()` error → **HTTP 409** via the route's existing mapper, mirroring `drawNotAllowedKnockout`. Guard order `lock → undecided → DRAW` (undecided rejects ANY prediction). The server gets team identity from `getMatchFacts`, now selecting `homeTeam.name`/`awayTeam.name` (mirrors `loadPool`) onto `PoolMatchFacts`; the engine validator's input is a NEW `PoolPickFacts extends SubmittableMatch` sibling so `PoolMatch`/`LeaderboardMatch` (the scoring projection) stay byte-untouched. The 3rd-place play-off is covered for free — `getMatchFacts` already routes through `resolvePoolPeriod` (T-3RD synthesizes `knockout_round`), so it's rejected while TBD and accepted once its sides resolve.

**Deliberate boundary (review-recorded).** The guard keys on `periodKind === "knockout_round"`: group fixtures (always two teams) never trip it, and an unseeded fixture (`periodKind` null) no-ops it — consistent with the engine's permissive-when-unseeded stance; the residual (an unseeded placeholder fixture) is data-integrity-only and cannot affect scoring or the reveal. See DECISIONS → 2026-06-28 (SEC-P4).

**Verification.** TDD RED→GREEN (the RED run proved a crafted undecided pick was ACCEPTED on current code — 14 failures pinpointing the missing guard; GREEN after the one guard line). New tests: `pool.test.ts` (+9: undecided HOME/AWAY/null cases, undecided-beats-DRAW precedence, resolved-knockout regression-pin, group no-op, predicate tests), `handlePick.test.ts` (+4: placeholder/null 409s, resolved 200, undecided-then-resolved 3rd-place), `thirdPlace.test.ts` (+2), and a new `undecidedGuard.test.ts` (+8: a fixture-matrix proof that the server gate and `isKnockoutFixturePickable` agree). Full DoD gate green: `pnpm -r typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + `vitest run` (2720 passed / 48 gated-skipped, +23) + `@app/web build`. Adversarially reviewed (4-lens workflow → over-reject/drift/scope CLEAN; the lone substantive finding is the documented unseeded-period boundary). **Merge:** docs-adjacent code change, NO migration → Chat-cleared; MERGED to main `184391a` (2026-06-29, `--ff-only`) on a green gate.

### 2026-06-29 — P1a: dual-writer status-advance removes the status-open SPOF (`feat/tick-status-advance`, MERGED `4c53d0a`)

The resident 60 s worker tick now co-hosts period status-advance as a SECOND writer alongside the hourly `wc-fantasy-period-close` cron: `dispatchPeriodStatusAdvance` (`apps/worker/src/period/dispatch.ts` + `store.ts`/`prismaStore.ts`/`memoryStore.ts`, wired LAST in `scheduler.ts`'s tick) re-runs the UNCHANGED pure `selectPeriodStatusTransitions` and applies the SAME guarded `updateMany` (close `status != "closed"`, open `status = "pending"`), so a cron stall no longer silently drops a knockout round's `pending → open` FA-window mount. Idempotent (WHERE-guard makes the race-loser a clean no-op; `batch_cleared_at`-latch precedent); `periodClose.ts` + `periodStatus.ts` byte-UNCHANGED; freeze stays cron-only by design. ### 2026-06-29 — A-lite: cron-resilience DETECTION via external observational signals (`feat/period-close-heartbeat`, MERGED `438ae91`)

Two env-gated, fire-and-forget HTTP signals from `job:period-close` (`apps/worker/src/jobs/heartbeat.ts`): **liveness** = `ping(PERIOD_CLOSE_HEARTBEAT_URL)` after `job.periodClose.done` (+ `…/fail` on the crash path) → monitor alerts on ABSENCE (catches a stalled/crashed cron, complementing P1a's PREVENTION with VISIBILITY); **attention** = `ping(PERIOD_CLOSE_ATTENTION_URL, {anomalies, leagueId})` when a run's `anomalies > 0` → monitor alerts on PRESENCE (catches P1b: a HEALTHY cron blocked by a postponed/abandoned fixture, which liveness cannot see). PURELY observational — `ping`/`safePing` swallow every failure (throw / ~5 s timeout / DNS / non-2xx), an UNSET URL is a silent no-op, and `job.periodClose.done`/`.error` + the 0/1 exit codes are byte-UNCHANGED. No schema/RLS/web/migration; the DB-heartbeat column stays REJECTED (P2). ### 2026-06-30 — T2: Waiver Watchlist — private per-manager "star" on `/waivers` (`feat/waiver-watchlist`, migration-class, **MERGED `fb808bf`** — Sergio applied the migration + RLS)

A **private, per-manager bookmark** ("star a player") surfaced on `/waivers`. New `watchlist` table (`id`, `league_id`, `manager_id`, `player_id`, `created_at`; League/Manager/Player FKs all `onDelete: Cascade`; `@@unique([managerId, playerId])` = `watchlist_manager_player_uq`; indexes on each FK) with **owner-only RLS** — `ENABLE` (not FORCE) + **four `TO authenticated` owner-only policies** (`_select_own` / `_insert_own` / `_update_own` / `_delete_own`), each gating on `EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM manager m WHERE m.id = watchlist.manager_id AND m.user_id = (auth.uid())::text)`. Exactly **ONE SELECT policy, owner-only** — it mirrors `faab_bid`'s strictly-private family (NOT `pool_pick`'s league-visible reveal), so a star never leaks to rivals. The migration is **DDL-only** (the `prisma migrate diff` output verbatim — zero drift), copying `pool_pick`'s portability shims (the policies read the JWT) but with **no in-migration self-test, no `supabase_realtime` entry, no `SECURITY DEFINER` helper** (mirrors `group_standing` / `fix_faab_settled_rls`). **Write path** = `POST /api/manager/watchlist` (`{ playerId, watched }`; managerId resolved server-side, never trusted from the client) — a thin route over pure `handleToggleWatch`/`parseWatchlistBody` behind `getSessionManager()` (401/403 BEFORE any DB), `watched:true` → idempotent upsert / `watched:false` → delete (missing row still 200), written via the Prisma owner (RLS = defence-in-depth). The toggle is **scope-agnostic** (accepts any valid `playerId`; FA-only is a UX-surface choice) and **fully decoupled** — it touches no bid/batch/roster/lineup/budget row and no engine/recompute/dirty-mark/Realtime. **Read path** = `loadWaivers` adds one self-scoped `watchlist.findMany({ where: { managerId } })`, surfaced as `WaiversView.watchedPlayerIds: readonly string[]` (an id-set, NOT a per-`WvPlayer` boolean) + a pure `watchedFreeAgents(...)` helper. **UI:** a third sibling control in `.wv-comp-fa-wrap` on `FaPickRow` (same `stopPropagation` as the info button → a star tap never selects-for-acquisition) + a star in the `FaPlayerCardSheet` header + a "Watched" filter chip beside `<NationFilter>`; optimistic flip → `fetch → router.refresh()`, route-scoped CSS only (`ds.css` untouched). Tests (TDD-equivalent, all green): gated-PG `watchlistRls.integration.test.ts` (own `WATCHLIST_RLS_PG_TEST_URL` var + SAFE guard, uuid-casting `auth.uid()`, **11** — owner/league-mate privacy, cross-league 0, anon 0, owner-bypass, insert/delete, WITH-CHECK block, unique-key block, non-uuid-sub 22P02), pure `watchedFreeAgents` (5), route contract `handleWatchlist.test.ts`, decoupling source-contract `watchlistDecoupling.test.ts`, and `loadWaivers.contract.test.ts` (+1). Verified on a throwaway Postgres: full-chain `migrate deploy` clean, `migrate diff` from the migrated state empty, RLS proof under a uuid-returning `auth.uid()` (the SEC-P1/22P02 trap). Full DoD gate green: **2761 passed / 59 skipped** + web build + gated-PG RLS 11/11. **MERGED `fb808bf`** (Sergio, migration + RLS applied). See DECISIONS → 2026-06-30 (T2) + ARCHITECTURE → `watchlist` table + BACKLOG → T2.

### 2026-06-30 — Eliminated predicate is DATA-EXISTENCE, not `status="eliminated"` (`fix/eliminated-predicate-data-existence`, shared-contract, merge HELD)

**Bug.** Both §27 surfaces — the `/vsfield` live-field *hide* and the `/waivers` budgets-rail *strike* — keyed "eliminated manager" on `playoff_entry.status = "eliminated"`. But **group-phase non-advancers have NO `playoff_entry` row at all** (`status` is NULL, never the string `"eliminated"` — only guillotined-during-playoffs managers get that value; the transition writes `alive` rows for advancers and leaves non-advancers rowless, §20/§21). Confirmed against prod: the 10 playoff managers are `status='alive'`, the 2 non-advancers have no row — so neither surface ever caught the 2 who are actually out. **Fix (data-existence, the CONTRACT-P2/P3 contract applied correctly).** A manager is eliminated iff the playoff phase is active AND he does **not** hold an `alive` entry — catching BOTH the no-row non-advancers AND the `status="eliminated"` guillotines, leaving only `alive` survivors unstruck/unhidden. Both surfaces now call ONE shared helper, **`loadEliminatedManagerIds(db, leagueId)` in `@app/faab/prisma`** (the set-form of `loadIsPlayoffParticipant`, negated), which returns `managers − aliveSet` and is PHASE-GATED to an **empty set** when `loadPlayoffPhaseActive` is false — the field-blanking guard, since the group phase has zero `alive` rows and a naive "not alive" read would blank the whole live field. In each loader the helper call replaces the buggy `playoffEntry.findMany({ status:"eliminated" })` inside the existing `Promise.all` (destructured as `eliminatedManagerIds`), and the local `new Set(...)` line is deleted; `filterEliminatedFromField` (vsfield) and `eliminated: eliminatedManagerIds.has(m.id)` (waivers) are byte-unchanged. **Champion = alive-equivalent for display (follow-up commit, same held branch):** the survivor set is `status IN ('alive','champion')`, so the tournament winner is NOT struck (`/waivers`) or hidden (`/vsfield`) — `champion` is the terminal form of "survived." Display-only; deliberately does NOT touch `loadIsPlayoffParticipant` or the FAAB enforcement/roster-cap predicates (they stay `status==='alive'`, a separate axis, moot post-tournament). Also closes the sub-60s Final-advance-before-tick window that a strict `alive`-only set would have blanked (empty survivor set + `isLivePeriod` still true). Pinned by the flipped champion-not-struck test + a Final-advance-before-tick composition test. No migration / RLS / Realtime / engine change. **TDD red→green:** new `packages/faab/src/eliminatedManagerIds.test.ts` (a–d + champion + union, fake-db), extended `loadVsField.eliminated.test.ts` (helper→filter composition), the two source-contract smokes repointed onto the shared-helper delegation. Full DoD gate green: `pnpm -w typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2789 passed / 59 skipped** + `@app/web build`. **Shared-contract, live FAAB/waiver + vsfield read path — merge HELD for Chat clearance + Sergio merge; live-verify (strike + hide, visible only post-playoff-transition) is Sergio's on Render.** See ARCHITECTURE → §27 + DECISIONS → "FAAB/waiver phase derives from `playoff_entry` existence".

### 2026-06-30 — `/playoffs` Chocoyo hero re-skin + client-side blade choreography (`feat/playoffs-chocoyo-reskin`, review-class, **merge HELD**)

**Presentation-only** re-skin of the live `/playoffs` guillotine hero to the CHOCOYO "theater" design (`design/design_reference/screens_2026-06-14/theater/{parrot,screen,app}.jsx`). The tiny `.po-parrot` header glyph — which had **shipped invisible** (its source smoke passed, but the live render was a 24px chip, not the intended hero: the third source-smoke-passed-but-render-wrong bug of the session) — is replaced by the real Chocoyo hero: the parrot mascot peeking out of the pixel-art trophy mark (`/brand/trophy.png`, the same plain-`<img>` personality-moment pattern as the landing `.lp-cta-parrot`, **not** `next/image`) hoisting a **pure inline SVG MACHETE** (its belly is the functional `--elim` cutting edge; no raster, so the blade paints with zero asset dependency). Copy binds to the view-model — headline `LOWEST {round.cutCount} GET THE CHOP` → `CHOP!` on the drop; static "The Chocoyo doesn't miss." + "Chocoyo · your executioner"; substats `{fieldCount} still standing · {cutCount} get chopped · {survives} advance`; the "on the block" doomed = **`round.eliminatedIds`** (past = the actual cut, live = the provisional zone the view-model already surfaces), struck with the cross-surface row-elim treatment (`--text-tertiary` + `line-through` in `--elim`, now also added to `.po-row`/`.po-lr`/`.mpo-row`). **The blade choreography is a purely CLIENT-side state machine** (`PlayoffsClient.tsx`): the loader is CLOCKLESS (no "just happened" signal — a STOP seam held), so a transition latch over per-round-idx `status` fires a ONE-TIME wind → drop → settle **only** when a watched round flips `live→past` between refetches (`focusIdx` centres the CHOP on the just-cut round mid-swing, then settles onto the new live round); it never loops, never fires on mount for an already-past round, and is fully gated behind `prefers-reduced-motion`. The **complete/champion endgame is now owned by the hero** (celebratory trophy in-place, replacing the CHOP framing), retiring the standalone `ChampionBanner`. Board/ladder toggle + reduced-lineup + reinforce panels are visually aligned only. **INVARIANTS (git-verified byte-untouched):** `buildPlayoffsView` / `guillotine` / `playoffRound` / `loadPlayoffs` — no engine/loader/view-model change, no migration/RLS/Realtime; `/playoffs` stays `ƒ`. **RENDER PROOF (the whole point — a source smoke cannot prove a screen):** `apps/web/scripts/verify-playoffs-hero.mjs` (Playwright + inlined real ds.css/playoffs.css + served `/brand/*.png`) asserts by true paint geometry that the trophy image LOADED (not a broken box), the machete PAINTED, the blade rotates raised→down on the drop, and the swing/sway animations are wired (desktop + mobile, live + dropped; 8/8 checks + the four screenshots eyeballed in-session); `chocoyoHero.test.ts` pins the real component to the harness replica. Full DoD gate green: `pnpm -w typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2800 passed / 59 skipped** + `@app/web build` (`/playoffs` ƒ). **Review-class + high-visibility live screen mid-tournament → merge HELD for Chat clearance + Sergio merge; live-verify (hero paints + blade animates on a real cut) is Sergio's on the Render deploy.** See ARCHITECTURE → §21 (Chocoyo hero re-skin) + DECISIONS → 2026-06-30 (Chocoyo theater re-skin).

### 2026-07-01 — FAAB copy reconciliation: strip stale "reset to $100" strings (`fix/faab-copy-reset-strings`, contained, MERGED `4e98f1d`)

**Copy-only correction.** The FAAB budget rule was corrected on 2026-06-28 — the engine "reset to a fresh $100" step at the group→playoff transition was removed (`transitionStore.ts` `cca831c`) and DECISIONS updated (`7b12f18`) — but the **UI strings were missed**, so `/playoffs` (and several sibling surfaces) were still telling managers their FAAB "resets to $100" at the knockouts, the exact opposite of the live rule: FAAB is **one $100 budget for the ENTIRE tournament** (group + playoffs), NEVER reset or replenished — group-stage spend carries straight into the knockouts (each survivor enters with whatever balance they had left). Rewrote every **user-facing** occurrence to the carry-forward framing: `/playoffs` `ReinforceModule` (meter caption → "of your $100 tournament budget", the `po-reset-tag` pill → "Carries over · no reset", and the "clean $100 at the group→playoff transition" sentence → "one $100 for the whole tournament … it does not reset") + the `PLAYOFF_EXPLAINER` clause; plus the grep-surfaced siblings — `/waivers` `wv-resetbanner` ("FAAB reset. Budgets returned to a fresh $100…" → "FAAB carries over…"), the dashboard `PrimaryBanner` playoff sub + the dashboard `ReinforceModule` pill/note, and the marketing-landing bullet. The misnomer local `FAAB_RESET = 100` is renamed `FAAB_TOURNAMENT_BUDGET` (used only as the display-meter denominator; a comment cites the DECISIONS rule). It is deliberately **not** sourced from `LEAGUE_SEED_DEFAULTS.faabBudget` — that constant carries an explicit ⚠️ never-read-as-runtime warning, so wiring a display path to it would be wrong; no new hardcoded `100` was introduced elsewhere. **Copy/labels only — no engine/resolver/validator/schema/RLS/migration change** (the merged no-reset engine guard left untouched, `pnpm test` green). Test: a jsdom render test mounts the real `ReinforceModule` and pins (a) no affirmative-reset claim renders, (b) the carry-forward framing is present, (c) the meter denominator reads the whole-tournament budget; a source-contract check covers the module-private `PLAYOFF_EXPLAINER`. Full DoD gate green: `typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2809 passed / 59 skipped** + `@app/web build` (`/playoffs` ƒ). Contained + code-autonomous → merged `--ff-only` + pushed on the green gate (copy commit as the push tip so the Render web deploy fires); brain docs a `[skip render]` follow-up. **Left as-is (non-user-facing, out of scope, noted in BACKLOG):** the stale reset comments in `schema.prisma:173` and `packages/recompute/src/transition.ts:14`, and the CSS/gate identifiers `wv-resetbanner` / `db-rf-tag` / `isPlayoffPhase`. See BACKLOG → FAAB-COPY-P1 (DONE) + DECISIONS → §guillotine-FAAB (stale-surface note resolved).

### 2026-07-01 — Web Push: framing correction (docs-only; the feature is COMPLETE in code)

**A correction, not a build.** Two earlier planning passes mis-framed Web Push — first as "VAPID keys not configured → the feature is non-functional," then as "the whole notify IO shell is absent → build a 5-part epic (PUSH-1..5)." A hash-anchored read of **origin/main** shows both are wrong: Web Push is **fully built and merged**, shipped in two commits — `f054ded` (Prompt 41a: transport + the `push_subscription` / `notification_preference` / `notification_sent` tables + self-only RLS + `/api/notifications/{subscribe,unsubscribe,preferences,test}` + `apps/web/public/sw.js` + `apps/web/src/notifications/pushClient.ts` + the Settings toggles, with the sender inert) and `2ffb3a8` (Prompt 41b: the three worker triggers — `draft_turn` `${draftId}:${pickNo}`, `player_not_starting` `${matchId}:${playerId}`, `match_starting` `${matchId}` — each idempotent via the `notification_sent(manager_id, kind, subject_id)` UNIQUE ledger claimed inside `dispatchToManager`; sender now live). In-app live updates run on Supabase Realtime and are unrelated to push. **The ONLY remaining work is operator-only, no code:** generate a VAPID keypair and set `NEXT_PUBLIC_VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY` / `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` / `VAPID_SUBJECT` (all `sync:false` and empty on both services) on `wc-fantasy-web` + `wc-fantasy-worker` in Render, then rebuild web — tracked as **PUSH-KEYS**. No `PUSH-1..5` epic exists; the state is neither unbuilt nor non-functional but "complete in code, unkeyed." Docs-only sweep (no code touched); commit carries `[skip render]` (the docs tip must not deploy). See BACKLOG → Web Push notifications + DECISIONS → 2026-07-01 (Web Push status) + ARCHITECTURE §13 (Deploy state).

### 2026-07-01 — Commissioner console Thread 1: gated `/commish` shell + `commish_audit` ledger + read-only view-as inspector (`feat/commish-shell`, migration-class, **MERGED to main `e29e0eb`** — migration applied)

**The FIRST commissioner-only PAGE in the app + the cross-cutting audit table every later write slice writes into.** Thread 1 of the locked commissioner-console sequence (Threads 2–5 = Tier-1 writes → roster/lineup → freeze → field-lock). Ships exactly three things, all read-heavy; NO write action is wired.

**(1) The gated `/commish` route + console shell.** New lowercase-single-word route matching the nav convention (`/draft`,`/lineup`,`/pool`,…). The page is a thin gate — the pure `resolveCommishAccess` (`apps/web/src/commish/commishGate.ts`, mirrors `api/draft/timer`): logged-out → `redirect("/sign-in")`, any non-`ok` or a logged-in non-commissioner → `redirect("/auth/denied")`, a commissioner → the console. "Commissioner" is the shared `resolveCommissioner` predicate = the `is_commissioner` flag **OR** the `smrios07@gmail.com` email (from the validated `getUser` JWT) — literally the same gate the worker CLI uses, because the worker's `isCommissionerActor` now delegates to the same `@app/shared` function (single source of truth; the worker `core.test.ts` truth-table is byte-preserved). The UI is a faithful port of `design/design_reference/admin` into ONE responsive client component (`CommishConsole.tsx` + route-scoped `commish.css`): the slate "elevated privileges" ribbon (rides `--adm-edge = var(--locked)`; cobalt reserved for the tab selection + the view-as action; warning = `--ytp` orange; **never gold**), a 4-tab scaffold whose four task panels (Playoff field / Stat corrections / Game operations / Draft setup) render as **inert placeholders** ("coming in a later thread"), and a real System-status card (managers / periods / frozen periods / audit entries — cheap owner-bypass counts, no invented live-poller).

**(2) `commish_audit` — the audit table (the migration).** Additive `CommishAudit` model + `20260701120000_commish_audit` migration (DDL from `prisma migrate diff`, hand-appended RLS). Columns: id, league_id (FK→league Cascade), actor_user_id (FK→app_user SetNull, NULLABLE for future 'system' rows), action_type (TEXT — deliberately NOT a pg enum; the closed set is the app-level `CommishActionType` union in `@app/shared`, so a new slice's action needs no migration), summary, detail?, reason?, target_ref (JSONB?), delta?, reversible (bool default false), reversed_at?, reversed_by_user_id (FK→app_user SetNull?), created_at. Index `(league_id, created_at)` (plain btree; backward scan serves the desc log read). **RLS:** ENABLE (not FORCE), so the owner client bypasses; exactly ONE `FOR SELECT TO authenticated` policy, **commissioner-only** (the caller's own `manager` row — via `manager_select_own` — must share the row's league AND be `is_commissioner`; no SECURITY DEFINER helper needed since the table carries its own `league_id`); **ZERO** INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policies → client writes default-denied (server/owner-only via a shared `recordCommishAudit` helper, whose signature is shipped but has **NO callers** this thread — the table ships EMPTY); **not** published to Realtime. The read path is the owner-bypass `loadCommish`, so the SELECT policy is defense-in-depth on the Data API surface, not the app's read path. **`migrate deploy` proven on a throwaway Postgres**; a gated-PG role-switched RLS suite (`commishAuditRls.integration.test.ts`, own `COMMISH_AUDIT_PG_TEST_URL` + SAFE double-latch) is 9/9 green (anon 0 / non-commish 0 / cross-league commish 0 / commish sees league rows / owner reads+writes / authenticated INSERT rejected / non-uuid sub 22P02s) — and RED-verified (dropping the SELECT policy fails the commissioner-read tests).

**(3) View-as — a READ-ONLY manager inspector (NOT session impersonation).** HARD boundary: no token mint, no acting-identity swap, no mutation. A `ViewAsSwitcher` navigates via `<Link href="/commish?as=<managerId>">` (GET, server re-render); `loadCommish` builds the inspector from EXISTING reads — `loadStandings` (record + deterministic seed, pick the target's row), `manager.faabBudget`, and a contained `rosterPlayer.findMany` (owned players) — **same-league-only** (the `managers.find` guard runs before the roster read; an out-of-league `?as=` yields no inspector). The `ManagerView` shows seed / record / points / FAAB tiles + the owned-players list + the design's read-only assurance note; commissioner controls stay hidden.

**Nav.** `commish` added to the `NavId` union + an exhaustive-glyph key; a dedicated `COMMISH_NAV_ITEM` kept OUT of the always-rendered lists; AppShell/MoreSheet render it ONLY when a new optional `isCommissioner` prop is truthy. The hub page (which already has the `ok` outcome) + the `/commish` layout thread the flag, so a commissioner reaches the console from Home; surfacing it on every OTHER screen (without making the shared shell async or double-`getUser`) is a documented follow-up seam (DECISIONS).

**Invariants + gate.** No change to scoring/recompute/the resolver/`enforce_lineup_lock`/the FAAB engine/the transition-advance engines/existing RLS/any Realtime publication (git-verified additive). Full DoD gate green: `typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2831 passed / 68 skipped** + `@app/web build` (`/commish` `ƒ`) + the gated-PG RLS suite 9/9 + `migrate deploy` on a throwaway PG. **Adversarial 6-lane (opus) review of the diff — RLS leak, gate/email-fallback, view-as boundary, loader/audit, nav-leak, scope-purity — returned ZERO findings, all invariants held.** **Migration-class + a new privileged surface → MERGED:** Sergio applied `prisma migrate deploy` on `DIRECT_URL` from a Render shell, then merged (`e29e0eb`, verified ancestor of `origin/main`). See DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-01 (Commissioner console Thread 1) + ARCHITECTURE.md → §28 + BACKLOG.md → Commissioner console.

### 2026-07-01 — Commissioner console Thread 2: penalty entry + rating override — the FIRST `recordCommishAudit` callers (`feat/commish-tier1-writes`, source-only, **MERGED to main `9da186e`**)

**The first two commissioner WRITES.** Tier-1 of the console's Stat-corrections tab: enter a manual penalty (won/committed) and override a match rating, for a picked (match, player). Both feed the EXISTING, byte-untouched scoring plumbing — the adapter already maps `manual_stat_player_match.penalty_won/committed` (+2/−2) and the resolver already prefers `rating_player_match` source=`manual` over balldontlie — so this thread only WRITES the two feed-gap rows and fires a re-score. **No migration** (all three tables — `manual_stat_player_match`, `rating_player_match`, `commish_audit` — already exist); **stacks on Thread 1** (uses its `recordCommishAudit` seam + the shared commissioner gate).

**PART A — the two Thread-1 confirms, proven first.** (1) *Audit-read completeness* (gated-PG, `commishAuditRls.integration.test.ts`): a FOREIGN-actor row + a NULL-actor row in the commissioner's league are BOTH visible to the commissioner SELECT — the policy gates on "reader is a commissioner in this league," never "reader authored this row" (the converse is also asserted: the non-commissioner author of a row still reads zero). (2) *Worker-gate identity* (`apps/worker/src/commish/core.test.ts`): `isCommissionerActor` is IDENTICAL to the shared `resolveCommissioner` across the full (flag × email) matrix incl. the `smrios07` fallback + a non-commissioner — locking in the Thread-1 delegation as a pure pass-through. Both are GREEN (they characterize correct existing behavior).

**PART B1 — `POST /api/commish/penalty`.** Commissioner-gated (401 no-session / 403 non-commissioner, BEFORE any read). Validates: reason required; won/committed non-negative integers; the (match, player) exists AND the player's team is one of the match's two teams (`invalid_match_player` else — mirrors the adapter's `teamInMatch` participant gate). UPSERTs `manual_stat_player_match` by (match, player) — absolute + idempotent, "Clear" = 0/0 — with `reason` + `entered_by_user_id` + `dirty=true`, and records ONE `commish_audit` row (`penalty_applied`, `target_ref {matchId,playerId}`, reason, `reversible`, `delta` "+2/−2 pts") in the SAME transaction, then fires the re-score.

**PART B2 — `POST /api/commish/rating`.** Same gate/checks; `rating` 0–10. UPSERTs `rating_player_match` source=`manual` (`dirty=true`); "Clear override" DELETEs the manual row (the resolver falls back to balldontlie). Records ONE `commish_audit` row (`rating_override`, `target_ref`), then re-scores. The UI shows the current resolved rating + source before the override.

**The re-score trigger (importable, sync) + the FROZEN-override.** `recompute-one-(match,player)` (`recomputePlayerMatch`) IS safely importable by `apps/web` (via `@app/recompute` + `@app/recompute/prisma createPrismaStore` — already used by `loadPlayoffs`), so it is called SYNCHRONOUSLY after the committed write. Because the freeze gate lives at `recomputeManagerPeriod` (NOT `recomputePlayerMatch`), and the worker sweep runs WITHOUT `allowFrozen`, a correction on a FROZEN period would update `score_player_match` but leave the leaderboard stale — so the trigger restates the affected manager-periods + league standings with `allowFrozen:true` (the DECISIONS commissioner-override path). Surfaced via `frozenOverride` in the response + the audit `detail` + a panel banner. Belt-and-suspenders: `dirty=true` means the worker sweep re-scores within ≤60s regardless (its Phase-1 claim unions stat + rating + **manual**). Known property (documented, not a bug): a manual row for a player with NO feed participation yet is held (the adapter's participant gate needs a stat/event/shot) and folds in once the feed lands — the normal case (a penalty/rating belongs to a player who played) scores immediately.

**The UI.** The Stat-corrections tab (was an inert placeholder) becomes live: a match picker → a match-scoped player picker (URL-driven `?tab=stats&match=&player=` so it survives refresh) → the two forms (prefilled with current state, POST + `router.refresh`), a frozen-period banner, and a `// TODO(2b)` for the general stat-line editor. The other three tabs stay inert.

**`penalty_applied`, not `penalty_entry`.** The prompt's prose named the action string `penalty_entry`, but the closed `COMMISH_ACTION_TYPES` union pre-seeded **`penalty_applied`** for exactly this slice (the audit-log renderer's `ACTION_META` already has its label/tone, and the Thread-1 gated fixture already uses it). Using the pre-seeded value needs no union extension and keeps the renderer working — flagged for veto.

**Invariants + gate.** Engine byte-untouched — `git diff --stat main -- packages/scoring packages/recompute` is EMPTY (grep-verified). Full DoD gate green: `typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2873 passed / 72 skipped** + `@app/web build` (`/commish` `ƒ`, +2 API routes) + the gated-PG suites on a throwaway `postgres:16` — `commishAuditRls` **10/10** (incl. confirm-1) + the new `commishStatWrite` store suite **3/3** (atomic write+audit; idempotent penalty clear; rating set/clear). **Source-only → MERGED:** Sergio merged Thread 1 first with its migration, then this (`9da186e`, verified ancestor of `origin/main`; deploy fired, no `migrate deploy`). See DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-01 (Commissioner console Thread 2) + ARCHITECTURE.md → §28 + BACKLOG.md → Commissioner console.

### 2026-07-01 — Commissioner console Thread 2 follow-up: transient re-score throw → saved-but-restate-pending, not a bare 500 (`fix/commish-tier1-writes`, additive, **MERGED to main `b90b5a5`**)

**The closeout gap.** The write + `commish_audit` row commit in one `$transaction`, THEN the sync re-score fires. On a FROZEN correction that re-score runs the `allowFrozen` override rollup — and if it threw AFTER the commit, the throw propagated out of the handler and the route returned a **bare 500**, hiding the fact that the write + audit were already durable and inviting a confused re-entry.

**The fix (additive only).** `handleStatCorrection.ts` wraps the post-commit re-score in `fireRescore(...)`: a throw is caught and converted to a distinguishable **200** partial-success carrying `restatePending: true` + `warning: "restate_pending"` + a `message` (+ `scored:false`, `frozenOverride`) — mirroring the existing `no_match_participation` 200 shape, with `outcomeFields(...)` giving restate-pending precedence so the two warnings never co-appear. The payload conveys: write persisted, audit recorded, re-score failed, leaderboard not yet restated. **Remedy = re-submit the identical correction** — the writes are absolute + idempotent, so a repeat is safe, and for a frozen period it is the ONLY restate path (the worker sweep runs without `allowFrozen`). Stays DISTINCT from a pre-write validation reject (clean 4xx `{ error }`, nothing persisted). No engine/adapter/resolver/recompute/`commishStatStore` edit.

**Invariants + gate.** Additive (empty `git diff --stat main -- packages/scoring packages/recompute`). Full DoD gate green: `typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2878 passed / 74 skipped** + `@app/web build` + gated-PG `commishStatWrite` **5/5** — the added 5th assertion drives the real store on a frozen period with an injected throwing rescore → manual row + audit DURABLE, response `restatePending` (not a 500); two handler unit tests cover the penalty + rating throw paths + the reject-vs-restate distinction. **Additive follow-up → MERGED** (`b90b5a5`, verified ancestor of `origin/main`; stacked on Thread 2). See DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-01 (transient re-score throw → saved-but-restate-pending) + ARCHITECTURE.md → §28.2.

### 2026-07-01 — Commissioner console Thread 2b: general stat-line editor (`feat/commish-stat-editor`, source-only, **MERGED to main `db2a2bc`**)

`POST /api/commish/stat` lets the commissioner correct ANY of the **23 scoring-relevant** raw feed stats for one (match, player) via a sparse overlay in `manual_stat_player_match.extra.statOverrides`, re-scored through the byte-untouched engine. The adapter overlay (`adapter.ts`: `OVERRIDABLE_STAT_KEYS` + `parseStatOverrides` + `ManualRow.statOverrides`, merged `n(overlay[f] ?? feed[f])` inside `buildScoreInput`; plus the `prismaStore` parse) is the ONLY `packages/recompute` edit. Manual wins per field (nullish, so an explicit 0 wins), the feed passes through elsewhere, wholesale-replace rejected. **Gate isolation:** the overlay is carried on `ManualRow` and merged ONLY at the `ScoreInput` level — never into `b.stat` — so a manual-only correction on a feed non-participant stays `scored:false`/pending. Storage read-modify-writes a namespaced `statOverrides` sub-key preserving `rolePlayed` + the penalty columns; clear-all drops it (empty extra → SQL NULL). One `commish_audit` row per save (`stat_correction`, `delta`=field-change string); reuses Thread 2's audit + `allowFrozen` re-score + `restate_pending`. UI: `StatLineEditor` replaces the `// TODO(2b)` marker (feed value + override input per field, role-gated dimming, clear-all), fed by `loadCommish`'s extended `current` (`feedStats`/`statOverrides`/`hasStatRow`).

**Invariants + gate.** `packages/scoring` diff EMPTY; the only `packages/recompute` edit is the additive adapter overlay + `prismaStore` parse. Full DoD gate green: `typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2920 passed / 74 skipped** + `@app/web build` (`/api/commish/stat` in the manifest) + gated-PG `commishStatWrite` **8/8** (+3 2b: multi-field SET re-scores + idempotent, rolePlayed-survives-clear, gate-isolation on real Postgres); a structural allowlist-integrity test pins "23" against the engine. **Source-only → MERGED:** Sergio merged (`db2a2bc`, verified ancestor of `origin/main`; deploy fired, no `migrate deploy`). See design/COMMISH_2B_stat_editor.md + DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-01 (Thread 2b) + ARCHITECTURE.md → §28.3 + BACKLOG.md → Commissioner console.

### 2026-07-02 — Commissioner console Thread 3a: SAFE roster/lineup repairs — `@app/commish-core` + `POST /api/commish/{roster,lineup}` + the Roster & lineup tab (`feat/commish-roster-repair`, source-only, **MERGED `a33f8cb` + DEPLOYED 2026-07-02** — stacks on Threads 1+2+2b; 3b deferred)

**Part 2 of the cleared Thread-3 design** (design/COMMISH_3_roster_lineup_repair.md, 3a SAFE slice only; D1/D2/D3 stay 3b, deferred). Surfaces the on-main worker `commish:roster` / `commish:lineup` / `commish:trim` repairs into `/commish` — window/eligibility/drop-lock bypass adds, drops/trims of **UNLOCKED** players only, and past-window lineup edits via the in-memory `relaxPeriodLock` (`allowLockedSlot:false`). **GUC-free by construction:** the three dangerous flags are hardcoded `false` literals in `handleRosterRepair.ts` (never body fields — a smuggled flag is ignored, unit-proven), so the `app.commish_override` GUC is unreachable from any web path; grep-proof = the only `SET LOCAL app.commish_override` lines in source remain the two flag-gated primitives (`faab/prismaStore.ts:837`, `lineup/prismaStore.ts:94`).

**Commit spine (hash-anchored).** `193193a` = the cleared reuse seam: MECHANICAL relocation of the worker DI orchestrators (`core/roster/lineup/trim.ts` + their 3 suites) into new **`@app/commish-core`** — all seven files `R100` byte-identical renames; worker `cli.ts`/`advance.ts`/`transition.ts` re-point imports; CLI behavior byte-identical. `a979abe` = the RED B5 spine (31 handler tests + the 6-case gated-PG suite, failing on the missing modules). `7fc268e` = GREEN web layer: pure `handleCommishRosterRepair` (add / add-drop / trim on one route) + `handleCommishLineupRepair`, the `commishRepairStore` IO adapter (passes the REAL `@app/faab` / `@app/lineup` prisma stores through VERBATIM + CLI-parity `getAddMatch`), and the two thin routes. `9c7a36b` = the console's new **Roster & lineup** tab (URL-driven `?rmanager=`/`?rperiod=` like the stats tab): searchable live-unowned add picker + unlocked drop + optional period pin, trim checkboxes vs the phase cap, XI editor prefilled from the current starter set — every form dry-run-first (Preview = the runner's `planned`), reason-required, and with **NO dangerous-bypass controls rendered anywhere** (not even disabled).

**Audit (B4, cleared) + restate (A6/B3).** The reused primitives own their `$transaction`s, so the `roster_repair` / `lineup_repair` audit row (both pre-existing union strings — no union edit, no migration; trim REUSES `roster_repair` with `detail` marked `trim:true`) lands POST-MUTATION in its own tx; a failed audit write returns **200 `audit_pending` carrying the complete would-be payload** (action_type/target_ref/detail incl. bypass flags/delta/reversible) for manual recovery — never a bare 500, never a silent unlogged mutation (the UI renders the payload inline). Restate = `createCommishRestate`: `recomputeManagerPeriod(..., {allowFrozen:true})` + `markManagerPeriodProcessed` per period + `recomputeStanding` once — NOT Thread-2's `createCommishRescore` (that re-scores a (match,player); a repair changes slot MEMBERSHIP). Scope: lineup = exactly the edited period; roster/trim = the league's not-closed periods ∪ the pinned period (the B3 conservative named sub-decision). A restate throw → `restate_pending` (Thread-2 `fireRescore` pattern).

**Fail-closed lock boundary.** A locked-slot attempt on any 3a path refuses cleanly (409-class), writes NOTHING, sets no GUC, and the message names the deferred 3b/CLI path: `release-locked` (trim), `forfeit-requires-confirm`/`played-player-started` (lineup validator), the store latch `conflict` (lineup `saveLineup` re-check), and the kickoff guard's default-BLOCK (roster add). THE DECISIVE gated-PG proof (`commishRepair.integration.test.ts`, own `COMMISH_REPAIR_PG_TEST_URL` + SAFE guard): a trigger-armed canary (raw DELETE of a locked slot rejected) + a locked bystander slot immovable (same id/locked_at/is_starter) through a successful 3a lineup repair, negative guards with rollback-verified zero writes, roster-add idempotent re-run (ONE audit row), and the frozen-period restate landing in `score_manager_period`.

**Invariants + gate.** ZERO edits to `packages/faab` / `packages/lineup` / `packages/recompute` / `packages/scoring` (`git diff --stat main...HEAD` on all four = EMPTY — the relocation needed no import-path change inside them). Full DoD gate green: `pnpm -w typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2951 passed / 83 skipped** (RED commits in history) + `@app/web build` (`/api/commish/roster` + `/api/commish/lineup` in the manifest) + gated-PG on a throwaway `postgres:16`: `commishRepair` **6/6**, `commishStatWrite` **8/8**, `commishAuditRls` **10/10**, faab `release` **6/6**. **Source-only → Chat cleared, MERGED `--ff-only` to main `a33f8cb` + DEPLOYED 2026-07-02** (web+worker live, worker post-restart clean, no `migrate deploy`; worktree + branch torn down; 3b stays deferred). See design/COMMISH_3_roster_lineup_repair.md + DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-02 (Thread 3a) + ARCHITECTURE.md → §28.4 + BACKLOG.md → Commissioner console.

### 2026-07-02 — Commissioner console Thread 4: period freeze / unfreeze — `POST /api/commish/{freeze,unfreeze}` + the Game-operations tab (source-only; **MERGED + DEPLOYED** main `d367d41`, verified ancestor of `origin/main` — status reconciled by Thread 5; was `feat/commish-freeze` @ `9961c7f`, merge HELD)

Commissioner freeze/unfreeze of `period.frozen_at` — the AUTO-RESTATEMENT gate the worker sweep already honors (a frozen period's `manager_period` markers are skipped-and-left-pending unless `allowFrozen`). **Corrected semantics shipped, not the prototype's:** freeze = results final now (late corrections stop auto-restating); it does NOT lock lineups or pause scoring. Freeze guard = status-closed OR all-fixtures-FT (shared `periodFreezable` predicate drives both the server guard and the panel's button state); unfreeze requires a reason and surfaces `pendingDirty` + the re-freeze warning (the hourly periodClose cron re-stamps a settled period on its next pass — no manual-unfreeze exclusion exists, verified). Idempotency = typed 409 (`already_frozen`/`not_frozen`); the store's conditional `updateMany` + audit insert commit in ONE transaction, and a lost race (concurrent cron stamp) returns null → 409 with NO write and NO audit row. Audit: `period_freeze`/`period_unfreeze` (already in the union — zero edits outside apps/web), `targetRef {periodId}`, `reversible:true`. UI: the Game-operations tab goes live — period rows with frozen state/live pill/pending-held count, type-to-confirm FREEZE, plain-confirm unfreeze.

**Invariants + gate.** `packages/scoring` + `packages/recompute` + the periodClose cron BYTE-UNTOUCHED (empty `git diff` on packages/scoring, packages/recompute, packages/db, apps/worker — callers of the existing gate only; no schema/migration/RLS/Realtime). Full DoD gate green: `typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2974 passed / 88 skipped** (RED-first: 23 handler tests) + `@app/web build` + gated-PG `commishFreeze` **5/5** on a throwaway `postgres:16` (own `COMMISH_FREEZE_PG_TEST_URL` + SAFE guard): freeze blocks the plain sweep (marker unprocessed), unfreeze → next plain sweep restates it, `allowFrozen` override unaffected, audit-failure rolls the stamp back, lost-race writes nothing. **Source-only → merge HELD** for Chat clearance (writes `frozen_at` on live data; gates FINAL results). See DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-02 (Thread 4) + ARCHITECTURE.md → §28.5 + BACKLOG.md → Commissioner console.

### 2026-07-02 — Commissioner console Thread 5: playoff round-advance — `POST /api/commish/advance` + the Playoff-cuts tab (`feat/commish-advance`, source-only, **merge HELD** — surfaces the irreversible `playoff_entry` cut)

BACKLOG item 6 re-scoped per the 2026-07-02 discovery (accepted by Chat): the prototype's field-config panel is OBSOLETE (`commish:transition` ran — field locked at 10, `cut_count` seeded 2/2/2/2/1, presets retired for `cutScheduleFor`); Thread 5 surfaces `commish:advance` (dry-run render → type-to-confirm apply) + the cut ladder. **Mechanical relocation first** (own commit, the `193193a` precedent): `advance.ts` + `advanceStore.ts` moved to `@app/commish-core` as pure git renames (100% similarity, pre/post SHA-256 identical); the worker keeps the CLI + all advance tests with import re-points ONLY; the Prisma adapter + memory double sit behind the new `@app/commish-core/advanceStore` subpath export so the package's root export stays free of the `@app/db` runtime graph. **The write surface:** thin route → pure `handleAdvance` (the shared `gate()`, 401/403 before any read; `unknown_round`/`reason_required` 400s before any read) → the relocated `runRoundAdvance` VERBATIM (every guard — commissioner actor, seeded `cut_count`, ordering, frozen precondition, tie adjudication, idempotent conditional claim — lives in commish-core, nothing re-derived). Apply = the cut + exactly ONE `round_advance` audit row (`reversible:false`, names + counts in the summary, `targetRef {roundLabel, eliminated, champion}`) in ONE `$transaction` (`commishAdvanceStore.forAdvance` — reads delegate to the verbatim adapter; the write re-owns the transaction because Prisma interactive transactions don't nest, mirroring the Thread-4 freeze-store claims exactly). `allowIncomplete` is PINNED false — `parseAdvanceBody` never reads the flag (smuggled `allowIncomplete:true` test-pinned as ignored); the CLI's `--allow-incomplete` stays the only emergency path. Status→HTTP: planned/applied → 200; skipped/needs-commissioner/refused → typed 409 (never a silent 200 masking a refusal), every body carrying the discriminated `status` + plan when present. Dry-run reason: apply:false synthesizes the fixed `ADVANCE_PREVIEW_REASON` (nothing persists; the orchestrator's front-guard stays byte-verbatim); the REAL reason is required only on apply:true. **UI:** the Playoff-cuts tab replaces the inert "Playoff field" placeholder — SSR guillotine ladder (pure `buildAdvanceLadder`: enters → −cut → survives per round, actual eliminated counts for cut rounds, frozen/next-up chips, champion banner) + an SSR dry-run of the next uncut round through the SAME orchestrator+store (panel banner and server refusal cannot disagree); the sorted alive field renders leaderboard-style with the dashed cut line; a residual boundary tie gets a chip picker requiring exactly `cutsRemaining` picks, then a breakTie DRY-RUN so the SERVER names the full eliminated set before confirm; the type-to-confirm `CUT` copy names the eliminated managers + counts (+ champion) and states irreversibility.

**Invariants + gate.** `packages/scoring` + `packages/recompute` BYTE-UNTOUCHED (empty `git diff`); the `apps/worker` diff is import re-points only (zero logic); no schema/migration/RLS/Realtime (`round_advance` joins the free-TEXT `COMMISH_ACTION_TYPES` union). Full DoD gate green: `typecheck` + `lint` + `format:check` + **2999 passed / 94 skipped** (RED-first: 20 handler tests + 5 ladder tests) + `@app/web build` + gated-PG `commishAdvance` **6/6** on a throwaway `postgres:16` (own `COMMISH_ADVANCE_PG_TEST_URL` + SAFE guard): apply flips exactly `cut_count` entries + ONE audit row atomically, FK-failing audit rolls the flips back, second apply → 409 skipped with zero new audit rows, boundary tie → 409 needs-commissioner then breakTie end-to-end, Final → champion named in the audit, dry-run writes nothing; plus the relocated worker `advanceStore` PG suite 3/3. **Source-only → merge HELD** for Chat clearance (surfaces the irreversible `playoff_entry` cut on live data). See DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-02 (Thread 5) + ARCHITECTURE.md → §28.6 + BACKLOG.md → Commissioner console.

### 2026-07-03 — T-LAUNCH: public multi-league relaunch readiness audit — UCL retarget + App Store/Play Store distribution (`audit/launch-readiness`, docs-only, **MERGED `--ff-only`** main `9c71375`)

**Scope.** Read-only readiness diagnosis for the post-tournament relaunch: XI ships WC2026 as one private 12-manager league today; after 2026-07-19 it goes PUBLIC, MULTI-LEAGUE, retargeted to UEFA Champions League (36-club Swiss league phase, top-8 direct to R16, seeds 9–24 two-legged playoff round, two-legged knockouts to a single-match Final), distributed natively via Apple App Store + Google Play at a best-in-class bar. 20 read-only lanes (A1–A5, B1–B3, C1a–C3, D1–D3), each finding adversarially re-verified by an independent agent (284 verdicts; 5 candidate findings REFUTED and dropped in verification). 4 completeness critics ran a follow-up pass on every confirmed gap. Zero source changes.

**Method — the Sonnet-lane failure and recovery.** Broad-scope lanes running on Sonnet repeatedly died in a loop (huge read sweep → giant single-shot StructuredOutput → schema-validation fail → retry → "Prompt is too long" → respawn), requiring three manual interventions before the run was moved onto Opus for every mechanical lane, with narrower per-lane scope + hard output caps as the actual fix (model choice alone wasn't sufficient — see `[[no-sonnet-subagents]]` memory). This is now locked as standing process (DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-03, PROCESS decision).

**Verdicts.** All four launch gates come back **NOT READY**: public multi-league web (membership/session/commissioner/RLS-server-bypass all single-league-shaped), Google Play/TWA (store-side gaps are bounded S/M-effort but inherit the public-web NOT READY), Apple App Store (magic-link PKCE cannot complete inside WKWebView, Web Push is dead in a non-PWA wrapper, reviewer's first screen is an unpassable allowlist wall — the deepest gap), UCL retarget (schema models national teams in 12 WC groups with no club/competition-edition concept, exactly 5 hardcoded single-match knockout rounds, no aggregate/two-legged-tie concept anywhere, no team-at-time-of-match snapshot, WC-string-literal period derivation, feed client hardcoded to `/fifa/worldcup/v1`).

**Headline P0s (of 98 verified findings — 6 P0 / 35 P1 / 38 P2 / 19 P3, 34 launch-blockers):** `fifa_match→period` single global FK (F-C01/F-C12) — N leagues cannot bind shared fixtures to their own scoring windows; core schema models national teams not clubs with no Swiss-table path (F-D02); no aggregate/two-legged-tie concept in the period/lineup-slot/guillotine-cut models (F-D03/F-D04); mid-season transfer silently erases a player's pre-transfer stats via a current-team-only gate (F-D01/F-D07); `AllowlistEmail.claimedByUserId` globally unique — a user physically cannot join a second league (F-C03); no exception/error-tracking service anywhere, every failure is a console line a human has to tail (F-A01).

**Carries over clean (verified, load-bearing good news):** the scoring engine's stat math is feed-shape-neutral and portable; the guillotine ladder + all-play-all standings arithmetic operate purely on `score_manager_period`, format-agnostic; the RLS policy layer already rides multi-tenancy as-is (every tenant-data SELECT policy is already league-scoped); the notify "brain" (triggers/ledger/preference gate) is transport-agnostic and reusable under a native-push swap; worker tick supervision needs no rework for the format change itself.

**Inventories + decomposition.** 96-row multi-tenant assumption inventory (Lane C) + 57-row tournament-abstraction assumption inventory (Lane D), each graded structural/moderate/trivial with file:line evidence. 30-migration rollback-safety classification: 5 forward-only (all dated 2026-06-03..06-10) / 25 additive; the realistic rollback window (last ~3 weeks, everything from 2026-06-09 onward) is currently DB-rollback-tolerant, but the UCL retarget will introduce non-additive migrations where a code rollback across them would crash or silently corrupt against the advanced schema — expand/contract discipline + a written DB-rollback runbook is the fix theme, not yet an enforced gate. 11-thread build decomposition (§5, PROPOSAL ONLY — Chat sequences it) gated on a DEC-0 decisions/investigations thread (feed provider, membership model, commissioner model, wrapper tech, pool tie semantics), with HARD-1 (observability core) and STORE-1 (compliance artifacts) parallelizable and startable immediately.

**INV-1 (UCL feed coverage) resolved same day.** The audit itself flagged INV-1 as the mandated critical-path unknown (BALLDONTLIE had no announced UCL/Champions-League endpoint set comparable to `/fifa/worldcup/v1`). Resolved 2026-07-03: BALLDONTLIE ships a live UCL API (`api.balldontlie.io/ucl/v1`), covering teams/players/rosters/League-Phase standings/real-time matches with advanced data including player ratings from the 2025-26 season onward, webhooks under league code `ucl`. Two residuals carried forward: an operator/business decision (per-sport pricing — the WC tier does not carry over; a UCL tier or ALL-ACCESS at $499.99/mo is a separate purchase, Sergio decides) and a tech residual (the `/ucl/v1` vs WC feed schema diff — stat categories, incident types, period fields — folded into the UCL-1/UCL-3 tournament-abstraction threads, not a standalone thread).

### 2026-07-03 — T15-CUT: "The Cut" — the unified knockout surface on `/vsfield` + `/playoffs` demote-lite (`feat/the-cut-reskin`, review-class, **MERGED `--ff-only`**)

**What.** The knockout surfaces re-skinned to the locked `design/design_reference/the_cut_knockout` reference (Shape B hybrid): `/vsfield` IS the one knockout surface — relabeled **"The Cut"** during knockout rounds — keeping everything it had (live all-play-all field, T11 period strip, Season power record, H2H drill-in, score sheets) and gaining the guillotine framing: **KOMarquee** (trophy bursting out + looming machete + `LOWEST {n} GET THE CHOP`), **KOYouBand** (the #1 glanceable fact — safe/block/pend/out/champion, always color+icon+word, live margin-to-the-blade), **KOCutLine**, ON-THE-BLOCK zone rows, **KOFallen** ("one ladder, two sections" — cut managers struck below the blade, tappable via the T11 cut-round lookup `?period=<round>&manager=<id>`, auto-expanded when the viewer is fallen), **KOChampion** endgame with win-green CHAMPION/neutral RUNNER-UP medals, and the **KOCeremony** takeover (armed→wind→drop→aftermath on the live→past latch — the Chocoyo pattern; class-driven opacity, transform-only, reduced-motion → static aftermath; victims row flex-wraps). The phone drill-in is a bottom sheet pinned to the MANAGER, pushed on the EXISTING validated `?manager=` param via native history so the hardware back-gesture closes it; the ladder re-sorts at most once per 10s (FLIP transform moves) while the YOU-band margin updates instantly. `/playoffs` demoted to the ceremonial **Theater** (hero + blade choreography + champion endgame + OnTheBlock + the carry-over explainer; board/ladder/round-nav/rails/mobile-myband removed, −853 lines). ### 2026-07-03 — Thread 5 extension: `commish:advance` cut + release as ONE atomic action (`feat/commish-advance-release`, review-class/HIGH-RISK, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** main `c37001b`)

**What.** Removed the Thread-5 scope-out ("no roster/FAAB writes") **for the just-cut managers only**: the `commish:advance` apply is now ONE atomic action that BOTH flips the round's cut AND releases those managers' ENTIRE active rosters to the free-agent wire, on BOTH surfaces (CLI `commish:advance` + `POST /api/commish/advance`), inside the SAME `$transaction` as the cut. The champion + previously-eliminated managers are never released.

**Seam.** `releaseRoster` (`@app/faab/prisma`) gains an OPTIONAL `tx` param that ENLISTS in the caller's outer transaction (body factored to a shared `runReleaseInTx`) — the **optional-tx enlistment pattern**; the param rides the CONCRETE adapter only, off the pure `FaabReleaseStore` port (`purity.test.ts` bars `@app/db` there), so `commish:trim`/manager release stay byte-behavior-identical. New `releaseEliminatedRosters(tx, …)` reuses `runReleaseInTx` to drop each cut manager's whole roster + release slots, returning the released ids per manager. `applyRoundCut` (the relocated adapter + the web adapter that mirrors it) runs claim → champion → release → (web) the one `round_advance` audit insert atomically; the idempotency claim fronts the release (a re-run releases nothing). Locked slots release under the `app.commish_override` GUC scoped to `periodId: round.id` — deliberately clearing the eliminated managers' final-round lineup snapshot (Chat-cleared; frozen scores/standings unaffected — the preserve lever is `periodId: null`). Audit gains a `released` map + `releasedCount` in `target_ref` (JSONB, no migration) + the CLI `formatAudit` `released` field; the dry-run plan enumerates `releasePreview` via a new `loadActiveRosters` read, surfaced in the CLI report + the `/commish` Playoff-cuts tab + type-to-confirm copy.

**Gate + deploy.** Scope fences byte-untouched (empty diffs: `selectGuillotineCuts`, `resolveRoundCut`, faab `faEligibility`/`resolve`, scoring, recompute). Full DoD gate green: `-w typecheck` / lint / format:check / **3073 passed + 96 skipped** / `@app/web` build. Gated-PG (throwaway `postgres:16`, `migrate deploy`): **commishAdvance 7/7** (cut+release+ONE audit atomic; FK-failure rolls back cut AND release; 2nd apply → skipped, 0 new drops/audit; locked-slot GUC release + survivor untouched; champion + prev-eliminated unaffected; dry-run enumerates + writes nothing) · **worker advanceStore 4/4** · **faab release 6/6** (refactor regression). ### 2026-07-04 — Cut ceremony fires on FIRST OPEN per device — the shared `seenCeremony` latch (`feat/cut-ceremony-first-open`, review-class, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** main `463089a`, web green)

**What.** Both knockout ceremonies — the `/vsfield` "The Cut" `KOCeremony` takeover and the `/playoffs` "Theater" blade choreography — fired ONLY for a client actively watching a round flip `live→past` between refetches; a manager who opened AFTER the cut missed the reveal. Now the ceremony plays the FIRST time a device opens EITHER surface after an unseen cut — exactly once — then settles to aftermath on every later open.

**Seam.** New shared client utility `apps/web/src/playoffs/seenCeremony.ts` — a per-device localStorage seen-set keyed `xi:seenCut:<leagueId>:<roundLabel>` (`seenCutKey`), storage-safe `hasSeenCut`/`recordCutSeen` (SSR-safe; quota/blocked-store **fail-toward-seen** so a takeover never replay-spams), and the pure `decideCeremonyLatch` (fire the most-recent past cut IFF unseen; `record` = ALL past labels; `SEED_SILENT_DEFAULT=false` ⇒ fire-latest-once). Each surface KEEPS its existing between-refetch live→past latch and GAINS a CLIENT-ONLY mount-once effect that diffs the exposed past cut rounds against the seen-set and fires once (Theater: play the blade for the unseen round's idx; The Cut: `setCeremonyOpen`); the two are DISJOINT (on mount `prev=null` ⇒ live latch no-ops) and BOTH now `recordCutSeen` into the SAME key ⇒ cross-surface no-replay. Reduced-motion records-but-static; champion/complete is a persistent resting state, out of the latch. `leagueId` threaded to `PlayoffsClient` via a PARALLEL indexed read in `page.tsx` (the clockless `loadPlayoffs` stays the fenced STOP seam, byte-untouched).

**Gate + deploy.** Fences byte-untouched (loader/view-model/engine + `ds.css`/`knockout.css`/`playoffs.css`/`KnockoutUI.tsx`/`components.tsx`); `packages/` diff EMPTY; no schema/migration/RLS/Realtime. Full DoD gate green: `-w typecheck` / lint / format:check / **3094 passed / 96 skipped** / `@app/web` build; render proofs `verify-the-cut.mjs` **43/43** + `verify-playoffs-hero.mjs` all-green (both extended with a real-browser localStorage-gated latch proof — first-open-fires+writes / second-open-settles / cross-surface-no-replay / reduced-motion, desktop+mobile); `ceremonyLatch.dom.test.tsx` (7, REAL clients mounted) + `seenCeremony.test.ts` (14). Review-class → cleared by Chat + **MERGED `--ff-only`** (`e454c84..463089a`); `wc-fantasy-web` deployed green (asset-fingerprint cutover confirmed; `/vsfield` + `/playoffs` are web routes; worker untouched). See DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-04 + ARCHITECTURE §21 (Theater) / §28.6 + BACKLOG → Thread B.
### 2026-07-04 — FAAB waiver move-to-bottom fires on EVERY won claim (commissioner amendment to §D) (`feat/waiver-order-move-on-win`, resolver-class, **MERGED `--ff-only`** main `770a15a`, Chat-cleared 2026-07-04)

**What.** Commissioner amendment to DECISIONS §D: the waiver move-to-bottom rule is widened from "fires ONLY when the tiebreak is USED" to "fires on **every won blind-bid claim**." Every won claim — contested, uncontested, or a winning **$0 bid** — now moves the winner to the back of the rolling waiver order; the waiver tiebreak therefore now reads as **"longest since last win."** Applies to **future batches only** — no backfill / migration / retroactive reshuffle.

**How.** A single pure-resolver change (`packages/faab/src/resolve.ts`): the move-to-bottom trigger goes from `if (tiebreakUsed)` to `if (winnerIdx !== -1 && winnerIdx !== order.length - 1)` — i.e. fire whenever the winner is a **seeded** manager not already at the bottom. This keeps the three load-bearing invariants intact: (a) the **immediate within-batch drop** (the winner slides to the back mid-batch, so it can't keep winning from the front — the **generalized anti-sweep**, which now also means an amount-win costs a *later* tied player you'd have taken on priority); (b) the **two-phase, contiguity-preserving** (1..N) reassignment — the store (`prismaStore.commitBatch`, gated on `waiverOrderChanged`) is **byte-untouched**; (c) the emergent multi-/repeat-winner back-order — highest-$ win processed first lands furthest forward among the moved set, a repeat winner's *last* win re-sinks it, no special-casing. `tiebreakUsed` is still computed + stamped on the winning bid (now = "was this win decided by the order"); it just no longer gates the renumber. An **unseeded** winner (null position) is never inserted (`winnerIdx === -1` guards it) — seeding stays out of scope; a winner already at the bottom is a no-op, so `waiverOrderChanged` still means the order actually changed.

**Composition (verified, no change).** The $0 **FA grant** (`claimFreeAgent`) is bids-free and **never writes `waiverOrderPosition`** (grep-proven; the FA route `handleFaGrant.ts` never references the order) — deliberately distinct from a winning $0 *blind bid*, which does move. Playoff **carry-forward** (`carryForwardWaiverOrder`, `packages/recompute`) reads whatever contiguous order the move-on-win rule leaves, removes the eliminated, and re-packs survivors 1..K with no re-seed — unchanged.

### 2026-07-04 — Auto-fire the playoff round cut (`feat/autofire-round-cut`, review-class/HIGHEST-RISK, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** main `98bf720`, worker + web green)

**What.** The resident worker tick now AUTO-fires the combined cut+release for a knockout round shortly after it becomes score-complete — behind `AUTOFIRE_CUTS_ENABLED` (default OFF ⇒ byte-identical no-op), idempotent, running LAST in the tick (after status-advance) in its own try/catch. It INVOKES the untouched `runRoundAdvance` (SYSTEM actor + `apply:true`); it re-implements NO cut/release/resolution math. A boundary tie is NEVER auto-cut — it dispatches a ledgered commissioner alert and holds. New module `apps/worker/src/autofire/` (pure `selectAutoFireCut` + `selectRoundDataComplete`, store port + prisma/memory adapters, the IO dispatch, and `createPrismaAutoFireAdvanceStore`).

**FIX 1 — the fire gate is DATA-COMPLETENESS, not a clock.** A settle-after-kickoff gate is a near-no-op (no completed-at column; the round's period is already `closed`=all-completed when we look) and could fire before RATINGS land (the last-arriving input; the round score is the cut criterion). So the pure `selectRoundDataComplete` requires, per fixture: completed + ≥1 appeared player (zero ⇒ feed not ingested ⇒ hold) + every APPEARED player (reused `playerAppearedInMatch`, appearance NOT reinvented) rated (`rating_player_match.rating != null`) + no dirty stat/rating inputs, plus the period's `manager_period` `recompute_dirty` drained. Fail-safe HOLD on any doubt. **Never-rated-appeared-player rule:** holds for manual `commish:advance --allow-incomplete` indefinitely (per-tick `log.warn`, VISIBLE, no timer escalation) rather than firing incomplete. This justifies `allowIncomplete:true` — scores PRESENT (provisional), never ABSENT; the web route keeps it pinned false. `AUTOFIRE_SETTLE_MIN` (5) stays a harmless secondary floor.

**FIX 2 — durable null-actor `auto_advance` audit in the cut `$transaction`.** The apply writes a `commish_audit` row atomically with the cut+release (forced-failure rollback gated-PG-proven), replacing the stdout-only audit. `actor_user_id = NULL` (nullable system row); new free-TEXT `action_type` `"auto_advance"` (in `COMMISH_ACTION_TYPES` — NO migration) separates auto from operator `round_advance`; `target_ref` byte-identical to the web path. The worker store MIRRORS the web `commishAdvanceStore.forAdvance` (claim + champion + `releaseEliminatedRosters`) + a tx-bound audit via the shared `@app/commish-core/advanceAudit` builder (byte-identical to web `buildAdvanceAudit`+`recordCommishAudit`). Tie-hold uses `dispatchCommissionerAlert` (additive un-gated sibling of `dispatchToManager`, free-TEXT `cut_needs_review`).

**Follow-up debt (recorded in DECISIONS).** (a) 3-way cut-apply-tx mirror (commish-core adapter + web store + worker store), pinned by gated-PG, dedup candidate; (b) the shared advanceAudit builder duplicates the (untouched, fenced) web copies — web dedup is a follow-up.

**Gate + deploy.** Fences byte-untouched (`resolveRoundCut`/`selectGuillotineCuts`/`runRoundAdvance`/`advanceStore`/`releaseEliminatedRosters`/FAAB/web advance route/`recordCommishAudit`/sweep+recompute/freeze+status/`schema.prisma`); no schema/migration/RLS/Realtime. Full DoD gate green: `-w typecheck` (17/17) / lint / format:check / **3139 passed / 101 skipped** / `@app/web` build; gated-PG (docker `postgres:16`, `--no-file-parallelism`) **9/9** — advance 4 + autofire 5 (data-complete+determined⇒cut+release+durable auto_advance audit over an unfrozen-but-closed round; 2nd tick no-op no dup audit; data-incomplete⇒hold no cut no audit; tie⇒one `cut_needs_review` row no dup; forced audit failure rolls cut+release back). Review-class/HIGHEST-RISK → Chat-cleared + **MERGED `--ff-only`** (`aa2d17c..98bf720`, rebased onto current origin/main first); **both services deployed green** — `wc-fantasy-worker` (the substantive one — the autofire module + scheduler wiring + config; active deployment success) and `wc-fantasy-web` (rebuilt for the `@app/shared` `auto_advance` union + `@app/commish-core/advanceAudit` additions — harmless; success). **`AUTOFIRE_CUTS_ENABLED` is the operator on-switch** (Sergio flips it on in the Render dashboard when he wants unattended cuts; owes a live check). See DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-04 (Auto-fire the playoff round cut) + ARCHITECTURE.md §28.6 + the notify § + BACKLOG.md → Thread C + [[autofire-round-cut]].

**Tests.** `resolve.test.ts` (31/31): **inverts** the former "win-on-amount-does-NOT-move" asymmetry test → now DOES move; pins the multi-winner emergent order (`D,B,C,A` — non-winner keeps the front, winners ordered highest-$-first) + repeat-winner sink, the generalized anti-sweep, a winning $0 blind bid, the unseeded-winner guard, and 1..N contiguity. The existing tiebreak-consults-order + tiebreak-anti-sweep tests still pass unchanged. All other FAAB suites green (the store/memory-double semantics are unchanged).

**Gate + disposition.** Full DoD gate green: `-w typecheck` / lint / format:check / **3100 passed + 96 skipped** (the skips are gated-PG integration tests — unaffected: the change is IO-free and the store is untouched, so no DB/RLS/scoring surface is touched). **Resolver-class → Chat-cleared → rebased onto `origin/main` past the cut-ceremony merge, then merged `--ff-only` + pushed on a green gate.** Docs are a separate `[skip render]` commit. **Follow-up (live data, separate from the code rule):** the live waiver order was backfilled to reflect Batches 1–4 — the two previously NULL-seed managers (`yader` + `Los que todo`) seeded into reverse-draft order; the move-on-win rule then governs every FUTURE batch (the resolver itself never backfills). NB: the prompt referenced "BACKLOG task 7," but no such task existed (the two `7.` entries are unrelated — Quiniela bracket + the still-open Commish-core consolidation seam); recorded under BACKLOG → Recently shipped instead. See DECISIONS §D (move-on-win amendment) + BACKLOG → Recently shipped (2026-07-04) + [[faab-engine-layer]].

### 2026-07-04 — Commish-core consolidation: collapse the 3-way `applyRoundCut` mirror onto ONE canonical writer (`feat/commish-core-consolidation`, review-class/HIGH-RISK, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** main `99c653c`, worker + web green)

**What.** The three hand-mirrored copies of the irreversible cut+release `$transaction` (BACKLOG item 7 + the autofire thread's recorded follow-up debt) collapse onto ONE canonical writer — a pure STRUCTURAL refactor, ZERO behavior change on any surface (CLI, web, auto-fire).

**How.** `packages/commish-core/advanceStore.ts` extracts the module-private `runRoundCutInTx(tx, input, recordAudit?)` — the single `alive→eliminated` claim → champion flip → `releaseEliminatedRosters` → optional durable-audit body. `createPrismaPlayoffAdvanceStore().applyRoundCut(input, opts?)` self-opens its `$transaction` and takes an optional tx-bound `recordAudit` hook. Both mirror WRITE bodies are DELETED: the web `commishAdvanceStore.forAdvance` calls the canonical writer with a `round_advance` hook (real actor, still through the pinned `buildAudit`→`recordCommishAudit` seam, capturing `auditId()`); the THIRD mirror `autofire/advanceStore` (folded in beyond the original two-copy scope) calls it with an `auto_advance` hook (null actor) via the shared `recordAdvanceAuditTx`; the CLI passes no hook (stdout `formatAudit` only). `handleAdvance.buildAdvanceAudit` is deduped onto the shared `buildAdvanceAuditRow` — ONE builder now owns the round-cut audit shape for both durable callers.

**Review cleanup (pre-merge).** The first cut gave `applyRoundCut` an optional-`tx` enlistment param mirroring `releaseRoster`; Chat cleared the merge conditional on removing it as dead (no caller passed it — exactly `releaseRoster`'s own unused-tx state). Removed → `applyRoundCut` always self-opens; re-ran the full gate + all four pins GREEN with unmodified expectations, proving the param was genuinely unused. `runReleaseInTx`/`releaseRoster`'s own tx param (used by `releaseEliminatedRosters`) untouched.

**Gate + deploy.** Only 4 source files touched (canonical writer + 2 deleted mirror bodies + the dedup); ZERO test files changed. Fences byte-untouched (empty diffs): `advance.ts` orchestrator, `releaseRoster`/`runReleaseInTx`/`releaseEliminatedRosters` internals, FAAB engine, `resolveRoundCut`/`selectGuillotineCuts`/cut math, `advanceAudit.ts`, `recordCommishAudit.ts`. No schema/migration/RLS/Realtime, no new `action_type`. Full DoD gate green: `-w typecheck` (17/17) / lint / format:check / **3139 passed / 101 skipped** / `@app/web` build.
