### 2026-07-04 — Quiniela ≥24h Completed archive extends to the knockout rounds (`feat/pool-archive-knockout`, display-only, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** main `8bfd3ee`, web green)

**Why.** The group-stage archive (`ARCHIVE_AFTER_MS`, main `122b893`) partitioned `group_md` fixtures so a completed match ≥24h past kickoff left its matchday and sank to the Completed drawer; the knockout `bracket` rounds were explicitly left out as a "fixed frame." In the live R16 phase the 16 completed R32 matches (all >24h) stacked at the top of the vertical round layout and pushed the live/upcoming rounds down.

**What.** Two files, display-only. (1) Pure `selectPoolPicksView` (`poolView.ts`) applies the EXISTING `status === "completed" && now − kickoffAt ≥ ARCHIVE_AFTER_MS` cut to the `bracket` fixtures exactly as it does the `group_md` lists — an archived knockout match leaves its `.pl-round` and joins the SAME `completed` bucket (group + knockout intermixed, kickoff-desc); a round emptied by archiving is dropped (mirrors the emptied-matchday drop) while a never-seeded round stays an honest TBD skeleton (drop keys on `had-a-fixture && none-visible`). Non-canonical rounds (finished `3P`) sink too. Same `ARCHIVE_AFTER_MS`, same server `now` (no second constant, no second clock). (2) `PoolClient` re-shows the unified `completed` `<details>` drawer in the playoff phase (was `showGroup`-gated); the `group_md` matchday lists + `unscheduled` section stay playoff-hidden.

**Safety.** Only `completed`-status matches archive — a `scheduled`/`in_progress` knockout match NEVER leaves its round regardless of clock (archived ⟹ completed ⟹ locked, so no pickable match can vanish — pinned RED-against-old in `poolView.test.ts`). The selector only BUCKETS (`bracket`→`completed`), never drops: the drill-in (`selectManagerPicks` reads `...picks.completed`) and the leaderboard (built from full `leaderboardMatches`, independent of the partition) keep full group+knockout history.

**Gate + deploy.** BYTE-UNTOUCHED: `@app/pool` engine, `derivePoolResult`, `readVisiblePicks` reveal gate, `isKnockoutFixturePickable`/`isTeamResolved`/`isPlaceholderTeamName`, `selectTournamentPhase`, leaderboard, `packages/scoring`, `packages/recompute`. No schema/migration/RLS/Realtime/API change; `ds.css` + `pool.css` byte-identical (reuses the existing `.pl-completed` styling). Full DoD gate green: `-w typecheck` (17/17) / lint / format:check / **3149 passed / 101 skipped** / `@app/web` build. Contained/display-only → **MERGED `--ff-only`** (`dd30209..8bfd3ee`) + pushed + web deployed. See DECISIONS.md → Quiniela archive (knockout supersede) + ARCHITECTURE.md §3 (pool Picks-tab) + BACKLOG + [[t-3rd-pool-third-place]].

### 2026-07-04 — Auto-derive WC team elimination from frozen knockout results (`feat/auto-team-elimination`, worker-write / results-derivation class, **HELD for Chat clearance** — worker + gated-PG green)

**Why.** `fifa_team.eliminated` (the add-side FAAB gate — `feat/faab-exclude-eliminated`) was **manual by design**: the commissioner set it by raw SQL, with an explicit "no worker / derivation / standings logic" note. Chat reversed that decision: as the knockout rounds run, flagging every loser by hand is error-prone and easy to forget, and the gate silently under-restricts (eliminated-team players stay addable) until it's done. This thread auto-derives the flag from the results already in the DB.

**What.** A new worker module (`apps/worker/src/elimination/`) mirroring the period status-advance shape (`{selectEliminatedTeams,dispatch,store,prismaStore,memoryStore}.ts`). Pure `deriveKnockoutLoserTeamId` + `selectEliminatedTeamIds` name the **loser** of a knockout match as the **non-advancer** via FT → ET → pens — a ~6-line co-located mirror of `@app/pool`'s `knockoutAdvancer`/`derivePoolResult`, DUPLICATED (not imported) to avoid a worker→pool coupling; its unit test mirrors pool's advancer cases so drift is caught, and it returns **null (skip, never guess)** on any undecidable / not-completed / null-team-FK match. `dispatchTeamElimination` runs on the resident 60s tick (after settle/ingest, beside the status-advance — disjoint tables, so order is irrelevant): it reads the **freeze-gated** completed knockout results (`status='completed'` AND `period.kind='knockout_round'` AND `period.frozen_at IS NOT NULL`; the period-less 3rd-place match is excluded by the join) and flags the losers through a **guarded, set-only, GLOBAL** `UPDATE fifa_team SET eliminated=true WHERE id IN (…) AND eliminated=false`. Logs `team.elimination.flagged` ({ count, teamIds }) / `team.elimination.error`.

**Safety.** Set-only, **never auto-reverted** — a post-freeze "un-loss" stays a commissioner action (`commish:roster --allow-eliminated` / manual SQL). TICK-ONLY, no cron, no dual-writer: unlike the P1a status-open SPOF (a *closing* FA window), a missed flag is **self-healing** — the idempotent tick re-derives it and the guarded write no-ops once flagged, so there is no window to miss. Defense-in-depth: the pure fn ALSO gates on `status==='completed'`, so a live/provisional score can never fire the one-way write, on top of the freeze gate. Every `eliminated` READER (`listFaIneligiblePlayerIds`, `getFaTargetFacts`, `claimFreeAgent`, `validateBidSubmission`, `resolveFaabBatch`) is byte-untouched — this thread only WRITES the flag they read. No feature flag (contrast the default-OFF auto-fire cut): low-stakes (a mild FA restriction), guarded, idempotent, and a pure no-op through the entire group stage (no frozen knockout period exists yet).

**Gate + hold.** BYTE-UNTOUCHED: `@app/faab`, `resolve.ts`, `@app/scoring`, `@app/recompute`, `@app/lineup`, `release.ts`, the status-advance path, ingest. NO migration (`fifa_team.eliminated` already exists — `20260628120000_fifa_team_eliminated`); the schema `///` doc comment was updated to match the reversal. SCORING.md untouched (stated explicitly). TDD: 13 pure + 6 handler (memoryStore) tests, RED→GREEN; the gated-PG suite (`ELIM_DERIVE_PG_TEST_URL`, 3 cases — flag / freeze-gate + knockout-only + period-less, idempotent, gate-opens) was **run for real against docker `postgres:16` — 3/3 pass** (validates the nullable-relation `is:` read filter). Full DoD gate exit 0: `-w typecheck` / lint / format:check / `pnpm test` **3168 passed / 104 skipped** (was 3149/101 → +19 passing, +3 gated-skip). **HELD, not merged** — worker write to shared reference data + results-derivation class; awaits Sergio's Chat clearance. See DECISIONS.md → team-elimination auto-derivation (reverses "manual by design") + ARCHITECTURE.md §3 (Team-elimination auto-derivation) + BACKLOG + [[faab-fa-live-unowned]].

### 2026-07-04 — PLAYERS-1: the `/players` full-tournament browser (`feat/players-browser`, contained read-surface, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** — feature commit is the push tip; web green)

**Why.** There was no way to browse the *whole* tournament player pool — only the draft board (pre-tournament) and the waivers free-agent list (window-gated, FA-only). PLAYERS-1 builds `/players` to the committed design reference (`design/design_reference/players/`, landed at `c0c7fce`): every player, rostered or free, filterable, tap → the existing two-tab player card, and — for a claimable free agent — a bid affordance that hands off to the existing /waivers composer. READ-ONLY: zero new write paths.

**What.** A new authenticated route `/players` (`requireManager` gate, `force-dynamic`, AppShell-wrapped, route-scoped `players.css` on the canonical global ds tokens). `loadPlayers` (owner-bypass, READ-ONLY page-load snapshot) assembles one `PlayersView`: nation via the `fifa_team.name` join (NEVER `player.country` — not even selected), `nationAlive` = `!fifa_team.eliminated`, `seasonPoints` via the SAME `score_player_match` `groupBy` shape loadWaivers uses (single source), ownership from active `roster_player` (`dropped_at IS NULL`, league-scoped) with manager display names attached SERVER-SIDE (the loadGameDetail precedent — the browser never reads manager rows), and the FA-window phase from `acquisitionWindowState` (same source loadWaivers uses). Pure `playersLogic` (no-DB, unit-tested) owns filter (search · position segmented · availability All|FA|Rostered|Mine · NationFilter reuse · Active-teams toggle) + sort (season pts desc default, nulls-last, name-stable, toggles) + 25-row paged reveal (never the ~1,200-row wall) + the bid-trailer predicate. Row tap opens the SHARED `FaPlayerCardSheet` (see below); FA rows show a trailing `<a>` → `/waivers?bid=<id>`.

**G1 resolved (Sergio) — DROP the row statline.** Six of the reference's eight artboards center on a per-row stat table (`Pld·Min·G·A·Sh·KP·CS·Tkl·YC` + stat-header tooltips) fed by a `statsFor()` stub; the strict data model carries none of it, and CS/YC have no clean per-row source. Ruling: rows use the reference's own simpler `.pl-row` vocabulary (Pos·Kit·Player/nation·Owner·Pts·Action, frame 6); rich stats live ONLY in the card's Stats tab (the real `GET /api/player-tournament-stats`). The mobile pinned-column scroller, swipe-hint, stat-header tooltips, and the desktop 3-column card variant are dropped (design deviations, flagged).

**Shared card — cross-module reuse, byte-equivalence gated (R1).** `/players` consumes `FaPlayerCardSheet` from `src/waivers/` directly (no physical move — its `faPlayerCardContract.test.ts` fs-pins that path). The card gained two OPTIONAL props: `onToggleStar?` (the star button renders only when present — /players is read-only, no watchlist write) and `footNote` (default = the LITERAL current /waivers copy). /waivers passes both exactly as before; the full waivers suite (incl. `faPlayerCardContract` + the `faPlayerCard` end-to-end) stays green unchanged — the byte-equivalence proof.

**Acquisition single-sourced via deep-link (R2/R3 riders honored).** The bid trailer only NAVIGATES; the actual claim path, FAAB engine, and composer internals are untouched. `/waivers?bid=<id>` is read server-side and threaded to `WaiversClient`, which resolves it ONCE (pure `resolveBidDeepLink`, mount-only) and PRESELECTS via the existing `setSelected` path (sealed-bid → composer opens; free-agency → FA panel armed) iff the window is open AND the player is still claimable — else a silent no-op. `AppShell`'s `active` prop was widened `NavId → NavId | null` (type-only + the single guard at `AppShell.tsx:167`; every downstream consumer already accepted null) so `/players` (NOT a nav tab — T15-2 owns nav) highlights nothing. Entry points: a dashboard "Player pool" tile (static, group + playoff phases — `loadDashboard` untouched) + a "Browse all players" link on /waivers. No new bottom-nav tab.

**Gate + merge.** BYTE-UNTOUCHED: `packages/faab`, bid routes, `BidComposer`/`FreeAgentPanel` submit internals, scoring/recompute/ingest/lineup/auth; NO migration/schema/RLS/Realtime; `design/design_reference/` frozen. Mobile hard-rules held (≥44px targets, `dvh` via the shared sheet, the card scrim lifted to z120 ABOVE the z100 bottom bar, sticky ✕ + scroll containment). New tests: `playersLogic` (26 — every filter alone + AND + Mine + sort nulls-last/stable + page slicing + trailer predicate all four clauses), `loadPlayers.contract` (8 — no-country/team-name nation, `dropped_at:null`, server-side name attach, read-only, window-from-`acquisitionWindowState`, R3 same-groupBy-as-loadWaivers), `playersClient` RTL (15), `waiversDeepLink` (14 — pure matrix + preselect/no-op integration). `theCutSkin.test.ts` green (19). Full DoD gate green (`-w typecheck` / lint / format:check / **pnpm test** / `@app/web` build, `/players` = `ƒ`). Contained → **MERGED `--ff-only`** + pushed (feature commit the tip → web deploy fired; docs commit `[skip render]` beneath). See DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-04 (PLAYERS-1) + ARCHITECTURE.md §29 + BACKLOG → PLAYERS-1/PLAYERS-2 + [[faab-fa-live-unowned]].

### 2026-07-04 — PLAYERS-1 REMEDIATION: reverse G1 (real statline), fix dead entry points, honor the design density (`feat/players-remediation`, contained, **merge HELD for Chat clearance**; gate green, render proof 11/11)

**Why.** Live verification of the shipped PLAYERS-1 (`/players`) found: the row statline had been dropped (G1), both entry points were effectively unreachable in-app, and the row under-honored the design density. This thread remediates all three — **diagnosis-first** (root causes differed from the initial hypotheses), merge HELD (not autonomous) for review of the screenshots + the YC call.

**Diagnosis (live DB via read-only queries).** (1) **Count:** `count(*) FROM player = 1272`; `loadPlayers` has no `where` → returns all 1272. The live "422" = players on non-eliminated teams (the Active-teams-ON subset), NOT a filter. (2) **YC:** `stat_player_match.extra` has NO yellow-card key (its keys: aerial_duels, big_chances_missed, crosses_total, xG/xA, fouls_committed, tackles) — the handoff's "YC from extra" premise is FALSE; YC's only source is `event_match` (236 card events), a scoring-INPUT table. (3) **Entry points:** the waivers link was inside `tab === "claims"` (not a claims-open gate — the hypothesis), invisible on the Batch-results tab; the dashboard tile already renders as an `<a>` in R16 (phase ∈ {group, playoff}) → the "dead" report is most consistent with a stale pre-`c0bc6f6` deploy.

**What.** (a) **G1 REVERSED** — the row is the design `.mt-*` stat table: 7 REAL columns (Pld·Min·G·A·Sh·KP·Tkl) from ONE `stat_player_match.groupBy` over appearances (`minutes_played > 0`; Pld=`_count`, Sh=promoted `shots_on_target`), display-only from PROMOTED columns. **YC is REAL** (PLAYERS-YC, Chat pre-cleared) — a display-only `event_match` count folded via the shared `classifyCard` (`@app/recompute`, T-CARD1; non-rescinded `yellow` events, cards tournament-global; `packages/*` byte-untouched, no write/migration/engine-run). **CS renders "—"** — the ONE remaining gap (engine-derived). SH confirmed = `shots_on_target` (matches the shared card). (b) **KIT = shared flag-kit `kitOf`** (`apps/web/src/kit` — the T13/T16 primitive), never `background-size:cover`, emoji `<Flag>` overlay for coverage — NOT the /waivers text chip. (c) **Mobile "← swipe stats"** pinned-column table (Player `sticky left` / Pts `sticky right`, `overscroll-behavior-x:contain`, ≥44px bid target, no page h-overflow), widening to a desktop wide-list at ≥760px. (d) **Entry points:** waivers "Browse all players" moved to a persistent `.wv-browse-foot` OUTSIDE the tabs (renders in every phase incl. locked); a standalone "Browse players" `<a href="/players">` added to the MoreSheet (mobile reachability, NOT a NavId — a first-class Players tab is T15-2's); dashboard tile gate widened to group·playoff·complete. (e) Card NOT rebuilt (item 6 — its Stats tab already has the Match log).

**Scope + gate.** BYTE-UNTOUCHED: `packages/faab`, bid routes, `BidComposer`/`FreeAgentPanel` submit internals, scoring/recompute/ingest/lock-on-play/auth; NO migration/schema/RLS/Realtime; `design/design_reference/` frozen; `/waivers` flow byte-equivalent (its suite is the proof — the browse-link move is presentational). Full DoD gate green (`-w typecheck` / lint / format:check / **pnpm test 3237 passed / 104 skipped** / `@app/web` build, `/players` = `ƒ`) + **`verify-players.mjs` 11/11** (real-browser replica, real CSS, desktop 1180 + mobile 360/390 — statline + flag-kit + full count + tappable tile + closed-window waivers link + MoreSheet entry; screenshots in `apps/web/screenshots`) pinned by `playersRenderProof.test.ts`; `theCutSkin.test.ts` green (19). New tests: statline contract 2 + YC contract 2, statline render 2, playersRenderProof 7 (+13). **YC RESOLVED (PLAYERS-YC, Chat pre-cleared):** wired as a REAL display-only count from `event_match` via the shared `classifyCard` (`@app/recompute`, T-CARD1) — the render proof asserts a real numeric YC (Mbappé 1, Casemiro 2); CS stays the one "—" gap; SH confirmed = `shots_on_target` (matches the card). `packages/*` byte-untouched. The remediation + YC merge together `--ff-only` on the green gate + render proof (pre-cleared). A data-scoping/ingestion watch item (non-WC players in the pool + `nationAlive` over-marking) is filed as BACKLOG PLAYERS-DATA-scope (OUT of scope; connects to HELD T-ELIM). ### 2026-07-04 — PLAYERS-TAB: `/players` promoted to a first-class nav tab (`feat/players-nav-tab`, contained nav-config, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** — feature commit is the push tip; web green)

### 2026-07-04 — PLAYERS-DATA-scope: scope `/players` to WC participants via the `fifa_match` schedule — defensive guard, premise corrected against live data (`feat/players-wc-scope`, contained read-scope, **MERGED `--ff-only`**)

**Premise corrected first.** The thread was scoped on the belief that the 49 non-WC `fifa_team` rows inflate the `/players` pool and the Active-teams toggle over-marks ~80 teams "alive." A read-only prod query **disproves this at the `/players` surface**: all **1,272** players already sit on scheduled teams — the pool spans exactly **48** distinct teams, all ⊆ the **63** teams in the `fifa_match` schedule; the 49 non-scheduled teams carry **0** players (0 null-team). The "~80/422 alive" numbers are `fifa_team`-level artifacts (112 − 32 eliminated) that never reach the player pool. So scoping the pool is a **NO-OP on today's data (N stays 1,272)**. Chat re-authorized proceeding as a defensive guard with the acceptance criteria corrected (drop "N < 1,272" / "non-WC player excluded"; assert the TRUE invariant).

**What changed.** `loadPlayers` derives the participant set from ONE read-only two-column read — `prisma.fifaMatch.findMany({ select: { homeTeamId: true, awayTeamId: true } })` — over the **FULL** schedule (deliberately no `status` filter: an eliminated team has only completed matches, but its players are still participants and must stay in the pool greyed "out"; this is a SEPARATE read from the status-filtered kickoff-cutoff clock). Two pure, unit-pinned helpers in `playersLogic.ts` — `participantTeamIds(matches)` (home ∪ away, nulls skipped, deduped) and `scopeToParticipants(rows, set)` (keep `teamId !== null ∧ teamId ∈ set`) — filter the pool BEFORE rows are built, so `nationAlive = !eliminated` can only ever mark PARTICIPANTS (the toggle can no longer over-mark a non-WC nation).

**Why a guard, not incidental.** Today's no-op is a property of data, not a guarantee: the roster feed already knows all 112 teams (it populated the 49 empty rows), so a future ingest could attach players to a non-WC team. The filter makes "the `/players` pool contains only WC participants" an ENFORCED invariant. **Why not `group_id`/`eliminated`:** `group_id` is NULL for all 112 teams; `eliminated` marks only knockouts (32 set) — using it as membership is exactly the over-marking bug. **Why 63 not 48:** the schedule carries 63 teams (pool spans 48 — the extra 15 hold no players); narrowing to a clean 48-team bracket has no non-circular signal (no fixture-type/round column), so it is filed as an OPEN note, not closed.

**Invariants + gate.** READ-scope only: no `fifa_team` write, no `eliminated`/worker/FAAB/scoring/ingest touch, no migration/schema/RLS/Realtime; `/waivers` byte-unchanged; scope fence held to `loadPlayers` + its two tests + `playersLogic` + `verify-players.mjs`. Full DoD gate green: `typecheck` / lint / format:check / **3251 passed / 104 skipped** / `@app/web` build with `/players` = `ƒ`. `verify-players.mjs` gained a DB-backed invariant check (registers `tsx/esm/api` from `packages/db` so the plain-`node` `.mjs` can import the TS `@app/db`), asserting pool ⊆ participants + ≤63 backing teams and reporting the evidence — **15/15 green** (`N=1272 · 48 backing teams · 63 participants · 0 off-schedule`). Contained read-scope + Chat-authorized → **MERGED `--ff-only`**. See ARCHITECTURE §29.3 + DECISIONS → 2026-07-04 (PLAYERS-DATA-scope) + BACKLOG → PLAYERS-DATA-scope. Related: [[players-browser]], [[players-nav-tab]], [[auto-team-elimination]].

### 2026-07-04 — T15-2: shell stacking, z-scale, safe-areas + bottom-nav tap reliability (`feat/shell-stacking`, global chrome, **MERGED `--ff-only` after clearance** — feature commit is the push tip, web deploy fires)

The T15-2 pass over the shell's stacking model. **F-P0-A1 diagnosed BEFORE fixing** (full evidence in `audit/T15-2_SHELL_STACKING_NOTES.md`): the P0 is **multi-factor** — (C1, primary, code-verified) every tab is an MPA `<a>` with `-webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent`, NO `:active` style, a server-rendered active highlight, and zero `loading.tsx` — so on live-Render TTFB a tap produces *no visual change for seconds* and reads as "not registered" (matches walkthrough N1 + step 7 verbatim); (C2) the 6-slot bar's `white-space:nowrap` + flexbox `min-width:auto` squeezed "Players"/"More" under 44px at 360-430px and overflowed the viewport below ~340px effective width; (C3, runtime-only) Safari's minimized-toolbar tap-to-restore band swallows first taps in browser mode; (C5, cross-thread → T15-3) post-input-zoom layout/visual-viewport offset makes fixed-nav taps miss after any <16px input focus. **Ruled out with evidence:** resting-state scrim tap-swallowing (every overlay is conditionally rendered), blocked/passive handlers (the 5 tabs carry zero JS), 300ms tap delay. **Residual reported for clearance (outside visual-only scope):** the MPA navigation latency itself (no prefetch/progress affordance — a routing-behavior change, not chrome CSS). Bounded note: the More button is the bar's one React control — dead until hydration.

**What changed.** (1) **The z-scale** — ds.css `:root` now declares the ONE documented ladder (`--z-nav:100 < --z-overlay:120 < --z-overlay-stack:130 < --z-takeover:200`; 101/102 reserved for the shell's own More backdrop/sheet; The Cut's 110/120 already conform and stay literal/byte-untouched). Lifted onto tokens: pool drill-in (was 50), shared `.pc-scrim` (was 80 — changed in the canonical ds.css AND the four byte-identical per-route copies, which would otherwise re-override it by cascade order), draft toasts (was 80, + lifted clear of the nav band on phones), waivers composer (was 90), waivers FA card (95→`--z-overlay-stack`, it stacks on the open composer), commish view-as dropdown (was 40); the `/players` route-scoped 120 pioneer override is retired. The nav additionally goes **pointer-inert under any open route scrim** (`body:has(...)`, belt-and-braces over the z-order). (2) **F-P0-A1 in-scope fixes** — `.sh-btnav-item` gets `flex:1 1 0 + min-width:0` (EQUAL slots, ≥44px each at 360+, no overflow at any width, ellipsis guard on the label), `:active` press feedback, `touch-action: manipulation`, `min-height:44px`. (3) **F-P1-C1** — `.sl-savebar` pins `bottom: calc(58px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 10px)` under 640px. (4) **F-P2-I6/I7** — new shared `useSheetChrome` hook (stacked-count body-scroll lock + Escape + focus trap + opener-restore) wired into MoreSheet, BidComposer, FaPlayerCardSheet, PlayerScoreSheet, ForfeitConfirmSheet, pool ManagerPicksModal; `overscroll-behavior: contain` on every MODAL scroller (deliberately NOT the in-flow FA panel lists — they must keep chaining page scroll); every modal cap vh→dvh (88/90/85/80) + `env(safe-area-inset-*)` padding on every scrim; lineup root + empty states dvh. (5) **F-P2-PSC1** — both card sheets restructured to fixed chrome (✕ + header + tab strip) over an internal scrolling body (`.pc-body` / new `.sl-sm-body`); close targets 28px→44px. (6) **F-P2-A4 + F-P3-A1** — MoreSheet gains grabber + title + explicit ✕ + `aria-modal` + the hook's trap/Escape. (7) **F-P3-A2/G3** — safe-area-x on bar/sheet/topbar/content; commish console bottom clears the home indicator (the <640 nav band was ALREADY reserved by `.sh-content` — F-P3-G3's runtime question resolved static: the console mounts inside AppShell). (8) **Step-27** (in-flow instant-pickup, NOT a z problem — the composer is an in-flow `<section>` whose 420px+220px internal scrollers ate page-scroll gestures): at ≤768px the action column (Adding · drop-picker · confirm) orders ABOVE the pool list via `.wv-fa`-scoped `order:-1` — always reachable under the panel header; the modal instances instead scroll internally (`.wv-comp-body` overflow at ≤768px).

**Found-by-harness production bug (fixed):** `.wv-composer { width:780px; max-width:100% }` inside the grid-centered scrim — the percentage max-width resolves against the auto grid track the 780px width itself sized, so the bid composer rendered 780px wide at phone widths, pushing the ✕ AND the Place-bid row off-screen right. Fixed with the proven `.pl-modal` pattern: `width: min(780px, 100%)`.

### 2026-07-04 — NAV-LAT: route `loading.tsx` skeletons (`feat/nav-latency-feedback`, additive UI, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** — Sergio-cleared merge+push+deploy; Link-conversion clearance stays HELD)

The T15-2 **F-P0-A1 residual** (SHELL_STACKING_NOTES §5/§6): MPA navigation latency itself — the bottom-tab / More destinations are plain server-rendered `<a>`s with **zero `loading.tsx`**, so on live-Render TTFB a tap paints nothing for seconds and reads as a dead tap (walkthrough N1; compounds step 7's tab-switch freeze). T15-2 shipped the touch-down `:active` press; this thread adds the **content-level companion** so the destination starts painting structure the instant the response streams, instead of the frozen previous screen.

**What shipped — 15 new files, ZERO edits to any existing file** (all additive; `git status` = 15 `??`, 0 `M`). (1) A shared **`app/shell/RouteSkeleton.tsx`** — a **pure server component** (no `"use client"`, no JS) styled with inline token styles + the ONE existing global `.skeleton` shimmer class from `ds.css` (dark-correct `--surface-2`/`--surface-3`; NO new stylesheet, `shell.css`/`ds.css` byte-untouched). Six variants map to destination archetypes — `list` (players·standings·pool·waivers·scoring·commish), `pitch` (lineup), `cockpit` (vsfield·draft), `board` (playoffs), `form` (settings), `dashboard` (home) — each leading with a header band (every screen opens with one) over the body shape, so the real page paints INTO the same frame with no layout-shift jump. `role=status` + `aria-busy` + a visually-hidden "Loading …". (2) **11 one-line route `loading.tsx`** (lineup·vsfield·pool·players·standings·waivers·scoring·settings·playoffs·draft·commish). These routes mount AppShell in their **layout**, and `loading.tsx` is the Suspense fallback for the segment's `page.tsx` rendered INSIDE that layout — so the shell (top strip + bottom nav + active-tab highlight) sits OUTSIDE the boundary and survives the loading state for free; the skeleton only fills `.sh-content`. (3) A home **`app/loading.tsx`** — `/` mounts AppShell in `page.tsx` (not a layout), so its fallback would otherwise lose the shell; it renders its OWN static, **inert** shell chrome (top strip + bottom bar, Dashboard active, real `shell.css` classes + `crossNav` labels — NOT the async `<AppShell>`, which would suspend the fallback) around the dashboard skeleton. **`/games` is OUT of scope, confirmed vs `crossNav.ts`** — no `games` id in the `NavId` union or in `BOTTOM_TAB_ITEMS`/`MORE_SHEET_ITEMS`, and no `/games` index route exists (`app/games/` is only `[matchId]/`); `/games/[matchId]` is reached only by in-screen deep link (dashboard/vsfield/pool fixtures), never the bar/More (recorded so the next thread doesn't re-litigate it).

**Why `loading.tsx` helps before any Link conversion.** On a hard MPA nav, streaming SSR flushes the layout shell + the `loading.tsx` skeleton FIRST, then streams the slow page content behind them — so the new screen starts painting structure as soon as the response opens, rather than after every loader resolves. The **TTFB dead zone** (tap → server still thinking → old page frozen) is untouched by `loading.tsx` alone; `:active` (T15-2) covers the touch-down window, and client-side Link nav (analysis below) is what collapses it to ~0. So **F-P0-A1 stays *mitigated, verdict pending*** — this thread strengthens the mitigation (content now paints, not just a press flash), it does not close the finding.

**ANALYZE-only, NOT built (awaiting Sergio's clearance)** — full write-up `audit/NAV_LATENCY_NOTES.md §5`. Converting the 5 bottom-tab `<a>`s to `<Link>` is the change that makes these skeletons fire INSTANTLY on tap (client transition, no document reload). Key findings: AppShell can stay a **server component** (`<Link>` does NOT force `"use client"`); `<Link>` renders a real `<a href>` so it **degrades to MPA pre-hydration** — the 5 slots are NOT dead-until-hydration (correcting the brief's framing; only the More `<button>` is); prefetch warms only the `loading.tsx` boundary since every authed route is `ƒ` force-dynamic, but the fixed bar keeps all 5 Links in-viewport (recommend starting `prefetch={false}`); and **there is no shared authenticated nav layout** — each route mounts its own AppShell, so a Link transition re-renders the shell + re-runs `loadNavPhase()` per nav (a truly persistent, non-re-rendering nav is a separate, larger route-group refactor). **Recommendation: convert to Link** (low-risk, high-payoff), prefetch off initially, hold the shared-layout refactor as its own thread. Nothing converted on this branch.

**Verification.** `verify-nav-latency.mjs` **48/48** (Playwright + chromium) renders the **REAL** `RouteSkeleton` (transpile → `renderToStaticMarkup`; the component has zero non-type imports → no replica, no drift) and drives active-state through the **REAL** `crossNav` selectors. A local **streaming HTTP server** (flush shell+skeleton → hold 800ms → stream content) models Next streaming SSR and proves, at 390 + 1180: (a) the `[data-skeleton]` DOM paints DURING the delay, BEFORE the content marker (presence, not merely absence of the old page); (b) shell + bottom nav visible + tapped destination active-highlighted throughout; (c) no layout shift (shell/nav frame identical skeleton↔content); (d) no h-overflow. Screenshots `apps/web/screenshots/nav-latency-*.png`. Plus `routeSkeleton.dom.test.tsx` **30/30** mounts the real components (anti-drift pin) — including a **source-drift pin** (f63d144): because the home loader hand-mirrors AppShell's chrome (`/` mounts the async AppShell in `page.tsx`, not a layout), a 20-case block reads the REAL `AppShell.tsx`/`MoreSheet.tsx` source and asserts a shared `SHELL_VOCAB` of chrome classes is present in BOTH source and the loader, so a rename in AppShell breaks the test rather than silently diverging the chrome (`shellStacking.contract`/`verify-players` precedent; verified non-vacuous). Full DoD gate green: `-w typecheck` / lint / format:check / **3307 passed / 104 skipped** (+30) / `@app/web` build. Fence verifiers **UNMODIFIED**: verify-the-cut 43 · verify-playoffs-hero · verify-players 14 · verify-shell-stacking 33/33.

**Fences held.** `packages/*`, every loader/API/schema/RLS/Realtime/migration, `crossNav.ts` item arrays, `shell.css`, all `ds.css` copies, the T15-2 surfaces, and T15-CUT+Theater behavior are all **byte-untouched** (`loading.tsx` are additive UI files only). **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** (2026-07-04, Sergio cleared merge+push+deploy — the T15-2 precedent). **F-P0-A1 CLOSED + Link-conversion clearance CLEARED 2026-07-05** (Sergio's live on-device pass — see below). See DECISIONS.md → 2026-07-04 (NAV-LAT) + `audit/NAV_LATENCY_NOTES.md` + BACKLOG → NAV-LAT.

### 2026-07-05 — NAV-LINK: bottom-nav `<a>` → `next/link` `<Link>` (prefetch off) (`feat/nav-link-conversion`, additive UI, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** — feature commit is the push tip, web deploy fired)

The routing payoff for NAV-LAT (the `audit/NAV_LATENCY_NOTES.md §5` recommendation, now built). Converts the 5 bottom-tab anchors in `AppShell.tsx` (`.sh-btmnav`) and the MoreSheet item anchors in `MoreSheet.tsx` to `<Link prefetch={false}>`, so NAV-LAT's `loading.tsx` skeletons fire **instantly on tap** via a client-side transition instead of merely sooner after TTFB — collapsing the post-tap dead-zone that `:active` (T15-2) + skeletons only shortened. **AppShell stays a pure server component** (`<Link>` does not require `"use client"`, and none was added). The desktop top strip (`.sh-topnav` / `.sh-nav-item`) + brand link are **untouched, still plain `<a>`** — out of scope (the mobile bottom bar is where F-P0-A1 lived). MoreSheet keeps `onClick={close}` on every item, so the sheet still dismisses on tap.

**Prefetch posture.** Every converted `<Link>` carries `prefetch={false}` (commented at both sites, citing §5): the fixed bottom bar keeps all 5 tab Links in-viewport on every authed screen, so App Router's default eager prefetch would warm all 5 `loading.tsx` boundaries on every render — pure amplification given the always-in-viewport bar. Because every authed route is `ƒ` force-dynamic, prefetch would fetch only the static shell-up-to-Suspense boundary anyway; `prefetch={false}` fetches on hover/touchstart instead. Tunable, but recorded as a conscious choice.

**Verification model.** New **`verify-nav-link.mjs` 14/14** (Playwright): a real Next 15 App Router **fixture** (the production authed routes are `ƒ` force-dynamic + DB-gated → can't boot headless; the fixture mirrors the shell's per-route-AppShell transition architecture with the real `next/link` + a delayed route behind a real `loading.tsx`) proves the client transition (a post-hydration window sentinel **survives** the tap → no document reload) + the `[data-skeleton]` skeleton paints **before** content + `<Link>` SSRs a real `<a href>` (MPA graceful degradation is a property of the real primitive). A class-faithful **replica** (the shell-stacking pattern, real `ds.css`+`shell.css`) proves MPA tap-through on all 5 tabs + active-highlight tracks the landed route + MoreSheet nav + no h-overflow. Plus a **source-drift `describe`** in `routeSkeleton.dom.test.tsx` (5 cases, the SHELL_VOCAB precedent — **non-vacuous**, verified: reverting a bottom-tab `<Link>` to `<a>` fails assertions (b)+(c)), and the existing `moreSheetChrome.dom.test.tsx` proving sheet-dismiss-on-item-tap (6/6). Gate: `-w typecheck` / lint / format:check / **3312 passed / 104 skipped** / `@app/web` build with every authed route still `ƒ` dynamic; fence verifiers **UNMODIFIED** (the-cut 43 · playoffs-hero · players 14 · shell-stacking 33/33 · nav-latency 48/48 · mobile-nav). `playersRenderProof.test.ts`'s `<a href="/players">` markup pin retargeted to the `<Link>` markup (its /players-linkage contract unchanged). No DB/RLS/scoring ⇒ no gated-PG run.

### 2026-07-05 — T15-7 rulebook truth (/scoring) reconciled with the engine (`feat/scoring-rulebook-truth`, **MERGED `--ff-only` + DEPLOYED** `main@2267a4c`, Sergio's on-device visual PASS)

**What shipped.** The `/scoring` page had drifted from `packages/scoring` in three sections; all corrected against the engine (F-P1-J1/J2/J3). **§1** — the merged `7.5–8.4 → +3` band split into `8.0–8.4 → +3` and `7.5–7.9 → +2`; `7.0–7.4` fixed +2→+1; `6.5–6.9` fixed +1→**0** (the always-shown 0-band, matching the zero-rating-line engine behavior). **§4** — possession-lost ÷3→**÷10**, plus the five categories the engine scores but the page omitted: shots on target (+1/3), big chances created (+1/1), accurate crosses (+1/4), touches (+1/25), ball recoveries (+1/5, outfield-gated). **§8** — straight red `−5/−4/−3` → **`−4/−3/−2`**. Header "~25 categories" → "33". **§9** — all four worked cards regenerated; totals now **14/20/17/22** (the TEACH decision: possession-lost set ≥10 on each fixture so the −1 line renders, vs the OMIT alt of 15/21/18/23).

**Root-cause killed (the real fix).** The old `scoringData.test.ts` only asserted each card's rows summed to a *hand-written* total — internal consistency, never checked against the engine, so a wrong rate + matching wrong total passed green (exactly how the drift survived). Now: §1/§4/§8 render from local **data tables** (`RATING_LADDER`, `ACCUMULATORS`, `RED_CARD_BANDS`/`SECOND_YELLOW_BANDS`) in `scoringData.ts`, and §9 cards are **derived** — each is a `ScoreInput` fixture pushed through `scorePlayerMatch`, so rows and totals ARE the engine's output (no hand-totalling). `scoringData.test.ts` probes every displayed value against `scorePlayerMatch`, so **page-vs-engine drift now fails CI instead of reaching production.** The engine-side exported RULES manifest (generating §1–§8 from `packages/scoring` constants) was **explicitly DEFERRED** to its own later thread — not built.
